### [115] [Null-Space Constrained Low-Rank Adaptation for Response-Specified Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10989)
*Bocheng Ju,Jianhua Wang,Chengliang Liu,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NSRU是一种基于零空间约束的低秩框架，通过投影约束和结构化的安全目标响应，在抑制不需要知识的同时保留模型良性能力，在TOFU和WMDP基准上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型遗忘方法在抑制不需要答案时缺乏对更新局部性的约束，导致模型能力受损。目标引导变体虽可指定替代行为，但更新局部性仍不充分受控。

Method: 提出NSRU框架：1) 使用结构化的安全目标响应指定每个遗忘查询的期望行为；2) 从良性隐藏表示估计每模块的保留子空间；3) 使用正交投影的低秩参数化将LoRA更新约束在保留子空间的零空间中；4) 联合优化安全目标学习、不需要响应抑制和保留保持。

Result: 在TOFU上有效抑制可提取的遗忘集知识，同时改进保留QA性能、模型效用和安全目标对齐；在WMDP上将危险领域准确率保持在随机选择附近，同时保持广泛的MMLU效用。

Conclusion: NSRU通过零空间投影约束实现受控的局部更新，安全目标监督、不需要响应抑制、保留损失和零空间投影更新具有互补作用，在超参数和提示变化下表现稳定。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning aims to suppress designated undesirable knowledge while preserving benign capabilities. Many unlearning objectives focus on suppressing undesired answers, while recent target-guided variants specify replacement behavior but still leave update locality largely unconstrained. This paper introduces \emph{Null-Space Constrained Response-Specified Unlearning} (NSRU), a projection-constrained low-rank framework for controlled LLM unlearning. NSRU uses an explicitly structured safe target response to specify the desired behavior for each forget query, while suppressing the original undesired content. To localize adaptation, NSRU estimates per-module retain subspaces from benign hidden representations and uses an orthogonal-projected low-rank parameterization to confine LoRA updates to the null space of the retain subspace. The resulting objective jointly optimizes safe-target learning, undesired-response suppression, and retention preservation under this constrained parameterization. We provide a local first-order analysis showing that the projected update reduces retain-side perturbations while preserving editable directions for shaping forget-query behavior. Experiments on TOFU show that NSRU effectively suppresses extractable forget-set knowledge while improving retain QA performance, model utility, and safe-target alignment over representative baselines. On WMDP, NSRU keeps hazardous-domain accuracy near the random-choice region while preserving broad and domain-adjacent MMLU utility. Ablation studies support the complementary roles of safe-target supervision, undesired-response suppression, retention loss, and null-space projected updates, while sensitivity and robustness analyses indicate stable behavior across the tested hyperparameter and prompt variations.

</details>


### [116] [Structure from Reasoning, Numbers from Search: On-Premise Open LLMs as Structural Priors for Coupled MIMO Controller Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11015)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Haonan Li,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了开源大语言模型（LLM）在强耦合MIMO工业过程控制器调优中的应用。结果表明：对于单回路简单系统，LLM调优不如经典中继反馈方法；但对于强耦合多变量系统，基于LLM的辅助调优（结合经典优化器）能够突破局部最优陷阱，在样本效率和可解释性方面优于全局优化方法，且这一优势随系统维度增长而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合MIMO工业过程的控制器调优非常困难：分散式经典自整定忽略回路间耦合，局部数值优化在非凸代价景观中容易陷入局部最优。需要一种无需工厂模型、数据保留在本地、且能处理耦合效应的调优方法。

Method: 使用开源大语言模型（LLM）作为结构化先验，通过脚手架（scaffolding）引导LLM推理耦合特性、提出非直觉的不对称控制器结构，再结合经典优化器进行精调。对比了经典中继反馈调优、局部优化、全局优化（差分进化）等方法。

Result: 在强耦合四水箱系统上，朴素中继调优（J~28.6）和朴素LLM调优（29.7）不如开环（22.7）；有脚手架的LLM能达到J~16.9±0.2，再经经典优化器精调后达到全局最优J~12.0（10/10成功率 vs 局部优化0/10）。LLM的优势在于仅需18次评估即可获得可用控制器，而全局优化器此时性能还不如开环。在3x3系统上LLM节省约6倍评估次数。

Conclusion: LLM在控制调优中的价值不在于作为优化器本身，而在于提供样本高效且可解释的结构化先验。这一优势在强耦合、高维系统中显著，但在简单系统中不成立。研究为开源LLM在控制调优中何时有用划定了清晰的边界。

Abstract: Tuning controllers for strongly coupled multi-input multi-output (MIMO) industrial processes is hard: decentralized classical auto-tuning ignores loop interaction, and local numerical optimization from natural initializations stalls in the resulting non-convex cost landscape. We ask whether on-premise open-source large language models (LLMs), which keep data on-site and need no plant model, can help. On a single-loop CSTR, classical relay-feedback tuning (IAE 0.106, near the 0.102 optimum) beats an LLM tuner (0.162): for simple loops the LLM adds nothing. The picture inverts on a strongly coupled quadruple-tank with conflicting set-points, scored by a penalized cost J = IAE + lambda*TV(u) that rewards tracking without chattering actuators. There, naive relay tuning (J ~ 28.6) and naive LLM tuning (29.7) are no better than open loop (22.7), and a local optimizer from balanced starts fails in 10/10 runs. A scaffolded open LLM instead reasons about the coupling, proposes the counter-intuitive asymmetric structure, and reaches J ~ 16.9 +/- 0.2 from any start; refining it with a classical optimizer attains the smooth global optimum (J ~ 12.0, 10/10 vs. 0/10), which even applies a non-obvious negative integral correction decentralized tuning cannot. A global optimizer (differential evolution) also reaches this optimum, so the LLM is not the only route; its advantage is sample efficiency and interpretability: a usable controller in 18 evaluations (where the global optimizer is worse than open loop) plus a stated rationale. This edge grows with dimension, reaching ~6x fewer evaluations on a 3x3 plant. The behaviour generalizes across four open models, and on a benign plant the LLM offers no advantage, sharpening the boundary. We contribute a reproducible benchmark delimiting when open LLMs help in control tuning: not as optimizers, but as a sample-efficient, interpretable structural prior.

</details>


### [117] [Superficial Beliefs in LLM Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11016)
*Gabriel Freedman,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在二元决策任务中既非完全随机也非完全理性，其行为可通过行为模型预测，但自我报告与实际决策驱动因素存在差异，呈现出一种“浅层信念”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在选择决策时是仅仅模仿推理过程（imitate rationales），还是其选择反映了系统性的潜在决策结构。

Method: 使用合成二元决策设置，定义具有分级属性的选项，比较LLM自我报告的属性重要性（直接自述）与通过行为模型拟合其历史决策所推断出的实际驱动属性。同时引入基于分数的裁判（score-based judge）进行对比验证，并通过提示顺序扰动、采样扰动、替代行为模型、目标遮挡分析及结构变化等多种设置检验结果的稳健性。

Result: 行为模型能很好地预测LLM的保留决策，表明其行为与可见属性存在系统关联而非随机；但直接自述与基于分数的裁判仅能部分恢复行为推断的驱动属性。该模式在多种扰动和分析下保持稳定。

Conclusion: LLM在决策中表现出“浅层信念”（superficial belief）现象：模型行为受属性上的概率性局部优先级引导，但其对实际驱动决策的属性仅有有限的言语访问能力。模型输出既非任意行为，也非完全清晰的信念表达。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) merely imitate rationales when choosing between two options, or whether their choices reflect a systematic underlying decision structure. Using synthetic binary decision settings in which models choose between profiles defined by graded attributes, we compare the attribute a model says mattered most with the attribute that best explains its choice under a behavioural model fit to prior decisions. The behavioural model predicts held-out choices well, showing that model behaviour is systematically related to the visible attributes rather than being random. However, direct self-reports and a separate score-based judge recover the behaviourally inferred driver only partially. The resulting picture is neither one of arbitrary behaviour nor one of fully articulated belief - outputs are structured enough to support prediction, but explicit reasons track the recovered driver only imperfectly. This qualitative pattern persists across prompt-order and sampling perturbations, alternative behavioural models, targeted occlusion analyses, and structurally varied decision settings. We interpret this as evidence for ``superficial belief'' in LLM decision-making: models behave as if guided by probabilistic local priorities over attributes, while having only limited verbal access to the attributes that drive their decisions.

</details>


### [118] [Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11042)
*Liya Zhu,Jingzhe Ding,Jian Zhang,Jianbo Xue,Shihao Liang,Ge Zhang,Xiang Gao,Qingshui Gu,Mailun Gao,Huimin Che,Yan Zhao,Peiheng Zhou,Haojun Wang,Chaobo Xian,Lili Le,Chi Wu,Yiwei Liu,Shengda Long,Jiale Yang,Fangzhi Xu,Sijin Wu,Haodong Duan,Yi Zhu,Chao He,Zhaojian Li,Minchao Wang,Huan Zhou,Jiani Hou,Chuqian Yu,Weiran Shi,Hongwan Gao,Jiamin Chen,Guanhong Chen,Tingqin Luo,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zhixin Yao,Qing Hua,Yuhao Jiang,Jin Chen,Pu Chen,Zhenyu Hu,Xingyu Li,Zhengxuan Jiang,Meng Cao,Tianfeng Long,Haozhe Wang,Mingzhang Wang,Yichen Zhang,Yiming Dai,Chenchen Zhang,Jiaying Wang,Zhiyong Wu,Shen Yan,Yujia Qin,Wenhao Huang,Zaiyuan Wang,Xiaolong Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Workflow-GYM，一个针对专业领域长时GUI任务的基准测试。实验表明，最强模型成功率仅略高于30%，揭示了现有智能体在专业长时GUI工作流中面临工作流阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标漂移和对专业软件环境理解不足等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基准测试主要关注通用软件、简单应用和短时任务，缺乏对专业领域长时、高价值工作流的评估，无法判断现代AI智能体能否端到端地自主操作专业软件完成经济上有价值的工作。

Method: 构建Workflow-GYM基准，包含专业领域和特定软件环境下的长时GUI任务，并在多种最先进模型上进行大规模实验评估。

Result: 最强模型在Workflow-GYM上的成功率仅略高于30%，说明专业长时GUI工作流对当前智能体极具挑战性。进一步分析发现智能体存在工作流阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标漂移以及对专业软件环境理解不足等问题。

Conclusion: 当前GUI智能体在处理专业长时工作流方面存在显著局限，研究结果为下一代GUI智能体研究指明了关键方向。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.

</details>


### [119] [What Fits (Into Few Tokens) Doesn't Overfit: Compression and Generalization in ML Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11045)
*Martin Andres Bertran,Aaron Roth,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过LLM驱动的研究智能体实验，验证了"成功机器学习策略具有高可压缩性"的假说，发现输出压缩（短提示）和输入压缩（1比特反馈）对复现高性能模型影响很小，支持描述长度解释：成功策略存在于低复杂度区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为什么基准驱动的机器学习在实践中很少出现过拟合现象，检验"成功策略高度可压缩"这一假说。

Method: 设计了两个互补的信息瓶颈实验：1) 输出压缩：探索智能体在验证集上搜索高性能模型，再用"复现智能体"仅凭极短提示和训练数据复现性能；2) 输入压缩：探索智能体每次仅收到1比特反馈（模型是否优于当前最优）。在8个数据集上测试，涵盖表格分类、视觉、语言建模、扩散建模和奖励建模。

Result: 两种信息瓶颈对性能影响很小：短提示和可压缩反馈足以复现和搜索到高性能模型。只有在故意诱导验证集过拟合时，短提示才无法复现结果。

Conclusion: 支持描述长度解释：基准驱动ML中缺乏过拟合的原因在于成功策略位于策略空间的低复杂度区域，而非偶然。

Abstract: Reusing a held-out benchmark adaptively should, in principle, invite overfitting. Yet benchmark-driven machine learning (ML) has produced surprisingly little overfitting in practice. An attractive hypothesis is that successful ML strategies are highly compressible. We study this in the setting of LLM-driven research agents, where the hypothesis becomes directly testable via two complementary information bottlenecks. In \emph{output compression}, an exploration agent adaptively searches for high-performance models using a validation set, and we test whether a fresh ``reproducer agent'' can reproduce its performance given only an extremely short prompt and the training data. In \emph{input compression}, the explorer receives only one-bit feedback indicating whether each submitted model improves on the running best. Across 8 datasets spanning tabular classification, vision, language modeling, diffusion modeling, and reward modeling, we find that these bottlenecks have little effect on performance: short prompts and compressible feedback are sufficient to reproduce and find high-performance models. The hypothesis is falsifiable: when we deliberately induce validation-set overfitting, the results fail to reproduce with short prompts. Taken together, our results support a description-length explanation for the lack of overfitting in benchmark-driven ML: successful strategies occupy a low-complexity region of strategy space.

</details>


### [120] [CIAware-Bench: Benchmarking Control Intervention Awareness Across Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11063)
*Joachim Schaeffer,Thomas Jiralerspong,Alexander Panfilov,Guillaume Lajoie,Jonas Geiping,Yoshua Bengio,Roland S. Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CIAware-Bench基准测试，用于评估前沿模型对控制干预（CI）的感知能力，即模型能否区分自身轨迹与被控制干预修改过的轨迹。在四个任务领域上评估了11个模型，发现CI感知能力普遍较低（最高0.87，随机基线0.5），且在不同任务和模型间差异显著，CI感知并非固定的模型属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI控制协议中，受信模型会监控并修改不可信模型的潜在不安全步骤，但这种干预可能被不可信模型察觉，从而推断监控器的特性并适应以逃避控制。因此需要衡量模型对控制干预的感知能力，以设计更难被检测的控制协议。

Method: 构建了CIAware-Bench基准测试，包含四个任务领域（essay writing、BigCodeBench、Bash Arena、SHADE-Arena），通过变化轨迹水印、侧任务存在和控制协议来测试模型能否区分自身轨迹与被干预修改的轨迹。

Result: 在默认设置下，11个前沿模型的CI感知能力较低到中等（最高0.87，随机基线0.5），不同任务领域和模型对之间差异显著。跨模型家族的检测更容易，表明模型利用了提供商特定的风格或后训练差异。

Conclusion: CI感知不是固定的模型级属性，应对每个新模型版本和部署场景进行测量。发布CIAware-Bench以追踪CI感知能力，并为设计更难被检测的干预措施提供信息。

Abstract: AI control protocols oversee untrusted models by monitoring their actions and modifying potentially unsafe steps, often using a trusted model. This partially tampers with the untrusted model's trajectory. If the trusted model detects such an intervention, it may infer properties of the monitor and adapt to evade control. We introduce \textbf{CIAware-Bench}, a benchmark for measuring \textbf{c}ontrol \textbf{i}ntervention (CI) awareness across frontier models. CIAware-Bench tests whether models can distinguish their own trajectories from those modified by a control intervention. The benchmark is comprised of a suite of four task domains (essay writing, BigCodeBench, Bash Arena, and SHADE-Arena), while varying trajectory watermarking, side-task presence, and the control protocol. Evaluating eleven frontier models, we find low to moderate CI awareness under default settings (up to 0.87; random chance balanced binary classification accuracy is 0.5) with substantial variation across task domains and model pairs. Detection is generally easier across model families, suggesting that models exploit provider-specific differences in style or post-training. Overall, CI awareness is not a fixed model-level property, and should be measured for each new model release and deployment scenario. We release CIAware-Bench to track CI awareness and inform control protocols whose interventions are harder to detect.

</details>


### [121] [A History-Aware Visually Grounded Critic for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11078)
*Jaewoo Lee,Zaid Khan,Archiki Prasad,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Supriyo Chakraborty,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Sambit Sahu,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiViG是一个历史感知、视觉基础的多模态批判者框架，通过宏动作历史和视觉基础批评解决现有GUI代理测试时干预方法中的短视规划和执行错误问题，在多个基准上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理的测试时批评者存在两个关键局限：(1) 主要关注短视决策循环（例如忘记早期动作）；(2) 缺乏检测有缺陷动作所需的视觉基础（例如点击错误UI元素）。

Method: 提出HiViG框架，构建基于真实GUI轨迹训练的多模态批评者，将过去交互抽象为紧凑记录（宏动作历史），并通过视觉基础评估动作（视觉基础批评），在测试时集成到策略决策循环中，在预执行阶段拦截错误。

Result: 在Web、移动和桌面基准上，HiViG持续优于现有标量和口头批评者，相比最强基线，Qwen3-VL-32B提升5.8%，Gemini-3-Flash提升9.0%，并展现出强跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: 宏动作历史缓解了短视规划，视觉基础批评减少了执行错误，两个组件对于长程GUI任务中的测试时扩展至关重要。

Abstract: Various test-time interventions for Computer Use Agents (CUAs), including critic models, have been developed to improve performance through pre-execution action evaluation in complex Graphical User Interface (GUI) environments. However, existing critics suffer from two key limitations: they (1) focus primarily on short-sighted decision loops (e.g., forgetting earlier actions) and (2) lack the visual grounding needed to detect flawed actions (e.g., clicking wrong UI elements). To address these, we introduce HiViG, a History-aware Visually Grounded test-time framework, built around a multimodal critic trained on real GUI trajectories to abstract past interactions into a compact record and to evaluate actions with visual grounding. At test time, HiViG integrates the critic into the policy decision loop to provide macro-action history, which summarizes the policy's completed achievements, and visually grounded critique, which verifies raw execution coordinates against the current screenshot to intercept errors before execution. Across web, mobile, and desktop benchmarks, HiViG consistently outperforms existing scalar and verbal critics, improving average success rates over the strongest baseline by 5.8% for Qwen3-VL-32B and 9.0% for Gemini-3-Flash, and demonstrates strong cross-platform generalization. Ablations show that macro-action history mitigates short-sighted planning and visually grounded critique reduces execution errors, with both components being critical for test-time scaling in long-horizon GUI tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Monte Carlo Pass Search: Using Trajectory Generation for 3D Counterfactual Pass Evaluation in Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11120)
*Andrew Kang,Priya Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出蒙特卡洛传球搜索（MCPS），将足球传球评估重构为类似MCTS的评估问题，利用首个包含3D球轨迹的德甲公开高保真追踪数据集，结合价值模型、世界模型和反事实策略采样，通过分布感知的传球归因方法（均值基和百分位基准分）来评估传球质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传球评估方法缺乏对传球结果分布的建模，无法区分传球执行质量与战术决策质量，且缺乏高保真3D球轨迹数据支持反事实推理。

Method: 1) 构建MCPS框架，为每个观测传球推断踢球参数并采样执行变体和选项变体；2) 使用球条件世界模型（基于自动驾驶领域的SMART离散令牌自回归轨迹生成器）进行前向推演直至下一次球交互；3) 通过学习的价值模型对结果评分，得到传球价值分布；4) 设计均值基和百分位基准分两种互补的执行剩余评分用于分析和排名。

Result: 基于首个德甲公开高保真3D球轨迹数据集，SMART世界模型在有限公开数据下展现样本效率，在最佳20次预测准确度上优于基线方法，且支持完全假设性推演用于下游评估。

Conclusion: MCPS实现了分布感知的传球归因，将足球传球评估形式化为完整的MCTS式评估框架，并已开源模型检查点和代码。

Abstract: We recast pass evaluation in football (soccer) as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-like evaluation problem whose components mostly exist in the literature under different names: a value model (possession value), a world model (multi-agent trajectories with ball interactions), and a policy over counterfactual actions (sampling pass variants with noise). Building on the first public high-fidelity tracking dataset with 3D ball trajectories from the Bundesliga, we introduce Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS), which infers kick parameters for each observed pass, samples execution variants and option variants, rolls each candidate forward with a ball-conditioned world model until the next ball interaction, and scores outcomes with a learned value model to obtain a distribution over gained value. This distribution enables distribution-aware attribution with two complementary execution-surplus scores used for analysis and ranking: mean-based and percentile-based scores. To make the world model sample-efficient under limited public data, we adapt a discrete-token, autoregressive trajectory generator from autonomous driving (SMART) and show it yields strong best-of-20 forecasting accuracy compared to baselines, while supporting fully hypothetical rollouts for downstream evaluation. We have released model checkpoints and code.

</details>


### [123] [ABC-Bench: An Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark for Biosecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11150)
*Andrew Bo Liu,Samira Nedungadi,Bryce Cai,Alex Kleinman,Harmon Bhasin,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic生物能力基准（ABC-Bench），用于评估LLM智能体在生物安全相关任务上的表现。所有测试的LLM智能体在三项任务（编写液体处理机器人代码、设计DNA片段、规避DNA合成筛选）中均超过了中位专家人类基线。湿实验验证表明，OpenAI的o4-mini-high生成的脚本能在OpenTrons机器人上成功组装DNA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生物研究领域的能力迅速增强，既带来科学发现的机遇，也改变了生物安全风险格局。现有基准缺乏对LLM智能体在生物安全相关任务中自主执行能力的系统评估，因此需要建立专门的基准来量化这些风险。

Method: 构建ABC-Bench基准套件，包含三项任务：(1)编写代码操作液体处理机器人；(2)设计用于体外组装的DNA片段；(3)规避DNA合成筛选。使用多款LLM智能体进行测试，并与人类专家基线对比。对最佳模型进行三项湿实验室验证实验。

Result: 所有测试的LLM智能体在三项任务上均超过中位专家人类基线。智能体在依赖公开知识和良好文档协议的任务上表现优异，在需要新型生物信息学推理的任务上表现较弱。湿实验验证中，o4-mini-high生成的脚本成功在OpenTrons机器人上组装出预期序列的DNA。

Conclusion: LLM智能体已经具备超越中位人类专家的生物安全相关自主操作能力，尤其是在执行成熟协议方面。这提示需要更严肃地关注AI带来的生物安全风险，同时ABC-Bench可作为持续监测此类风险的工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly acquiring capabilities relevant to biological research, from literature synthesis to interpretation of experimental data. Increasingly, LLM agents can also perform in silico biology tasks that previously required experienced human biologists. These emerging AI capabilities offer new opportunities for scientific discovery and biomedical advances, but they also shift the landscape of biosecurity risks. To address this, we introduce the Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark (ABC-Bench), a suite of tasks to measure agentic biosecurity-relevant capabilities. ABC-Bench evaluates LLM agents on both benign and dual-use biology tasks: writing code to operate liquid handling robots, designing DNA fragments for in vitro assembly, and evading DNA synthesis screening. These tasks require a combination of biology and software expertise. All tested LLM agents outperformed the median expert human baseliner on all three tasks. Agents performed highly on tasks drawing on published knowledge and well-documented protocols, and more weakly on a task requiring novel bioinformatics reasoning. In three wet-lab validation experiments, we found that OpenAI's o4-mini-high produced scripts that, when run on an OpenTrons liquid handling robot, successfully assembled DNA with expected sequences.

</details>


### [124] [ReasonAlloc: Hierarchical Decoding-Time KV Cache Budget Allocation for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11164)
*Wenhao Liu,Hao Shi,Yunhe Li,Weizhi Fei,Xiangyuan Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Peisong Wang,Linqi Song,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonAlloc是一个无需训练的框架，通过分层预算分配（离线层级的"推理波"模式预分配+在线头级基于实时效用的重分配）解决长思维链推理中KV缓存膨胀问题，在数学推理基准上优于均匀预算方法，尤其在小预算场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长思维链推理中KV缓存快速膨胀导致推理瓶颈，现有解码时压缩方法采用均匀预算分配，忽略了不同层和头的差异化需求；而现有的非均匀预算方法仅适用于静态prompt预填充阶段，无法捕捉自回归推理中逐步变化的上下文需求。

Method: 提出ReasonAlloc框架，包含两个互补层级：(1) 离线层级的预算预分配策略，捕捉架构驱动的"推理波"需求模式；(2) 在线头级策略，在解码过程中基于实时效用将资源重新分配给信息量大的头。该框架即插即用，可与现有token淘汰策略结合。

Result: 在MATH-500和AIME 2024数学推理基准上，使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和AceReason-14B模型，ReasonAlloc优于均匀预算的R-KV、SnapKV以及静态单调递减层预算的Pyramid-RKV基线，在小预算（128-512 token）下提升最大。

Conclusion: ReasonAlloc有效解决了长思维链推理中KV缓存压缩的预算分配问题，通过感知架构特性的分层分配策略实现了优于均匀预算方法的性能，且计算开销可忽略，具备良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories in large language model (LLM) reasoning cause severe inference bottlenecks due to rapid key-value (KV) cache growth. Current decoding-time compression methods mitigate this issue via token eviction, but typically assume a uniform budget distribution across all layers and heads. In contrast, existing non-uniform budget allocation methods are predominantly designed for the static prompt prefill phase, and they do not capture the stepwise context demands of autoregressive reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ReasonAlloc, a training-free framework that recasts decoding-time KV compression as a hierarchical budget allocation problem. ReasonAlloc operates at two complementary levels: an offline layer-wise preallocation strategy captures an architecture-driven demand pattern which we call ``\textit{Reasoning Wave}'', while an online head-wise strategy reallocates resources during decoding to information-rich heads based on real-time utility. Evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks (MATH-500, AIME~2024) using DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B, and AceReason-14B show that ReasonAlloc outperforms uniform-budget R-KV, SnapKV, and Pyramid-RKV (a baseline enforcing a static, monotonically decreasing layer budget), with the largest gains at small budgets (128-512 tokens). ReasonAlloc is plug-and-play with existing token-eviction policies and introduces negligible inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [125] [The Role of Feedback Alignment in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11173)
*Semih Kara,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自我蒸馏中，与求解器推理轨迹对齐的逐步批评作为上下文，显著优于二元奖励和参考答案条件，因为对齐的反馈仅针对错误token，避免干扰正确行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我蒸馏方法通过让模型在无上下文时匹配有上下文时的输出分布来提升性能，但上下文设计的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在冻结批评家提供反馈的条件下训练求解器，比较三种条件：二元奖励（GRPO）、参考答案、与求解器推理轨迹对齐的逐步批评。

Result: 逐步对齐批评在Avg@12上比GRPO高16.11分，比参考答案条件高5.27分。

Conclusion: 反馈与求解器推理的结构对齐是自我蒸馏有效性的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Conditioning a language model on additional context, such as feedback on a previous attempt, typically improves its response. Self-distillation trains the model to retain this improvement when the context is not present. The method works by matching the model's output distribution under two settings: a student that sees only the question, and a self-teacher that also sees the context. What the model learns therefore depends on what context the self-teacher receives, yet the design of this context remains largely unexplored.
  We study context design for self-distillation by training a solver on feedback from a frozen critic. We compare three conditions: (i) a binary reward (GRPO), (ii) the reference solution, and (iii) a step-by-step critique aligned to the solver's reasoning trace.
  Step-aligned critique yields the largest gains, outperforming GRPO by 16.11 points and reference-solution-conditioned self-distillation by 5.27 points (Avg@12). Per-token advantage analysis reveals why: step-aligned feedback targets only the tokens where reasoning fails, leaving correct behavior intact. Conditioning on the reference solution, by contrast, pressures the model to change its behavior at every token (even correct steps) because an alternative derivation inevitably differs in phrasing and approach. This suggests that structural alignment between feedback and the solver's reasoning is a key driver of self-distillation effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [An algorithm for dynamical quantum optimal transport with applications to quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10075)
*Genevieve Dusson,Virginie Ehrlacher,Etienne Obermeyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文数值研究了基于Benamou-Brenier动态公式的量子最优输运（QOT）距离族，提出了一种内点正则化方法来计算半正定矩阵间的测地线，并展示了其在量子化学近似问题中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优输运是一个快速发展的领域，但现有公式众多，需要系统地数值研究基于动态公式的QOT距离族及其在量子化学中的应用效果。

Method: 提出一种内点正则化方法计算半正定矩阵间的测地线，通过积分核和密度可视化结果，并对距离的数值性质及随矩阵规模增大的收敛性进行研究。

Result: 动态QOT通过适当的参数调优可以很好地近似某些量子化学问题；研究了距离的数值性质以及对象随矩阵规模增大的收敛性。

Conclusion: 动态公式的QOT距离族在量子化学中具有应用价值，内点正则化方法能有效计算测地线，且参数调优是关键。

Abstract: Quantum optimal transport (QOT) is a rapidly developing field. Among the many formulations of this adaptation of classical optimal transport (OT) to spaces of density matrices, we numerically study a family of distances based on a dynamical formulation inspired by the Benamou-Brenier OT formulation. We introduce an interior-point regularized method to compute geodesics between positive semidefinite matrices and visualize the results in terms of integral kernels and densities, inspired by quantum chemistry applications. We show that dynamical QOT may provide a good approximation to certain problems in quantum chemistry with appropriate parameter tuning. We also study the numerical properties of the distances at hand, and the convergence of the objects when the size of the matrices increases.

</details>


### [127] [Branch and Price for Railway Crew Scheduling: Benchmark Instances and Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10081)
*Bart van Rossum*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种先进的铁路乘务调度的分支定价算法，并构建了包含75个公开基准实例的测试库（任务规模450-3016），系统评估了分支规则、启发式算法和减价固定策略的效果。实验表明，原始启发式方法贡献最大，平均最优性差距低于1%，而分支和减价固定作用有限，提升对偶界仍是精确方法的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路乘务调度是运营关键问题，但现有精确分支定价方法研究不足，且缺乏公开基准实例，限制了算法比较与进步。

Method: 开发了先进的分支定价算法，系统比较了多种分支规则、原始启发式方法和减价固定技术；同时通过模拟完整铁路规划流程生成了75个公开基准实例，并利用荷兰铁路数据验证。

Result: 在所有规模实例上平均最优性差距低于1%；原始启发式是缩小差距的主导力量，分支规则和减价固定贡献较小。

Conclusion: 列生成启发的原始启发式方法在实践中效果优秀，但精确方法仍受限于对偶界提升困难，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Railway crew scheduling consists of assigning a set of tasks to crew members in the form of feasible duties so as to minimise costs, and is one of the key planning problems faced by railway operators. While column generation is the dominant solution method, literature on exact branch-and-price approaches is scarce. Moreover, few publicly available benchmark instances exist. We present a state-of-the-art branch-and-price algorithm and conduct a systematic computational study of its components, including branching rules, primal heuristics, and reduced cost fixing techniques. To this end, we introduce a novel library of 75 benchmark instances, ranging in size from 450 to 3,016 tasks. The instances are generated by mimicking the full railway planning process, validated against data from Netherlands Railways, and publicly available with best known solutions and a solution checker. We report average optimality gaps well below 1% across all instance sizes, driven primarily by the strong performance of primal heuristics, whereas branching and reduced cost fixing contribute little to closing the gap. Our findings provide support for the popularity of column generation-based primal heuristics, and show that improving the dual bound remains the main challenge for exact methods in railway crew scheduling.

</details>


### [128] [The Dual Averaging Power-Prox Method with Application to Heavy-Tail Incremental Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10110)
*Yuan Gao,Jeremy Rack,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在增量梯度访问和重尾梯度噪声两个偏离经典SGD理论的实践场景下，有限和复合优化问题。提出了针对增量梯度的 Dual Averaging Power-Prox 方法，并建立了在组件梯度具有有界q阶中心矩（q∈(1,2]）条件下的收敛性分析，证明了该方法比带替换采样的SGD方法有更好的渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SGD理论假设梯度独立同分布采样且噪声为轻尾（如高斯），但现代ML训练中广泛使用确定性循环（增量梯度）访问，且梯度噪声常呈现重尾特性。现有理论难以解释这些实践中的现象，亟需建立更贴近实际的理论框架。

Method: 提出了 Dual Averaging Power-Prox 方法（基于对偶平均的幂近端方法），适用于增量（固定循环）梯度访问模式，并在组件梯度具有有界q阶中心矩（q∈(1,2]）的重尾噪声假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 首次建立了在该设定下的收敛性分析，并证明所提方法相较于独立同分布（带替换）采样的SGD方法具有更优的渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 增量梯度访问在重尾噪声条件下不仅可行，而且能获得比随机采样SGD更好的收敛性能，为现代ML训练中广泛使用的确定性梯度循环提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study finite-sum composite optimization under two departures from classical stochastic gradient descent theory that are central in practice: incremental gradient access and heavy-tailed gradient noise. Specifically, we consider fixed cyclic passes over component gradients and assume that, at the optimum, component gradients have a bounded $q$-th centralized moment for some $q\in(1,2]$. This setting is much closer to modern ML training practice than the assumptions used in classical SGD theory, yet its theoretical understanding remains limited. We propose a Dual Averaging Power-Prox method for incremental gradients and establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first convergence analysis in this regime. We further show that our method achieves a better asymptotic convergence rate than the corresponding SGD method with i.i.d. (with-replacement) sampling.

</details>


### [129] [Bidirectional SDDP with dimension-free complexity for solving strongly convex stochastic dynamic programming equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10161)
*Vincent Guigues,Pablo Barros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文分析了双向随机对偶动态规划（BSDDP）算法在强凸多阶段随机优化问题中的复杂度，建立了不依赖于状态空间维度的显式迭代复杂度界，并证明其优于传统SDDP算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDDP算法在处理强凸多阶段随机优化问题时复杂度较高，且通常依赖状态空间维度，限制了在高维大规模问题中的应用。作者希望提出一种更高效的算法并给出严格的复杂度分析。

Method: 采用双向随机对偶动态规划（BSDDP）算法，在标准正则性假设和无折扣因子条件下，利用强凸常数和Lipschitz常数推导期望迭代次数的显式复杂度界。

Result: 得到了显式的复杂度上界，该界依赖于阶段成本函数的强凸常数和Lipschitz常数，但与状态空间维度无关；复杂度随强凸性增强而降低，优于Lan (2020)中SDDP的复杂度结果。

Conclusion: BSDDP算法在强凸多阶段随机优化中具有维度无关的复杂度优势，尤其适用于阶段数适中、状态维度高的问题场景，强凸性越强算法效率越高。

Abstract: We analyze the complexity of Bidirectional Stochastic Dual Dynamic Programming (BSDDP) algorithm applied to multistage stochastic optimization problems with strongly convex cost functions. Under standard regularity assumptions and without discount factor, we establish an explicit complexity bound on the expected number of iterations needed to obtain an epsilon-optimal policy. Our bound depends on the strong convexity constants and Lipschitz constants of the stagewise cost functions but is independent of the dimensionality of the state space. This improves upon the complexity of SDDP for convex problems from (Lan, 2020), particularly when the number of stages is moderate and the state dimension is large. The analysis further reveals that the complexity decreases as the strong convexity of the cost functions increases, highlighting the algorithm efficiency in structured problem instances.

</details>


### [130] [Submodular Optimization with Applications to Decision and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10192)
*Shamak Dutta,Bahman Gharesifard,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了子模优化在系统与控制领域的理论、算法及应用，涵盖子模函数的性质（曲率、子模比）、约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统）、单调与非单调情形下的近似算法及最优性界限，并详细列举了传感器调度、多智能体协调、鲁棒优化、博弈论、资源分配、社会网络、信息路径规划等应用。特别指出控制论中某些经典目标函数（如可控性格拉姆矩阵的对数行列式与秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式）具有子模性，而另一些（稳态卡尔曼误差协方差、逆格拉姆矩阵的平均控制能量）则不具备，最后指出了开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模集函数因其边际收益递减性质为决策与控制中的子集选择问题提供了统一的组合优化框架。尽管精确最大化是NP难的，但子模函数的结构特性使得简单的贪心算法能够实现常数因子近似保证。系统综述子模优化在系统与控制中的应用具有重要的理论价值和实践意义。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了子模函数的理论基础（曲率、子模比等结构性质）、常见的约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统），以及单调和非单调情形下子模最大化的主要近似算法及其最新的近似比和难度结果。在此基础上，对传感器调度、多智能体协调、鲁棒优化、领导-跟随系统、分布式优化、博弈论、系统理论、资源分配、社会网络、信息路径规划等多个应用领域进行了分类综述。

Result: 揭示了控制论中某些经典目标函数的子模性质：可控性格拉姆矩阵的对数行列式与秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式具有子模性；而稳态卡尔曼滤波误差协方差、逆格拉姆矩阵的平均控制能量则不满足子模或超模性质。同时总结了依赖曲率和子模比的实例级优化改进方法。

Conclusion: 强调基于贪心的实用算法以及通过曲率和子模比实现的实例级优化改进，并指出了跨领域的开放研究方向。

Abstract: Submodular set functions, characterized by the diminishing-returns property, provide a unifying combinatorial framework for many subset-selection problems in decision and control. Although exact maximization is NP-hard in general, the structural properties of submodular functions enable simple greedy algorithms that achieve constant-factor approximation guarantees for monotone objectives, with randomized greedy-based variants extending such guarantees to the non-monotone case. This survey reviews the theory, algorithms, and applications of submodular optimization with a focus on systems and control. We cover the structural properties of submodular functions, including curvature and the submodularity ratio, the constraint families that arise in practice (matroids, knapsack, and $p$-systems), and the main approximation algorithms for monotone and non-monotone submodular maximization, with up-to-date approximation ratios and hardness results. We then survey applications across sensor scheduling, multi-agent coordination, robust submodular optimization, leader-follower systems, distributed submodular optimization, game theory, system theory, resource allocation, social networks, and informative path planning. The survey emphasizes practically implementable greedy-based algorithms and instance-dependent refinements via curvature and the submodularity ratio. We close with observations on canonical control-theoretic objectives: certain functionals are submodular (the log-determinant and rank of the controllability Gramian, and the log-determinant of the Kalman filter information matrix), whereas closely related objectives fail to be sub- or supermodular (the steady-state Kalman filter error covariance, and the average control energy obtained from the inverse Gramian). We also highlight the cross-cutting open directions that follow.

</details>


### [131] [A dual dynamic programming approach with dimension-free complexity for a class of convex optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10203)
*Pablo Barros,Vincent Guigues,Jiaming Liang,Renato Monteiro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了对偶动态规划（DDP）求解一类带线性耦合约束凸优化问题的复杂度。现有复杂度依赖于状态向量维度且指数增长，本文建立了与维度无关的复杂度界。通过提出灵活框架FDDP求解无约束强凸问题的动态规划方程，再通过光滑化和罚函数法将原问题转化后应用FDDP。FDDP采用束方法思想更新成本函数的低阶近似，包含经典多割DDP和新颖的双割DDP变体，后者每阶段仅维护两个仿射割，内存效率更高且理论保证相同。数值实验验证了两种变体的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DDP方法的复杂度随状态向量维度指数增长，限制了其在高维问题中的应用。本文旨在建立与维度无关的复杂度界，使DDP方法能处理更大规模的问题。

Method: 1. 针对无约束强凸问题提出FDDP框架，建立与维度无关的迭代复杂度界。2. 通过光滑化和罚函数法将原线性约束凸优化问题转化为无约束强凸问题。3. 引入束方法思想，提出通用更新过程，包含多割DDP和新颖的两割DDP变体。两割变体每阶段仅维护两个仿射割，内存更高效。

Result: 建立了与状态向量维度无关的迭代复杂度界；两割DDP变体在保留相同理论保证的同时显著降低内存需求；数值实验展示了两种变体对问题参数的依赖关系及与直接二次约束重构方法的性能对比。

Conclusion: 提出的FDDP框架和两割变体有效克服了传统DDP复杂度随维度指数增长的缺陷，实现了维度独立的复杂度保证，为大规模线性耦合约束凸优化问题提供了高效求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the complexity of a dual dynamic programming (DDP) method for solving a class of convex optimization problems with linear coupling constraints. Existing complexity results based on DDP depend on the dimensions of the state vectors and, in particular, grow exponentially with dimension. The goal of this paper is to establish a complexity bound that is independent of the dimension. Our approach first studies an unconstrained strongly convex problem and develops a flexible framework for DDP, called FDDP, for solving the associated dynamic programming equations, and establishes an iteration-complexity bound for it that is independent of the dimension. A dimension-independent complexity bound for the original linearly constrained problem is then obtained by applying FDDP to a corresponding unconstrained strongly convex problem obtained via smoothing and penalization. Inspired by the literature on bundle methods, FDDP updates lower approximations of cost-to-go functions through a generic procedure that includes both the classical multi-cut DDP and a new two-cut DDP variant as special cases. The two-cut variant maintains only two affine cuts per stage at each iteration, making it more memory-efficient while retaining the same theoretical guarantees. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the practical behavior of multi-cut and two-cut DDP, including their dependence on problem parameters and their performance relative to a direct quadratic constraint reformulation.

</details>


### [132] [Constructive Versions of Ekeland's Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10339)
*Douglas S. Bridges*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在实值函数下截面的初步结果基础上，为度量空间上实值函数的近似优化提供了Ekeland定理的构造性对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Ekeland变分原理提供构造性证明，而非经典的依赖于选择公理或非构造性方法的证明。

Method: 基于实值函数在度量空间上的下截面（lower sections）的初步结果，采用构造性数学框架进行推导。

Result: 成功给出了Ekeland定理的一个构造性版本，可用于实值函数的近似优化。

Conclusion: 该工作为构造性分析中变分原理的应用提供了基础，拓展了Ekeland定理在构造性数学框架下的适用性。

Abstract: Building on preliminary results about lower sections of real-valued functions on a metric space, we provide a constructive counterpart of Ekeland's theorem on the approximate optimisation of real-valued functions.

</details>


### [133] [Koopman Modeling and Stabilization of Discrete-Time Nonlinear Control Systems: Bilinearity on a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10344)
*Jarod Morris,Xiuzhen Ye,Wentao Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明，满足光滑性和正则性条件的非线性离散时间系统，在将状态和输入变量分别提升到再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）后，可精确表示为双线性动力学。在此基础上，利用核平方和形式的Lyapunov函数，将镇定问题转化为无限维优化问题，并通过离散化方案求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman模型在非线性系统中广泛流行，但存在输入变量时，即使无限维也不存在完全线性时不变模型，这使得基于Koopman的控制问题迄今仍是一个开放难题。

Method: (1) 将状态变量和输入变量分别提升到各自的RKHS中，采用线性-径向乘积核定义RKHS，使得函数由分量函数与Sobolev函数的乘积张成；(2) 证明非线性系统可精确表示为双线性动力学；(3) 将镇定问题转化为关于输入空间上状态依赖条件概率测度的无限维优化问题，目标是最小化核平方和形式的Lyapunov函数；(4) 采用离散化方案求解该优化问题。

Result: 非线性离散时间系统在适当条件下可精确表示为双线性动力学，且该表示不依赖于算子半群和无穷小生成元概念；通过核平方和Lyapunov函数和条件概率测度优化，可实现系统镇定。

Conclusion: 本文为非线性系统的Koopman控制提供了新的理论框架，通过双线性表示规避了传统线性Koopman模型在输入存在时的局限，并给出了可行的镇定控制设计方法。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of Koopman modeling for nonlinear systems, in the presence of input variables, the evident nonexistence of a fully linear time-invariant model even in infinite dimensions makes Koopman-based control largely an open problem to date. Focusing on discrete-time systems in this paper, which eschews from using operator semigroup and infinitesimal generator notions, it is proven that nonlinear systems, if satisfying appropriate smoothness and regularity conditions, can be expressed exactly as bilinear dynamics, when the state variables and input variables are separately lifted into their reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). To account for the knowledge of an equilibrium point at the origin, the RKHS is defined by a linear--radial product kernel, and hence the functions belonging to this RKHS are spanned by the multiplications of component functions and Sobolev functions. The stabilization problem, namely the determination of a feedback law that causes a Lyapunov function (expressed as a kernel sum-of-squares form) to decrease, is then posed as an infinite-dimensional optimization problem over state-dependent conditional probability measures over the input space, solved via a discretization scheme.

</details>


### [134] [Supermodularity and Submodularity in Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10345)
*Bo Zhou,Ruiwei Jiang,Siqian Shen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究双层网络拦截问题，分析了攻击者拦截网络组件后，防御者最优目标函数关于攻击决策的子模性与超模性条件，并通过利用这些性质生成有效不等式，实现了数量级级别的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更高效地求解双层网络拦截整数规划问题，通过揭示防御者目标函数在攻击决策下的结构性质（子模性/超模性）来加速计算。

Method: 针对最小成本流拦截问题，推导了三种攻击类型（供给/需求、流量容量、成本系数）下子模性或超模性成立的充要条件；扩展到设施选址、最大流和最短路径拦截问题；考虑系列-并行网络及防御者额外二进制决策（修复/加固）的情形。

Result: 条件在通用网络拓扑和参数设置下成立，仅依赖于攻击位置；在系列-并行网络中给出了最短路径和最大流拦截问题的充要条件；识别了防御者添加二进制决策后保持子模性或恢复超模性的条件；数值实验显示了数量级级别的计算加速。

Conclusion: 防御者目标函数的子模/超模性质可以显著提升大规模网络拦截问题的求解效率，且这些性质在多种网络拦截变体中普遍存在。

Abstract: We study a bilevel network interdiction problem, with an attacker interdicting (attacking) certain components of a network and a defender optimizing operations over the ensuing network. We study when the defender's optimal objective is submodular or supermodular with respect to the attacker's interdiction decisions, for optimizing the bilevel integer program more efficiently. We first consider the min-cost flow (MinCF) interdiction problem and derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the supermodularity or submodularity to hold under three types of attacks, respectively on supplies/demands, flow capacities, and cost coefficients. We extend to other variants, including capacitated facility location, maximum flow (MaxF), and shortest path (SP) interdiction. The conditions hold under general network topologies and parameter settings, and depend solely on the locations of the attacks. We further incorporate additional network information (e.g., detailed parameters and special topologies) to establish less restrictive conditions. We also derive necessary and sufficient conditions for supermodularity or submodularity in SP and MaxF interdiction in series-parallel networks. Furthermore, we explore more challenging interdiction problems where the defender may make additional binary decisions (e.g., repairing or reinforcing the network) and identify conditions that preserve submodularity or recover supermodularity. Via extensive numerical studies with diverse types of attacks, we demonstrate an order-of-magnitude computational speedup achieved by exploiting these properties and generating valid inequalities, for solving network interdiction at scale.

</details>


### [135] [A stochastic gradient algorithm for non-separable optimization with convergence guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10383)
*Yingzhou Li,Ruofan Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于非可分离目标的SGD框架，通过两种批次梯度构造（理想梯度G和缓存代理梯度H）处理全数据项计算昂贵的问题，并在温和假设下证明了局部线性收敛（强凸）和局部O(1/k)次线性收敛（凸）的收敛保证，给出了步长范围及缓存陈旧性等参数对收敛影响的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SGD方法主要适用于样本可分离的损失函数，但许多实际问题的损失函数依赖于整个数据集的统计量（非可分离），导致标准SGD无法直接应用或计算代价过高。为此，需要一种能够处理非可分离目标且保持SGD效率的框架。

Method: 提出SGD风格框架，定义两种批次梯度：理想批次梯度G（精确计算当前批次的非可分离项梯度）和缓存代理梯度H（缓存近似用于全数据项昂贵的场景）。在样本可分离情形退化为标准小批量SGD。理论分析基于局部强凸性和局部凸性，在光滑性和雅可比有界性假设下，使用固定步长证明收敛性。

Result: 在局部强凸条件下证明局部线性收敛；在局部凸条件下证明局部O(1/k)次线性收敛。两种更新策略（G驱动和H驱动）均成立。给出了缓存陈旧性、代理近似误差、批次大小和步长对收敛常数和允许步长范围的显式界。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了可分离与非可分离目标的SGD理论分析，为实践中使用缓存近似加速非可分离目标优化提供了理论依据和参数选择指导。

Abstract: We study non-separable objectives in which the loss depend on dataset-level quantities. We introduce an SGD-style framework that employs two batch-gradient constructs: the ideal per-batch gradient `$G$' and a cached surrogate `$H$' for cases where full-data terms are expensive.
  Notably, in the sample-wise separable case, our method reduces to standard mini-batch SGD. Our main contribution is a unified local convergence theory: under mild smoothness and Jacobian-boundedness assumptions,
  we prove local linear convergence under local strong convexity and local $O(1/k)$ sublinear convergence under local convexity for both `$G$'-driven and `$H$'-driven updates.
  Crucially, these guarantees hold for fixed step sizes within explicitly characterized ranges; we provide explicit bounds showing how cache staleness, surrogate approximation error, batch size, and step size influence the convergence constants and allowable step-size ranges.

</details>


### [136] [A Mean-Field Analysis of Multi-Head Self-Attention under Cross-Entropy Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10469)
*Cheng Huan,Hongfwei Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单层因果多头自注意力模型，在交叉熵最小化训练下建立了平均场理论。将每个注意力头视为参数空间中的粒子，在无限头极限下，平均注意力logits定义了一个概率测度上的风险泛函，其一阶变分生成非线性Wasserstein梯度流方程。证明了静态有限头近似界、刻画了全局最小化子的变分支撑条件，并建立了有限头随机梯度下降与极限PDE之间的定量有限时间传播混沌估计。进一步研究了PDE的长时间行为：能量耗散、收敛到平稳集、收敛到单一平稳测度以及显式收敛速率。最后证明了Wasserstein强单调性条件下的局部指数稳定性，并给出了Dirac平稳测度的可验证稳定性与不稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为注意力头训练提供严格的平均场理论基线，澄清从平稳性到收敛性和稳定性所需的额外紧性、景观和曲率假设。与以往聚焦于平方损失回归的浅层网络平均场分析不同，本文需处理交叉熵目标中的softmax残差以及掩码自注意力的查询-键-值结构。

Method: 将每个注意力头视为参数空间中的粒子，以头的经验分布作为大头状态变量；在无限头极限下定义概率测度上的风险泛函，利用其一阶变分导出Wasserstein梯度流PDE；结合传播混沌估计、变分分析、Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz不等式和Wasserstein强单调性等工具进行理论分析。

Result: 获得了静态有限头近似界、全局最小化子的变分支撑条件、有限头SGD与极限PDE的定量传播混沌估计；证明了PDE的能量耗散、在紧性条件下收敛到平稳集、在拓扑或KL假设下收敛到单一平稳测度、在梯度主导条件下的显式收敛速率；以及Wasserstein强单调性下的局部指数稳定性和Dirac测度的稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 为注意力头训练提供了严格的平均场理论框架，揭示了交叉熵目标和自注意力结构带来的额外分析挑战，明确了从平稳性到收敛性和稳定性所需的关键假设条件。

Abstract: This paper develops a mean-field theory for a simplified single-layer causal multi-head self-attention model trained by cross-entropy minimization. Each attention head is treated as a particle in parameter space, and the empirical law of the heads is used as the large-head state variable. In the infinite-head limit, the averaged attention logits define a risk functional on probability measures, whose first variation generates a nonlinear Wasserstein gradient-flow equation. Unlike classical mean-field analyses of shallow networks that often focus on square-loss regression, the present model contains the softmax residual from the cross-entropy objective and the query-key-value structure of masked self-attention. We prove a static finite-head approximation bound for the optimal risk, characterize global minimizers through a variational support condition, and establish a quantitative finite-time propagation-of-chaos estimate comparing finite-head stochastic gradient descent with the limiting PDE. We then study the long-time behavior of the PDE: energy dissipation, convergence to the stationary set under compactness, convergence to a single stationary measure under topological or Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz assumptions, and explicit convergence rates under gradient-domination conditions. Finally, we prove local exponential stability under a Wasserstein strong-monotonicity condition and give verifiable stability and instability criteria for Dirac stationary measures. The results provide a rigorous baseline mean-field framework for attention-head training and clarify the additional compactness, landscape, and curvature assumptions needed to pass from stationarity to convergence and stability.

</details>


### [137] [A Nonlinear Snell Envelope Representation for Path-Dependent Controller-Stopper Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10494)
*Magnus Perninge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时间区间、零和随机微分博弈，其中一方控制路径依赖随机系统，另一方有权提前终止博弈。通过控制随机化方法，证明上下值函数相等，并得到博弈值可表示为非线性Snell包络，其中停时过程由带约束跳跃的BSDE唯一最大解给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一方拥有提前终止权的不对称随机微分博弈中值函数的存在性与表示问题，弥合上下值函数之间的差距。

Method: 引入控制随机化（control randomization）框架，将终止权转化为可控随机变量，从而将原博弈嵌入到无终止权的辅助博弈中，利用BSDE理论中带约束跳跃的解来刻画值函数。

Result: 证明了上下值函数相等，即博弈存在值（value of the game），且该共同值可由非线性Snell包络表示，底层停时过程为带约束跳跃的BSDE的唯一最大解。

Conclusion: 控制随机化方法有效地处理了提前终止权带来的不对称性，为路径依赖随机微分博弈的值函数提供了统一表征，拓展了BSDE在博弈论中的应用。

Abstract: We consider a finite-horizon, zero-sum stochastic differential game in which one player controls a path-dependent stochastic system, while the opponent is given the opportunity to terminate the game prematurely. We introduce a control randomization formulation, which allows us to establish that the upper and lower value functions coincide.
  Our approach also yields a representation of the common game value in terms of a nonlinear Snell envelope, where the underlying stopped process is given by the unique maximal solution of a backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with constrained jumps.

</details>


### [138] [Short-Range IAM Operations to Overcome Access Gaps Around Bologna Airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10552)
*Jean-Claude Lebègue,Xuhao Gui,Daniel Delahaye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究创新航空出行（IAM）作为博洛尼亚马可尼机场（BLQ）的枢纽 feeder 和疏散服务，通过时间扩展网络建模、A*路径规划与动态规划调度，证明在50架次、10座、30m/s的配置下，IAM能显著缩短BLQ与周边机场间的出行时间（如BLQ-Il Ciocco从铁路的237-294分钟降至46分钟），并分析了速度与乘客留存率对覆盖范围与可达性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决短程机场间固定翼航班效率低、地面交通耗时长的问题，探索IAM作为新型航空出行模式提升枢纽机场区域可达性的潜力。

Method: 采用交错滚动窗口模型从BLQ航班时刻表生成需求；构建仅含IAM的时间扩展网络；依次求解两个子问题：A*算法进行乘客路径规划以揭示边级需求压力，动态规划进行机队调度以安排有限IAM机队。

Result: 50架次10座机队、30m/s速度下，IAM在多数链接展现大幅时间优势（如BLQ-Il Ciocco 46分钟 vs 铁路237-294分钟）；少数线路铁路仍具竞争力（如BLQ-佛罗伦萨）；速度与留存率敏感性分析量化了技术与采纳率对覆盖可行性的影响。

Conclusion: IAM可在不改变航空公司时刻表的前提下有效增强BLQ的区域覆盖能力，提升枢纽可达性。

Abstract: We study Innovative Air Mobility (IAM) as a dedicated hub feeder and dispersal service for Bologna Guglielmo Marconi Airport (BLQ), addressing short-range airport-to-airport connectivity where scheduled fixed-wing services are inefficient and surface access is time-consuming. Demand is generated directly from BLQ's flight timetable via a staggered rolling-window model: arriving passengers become IAM-eligible after a 20-minute buffer (a baseline 70% ''staying'' share is considered and distributed across nearby airports using a gravity model), while feeder demand for departures is timestamped backward by IAM travel time plus a 2-hour pre-flight buffer. We build an IAM-only time-expanded network (airports as vertiports) and solve two sequential subproblems: passenger routing with A* to reveal edge-level demand pressure, followed by fleet dispatch with dynamic programming to schedule a finite IAM fleet. Using OAG schedules for 5 May 2024, we simulate 09:00-17:00 with a 50-vehicle fleet (10 seats, 30 m/s) and 5-minute windows. Results show large travel-time advantages versus rail for many links (e.g., BLQ-Il Ciocco: 46 min IAM vs. 237-294 min by train), while a few pairs remain competitive for rail (e.g., BLQ-Florence). Sensitivity to IAM speed and staying share quantifies how technology and adoption shape feasible coverage, timing feasibility, and hub accessibility, demonstrating the potential of IAM to strengthen BLQ's regional reach without altering airline schedules.

</details>


### [139] [Accelerating SAV-based optimization via randomized low-rank Hessian approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10562)
*Ryo Sagawa,Daisuke Furihata,Yuto Miyatake*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Nyström增强松弛标量辅助变量法(N-RSAV)，通过随机低秩Nyström近似引入曲率信息加速收敛，同时保持无条件修正能量耗散律，在病态问题（如PINNs训练）中显著优于传统RSAV方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSAV方法仅依赖一阶信息，在病态问题（如PINNs）中收敛缓慢，需引入曲率信息加速收敛同时保持能量耗散结构。

Method: 在RSAV框架中利用随机低秩Nyström近似获取近似Hessian信息设计线性算子，通过特征值截断保证半正定性，并采用基于能量偏差的自适应策略重用近似Hessian以降低计算成本。

Result: 在凸二次问题和PINNs训练等病态低秩问题上的数值实验表明，N-RSAV比传统RSAV方法收敛速度显著更快。

Conclusion: N-RSAV在保持能量耗散结构的同时有效加速收敛，在PL条件和凸性假设下具有收敛保证，适用于低秩结构的病态优化问题。

Abstract: We propose a new optimization method, the Nyström-enhanced relaxed scalar auxiliary variable method (N-RSAV), which incorporates curvature information into the RSAV framework to accelerate convergence while preserving an unconditional modified energy dissipation law. Existing RSAV-based methods rely solely on first-order information and often suffer from slow convergence, particularly for ill-conditioned problems such as those arising in physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To address this limitation, we design the linear operator in the RSAV scheme using approximate Hessian information obtained from a randomized low-rank Nyström approximation. To preserve the dissipation structure, we enforce positive semidefiniteness through eigenvalue truncation. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive strategy that reuses the approximate Hessian based on the deviation between the original and modified energies, significantly reducing computational cost. We also provide a convergence analysis of the RSAV scheme with a general positive semidefinite operator under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition and establish corresponding convergence guarantees for N-RSAV under the PL condition and an additional convexity assumption. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned problems with effectively low-rank structure, including convex quadratic problems and training of PINNs, demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve substantially faster convergence than conventional RSAV-based approaches.

</details>


### [140] [Direct Data-Driven Approximate Optimal Control of Nonlinear Input-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10810)
*Benita Nortmann,Thulasi Mylvaganam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将数据驱动系统表示与非线性最优控制问题求解框架相结合的方法，通过将未知动态嵌入扩展状态空间，利用纯数据依赖的代数条件设计动态状态反馈控制器，直接为未知非线性输入仿射系统提供局部稳定性和性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知非线性系统的控制问题，传统方法需要先辨识系统动态再设计控制器，存在模型误差累积和计算复杂度高的问题。本文旨在无需显式辨识系统动力学，直接利用数据设计具有稳定性和性能保证的控制器。

Method: 将未知系统动态嵌入扩展状态空间，利用数据驱动系统表示将最优控制问题的解表征为纯数据依赖的代数条件，进而设计动态状态反馈控制器。

Result: 该方法能够直接基于数据为未知非线性输入仿射系统设计控制器，并提供局部稳定性和性能保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据驱动框架无需显式辨识系统模型即可实现对未知非线性系统的最优控制，为实际工程中难以建模的系统提供了一种有效的控制设计途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we combine a data-driven system representation with a framework to systematically construct (approximate) solutions to nonlinear optimal control problems. By immersing the unknown dynamics into an extended state space, solutions are characterised via purely data-dependent algebraic conditions. This allows us to design dynamic state-feedback controllers with local stability and performance guarantees for unknown nonlinear, input-affine systems directly using data, without explicitly identifying the dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [A Stochastic Maximum Principle for Partially Observed Jump-Diffusion Systems with Counting-Process Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10844)
*Jie Xiong,Ying Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了部分观测跳扩散系统(多变量计数过程为观测、状态依赖强度)的随机最大值原理，通过参考测度变换将问题转化为完全观测条件分布控制，得到条件哈密顿平稳性必要条件；应用于线性二次金融执行问题，导出闭式Riccati反馈律，粒子滤波实验表明该方法能捕捉非高斯滤波结构，优于高斯和忽略跳跃的近似，性能接近全信息理想情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分观测跳扩散系统的最优控制理论在处理观测为多变量计数过程(强度依赖于状态)时缺乏系统性的随机最大值原理，亟需建立相应的最优性必要条件框架。

Method: 引入参考概率测度将观测过程转化为单位泊松过程，将部分观测控制问题重写为条件分布上的完全观测控制问题，推导条件哈密顿平稳性条件作为最优性必要条件；针对线性二次金融执行问题，通过匹配系数导出基于条件均值的闭式Riccati反馈律。

Result: 粒子滤波数值实验表明该方法能捕捉非对称跳跃和非线性计数过程观测机制带来的非高斯滤波结构，滤波精度优于高斯近似和忽略跳跃的近似，闭环性能接近全信息理想情况。

Conclusion: 建立了部分观测跳扩散系统(计数过程观测)的随机最大值原理，并通过金融执行问题验证了理论框架的有效性和数值优越性。

Abstract: This paper derives a stochastic maximum principle (SMP) for partially observed jump-diffusion systems whose observations are multivariate counting processes with state-dependent intensities. By introducing a reference probability measure under which the observation processes become unit Poisson processes, the partially observed control problem is rewritten as a fully observed control one on the conditional distribution, leading to a conditional Hamiltonian stationarity condition as a necessary optimality principle. We apply the framework to a linear-quadratic financial execution problem. Matching coefficients yields a closed-form Riccati feedback law driven by the conditional mean of the latent state. Numerical experiments with a particle-filter implementation demonstrate that the resulting controller captures the non-Gaussian filtering structure arising from both asymmetric jumps and the nonlinear counting-process observation mechanism, improves filtering accuracy over Gaussian and jump-blind approximations, and achieves closed-loop performance close to the full-information oracle.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic inverse problems: Single-loop online algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10951)
*Jyrki Jauhiainen,Yassine Nabou,Tuomo Valkonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无限时域动态逆问题的单循环在线优化方法，该方法通过在每个时间步仅执行单步迭代求解PDE及其伴随PDE，并用单循环梯度估计替代精确梯度，从而在保证可证明的遗憾界的同时实现实时求解，数值实验在动态电阻抗成像（EIT）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速流场监测（如EIT）中，传统的优化方法需要在每个数据帧到来之前完整求解PDE，但快速测量模态使得计算时间不足。因此，需要一种不完整求解PDE、但仍能保证收敛性的在线优化方法。

Method: 将在线优化方法应用于PDE约束的动态逆问题，在每个时间索引（数据帧）仅执行单步或少量步长的标准迭代求解器（同时求解PDE和伴随PDE），得到单循环梯度估计 $\tilde\grad E_k(x^k)$；该估计满足标准光滑性条件且误差可和，从而使得标准的遗憾分析仍然成立。

Result: 动态EIT的数值实验验证了理论预测，证明了该方法在实时求解PDE约束动态逆问题方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 单循环在线优化方法能够在计算资源受限的情况下有效处理PDE约束的动态逆问题，实现了理论与实际的一致，为实时监测等应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study efficient online methods for dynamic inverse problems with infinite time horizon. We concentrate, in particular, on problems whose forward model arises from a PDE. Our motivating application is flow monitoring with Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT). The idea of such online methods is to take single steps of of standard optimisation algorithms, on each time index; each data frame. A predictor, based on problem dynamics, is used to transfer iterates one from time index to the next one. If we monitor a fast flow with a correspondingly fast measurement modality, such as EIT, basic methods are unable to solve the PDE before new data arrives. Our idea, then, is to not solve it, and instead, on each iteration, each time index, take single or few steps of standard iterative solvers towards the solution of both the PDE and an adjoint PDE. This is what ``single loop'' refers to. To the overall problem, we apply standard online optimisation methods, at the outside developed for exact gradients $\nabla E_k(x^k)$ of the iteration-dependent data fidelity $E_k$ that incorporates the PDE. We replace the gradient by a single-loop estimate $\tilde\grad E_k(x^k)$ that satisfies standard smoothness properties with summable errors. This allows standard regret proofs to go through. Our numerical experiments on dynamic EIT validate the theoretical predictions and highlight the potential of the proposed approach for the real-time solution of PDE-constrained dynamic inverse problems.

</details>


### [143] [Continuous Time Identification of Linear Systems: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10963)
*Fatima J. Ghadieh,Mireille E. Broucke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经形态计算原则的模型识别框架，不使用神经网络作为数学基础，所有计算在连续时间中进行。核心是采用过参数化模型解决自适应观测器中未知系统阶次与观测器阶次不匹配的问题，并通过离散算法逐步学习模型阶次，标准参数自适应律学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个遵循神经形态计算原则（连续时间计算）但不依赖神经网络的模型识别框架，解决传统自适应观测器中因未知系统阶次与观测器阶次不匹配导致的技术障碍。

Method: 采用过参数化模型（输入-输出等价模型）来处理过建模情况，并理论扩展到欠建模情况。使用离散算法编排连续实验以增量式学习模型阶次，同时使用标准参数自适应律学习模型参数。

Result: 过参数化模型为过建模情况提供了合适的参数化方案，理论扩展也覆盖了欠建模情况；离散算法能够逐步学习模型阶次。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了神经形态计算背景下模型识别中的阶次失配问题，为连续时间系统识别提供了不依赖神经网络的替代方案。

Abstract: We consider a problem to develop a framework for model identification adhering to the tenets of neuromorphic computation, without resorting to neural networks as the mathematical substrate. In particular, all computations take place in continuous time. We are naturally led to adaptive observers, where the main technical obstacle is the possible mismatch between the unknown plant order and the observer order. The key concept that informs the proposed framework is an overparameterized model, an input-output equivalent model that provides a suitable parameterization in the overmodeled case, with theoretical extensions also addressing the undermodeled case. A discrete algorithm orchestrates successive experiments to incrementally learn the model order, while a standard parameter adaptation law learns the parameters.

</details>


### [144] [Dominance and symmetry-breaking rules for the Graph Burning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10966)
*Nice Prado,Rafael Colares*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过研究图燃烧问题与支配集问题的相似性，提出了新的整数线性规划公式，并应用支配规则和对称破缺技术来减少搜索空间，同时引入目标函数扰动和剪枝规则，以加速图燃烧问题的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图燃烧问题（GBP）是NP难的组合优化问题，现有整数线性规划方法在大规模图上收敛困难，因此需要减少搜索空间以提高求解性能。

Method: 研究GBP与支配集问题的相似性，提出基于此的新公式；应用支配规则和对称破缺技术减少搜索空间；引入目标函数扰动及相应的剪枝规则以进一步加速求解。

Result: 所提方法通过减少搜索空间和加速求解过程，提升了GBP整数线性规划公式的求解性能。

Conclusion: 利用GBP与支配集问题的联系，结合支配规则、对称破缺、目标扰动和剪枝策略，能有效加速图燃烧问题的求解。

Abstract: The Graph Burning Problem (GBP) is a NP-Hard combinatorial optimization problem that models the propagation of influence or contagion in a network. The propagation is represented through the metaphor of a fire spreading through the vertices of a graph. A burning process takes place in a series of discrete time-steps. At each time step, the burning process is characterized by a propagation (where burned nodes spread the fire to their neighbors), and an ignition (where one additional unburned node is chosen to become burned). The minimum number of steps required to burn all vertices of a graph defines its burning number. Literature provides integer linear programming formulations to solve the problem, but with no surprise, such approaches struggle to converge as the graph size increases. Therefore, reducing the search space explored by these formulations becomes a key point to improve performances. In this work, we study the similarities of the Graph Burning Problem with the well-known Dominating Set Problem. We propose a new formulation based on this study and apply dominance rules and symmetry-breaking techniques to reduce the search space and consequently speed up resolution time. We also introduce a perturbation of the proposed objective function, as well as a pruning rule for the perturbed model in order to further accelerate its resolution.

</details>


### [145] [The Fuel Optimizer: A Data-Driven Numerical Framework for Formulation of Aviation Turbine Fuel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11099)
*Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Jacobo Porteiro,Dario Lopez-Pintor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为Fuel Optimizer的逆向设计框架，用于可持续航空燃料的优化配方设计。该框架根据用户定义的效能函数，从化学物种或碳氢化合物家族中识别出最佳组合以优化多个目标。案例研究中，通过反应器模型模拟满足特定性能标准的燃料混合数据库，获得巡航条件下的污染物排放数据。开发了替代模型以降低评估候选燃料的计算成本，并使用遗传算法根据两个效能函数优化燃料配方，旨在打破氮氧化物与CO之间的权衡，并最小化起降循环中的污染物排放。约束条件包括选定的性能标准（如密封膨胀）和组成限制。优化后的燃料候选在所有效能函数上均优于训练数据库，并通过反应器模拟进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种从用户定义的效能函数出发、逆向设计可持续航空燃料的框架，以系统性地优化燃料化学成分组合，在满足性能标准的同时最小化污染物排放。

Method: 1) 建立满足性能标准的燃料混合数据库；2) 使用反应器模型模拟巡航条件下的污染物排放；3) 构建替代模型以快速预测排放；4) 采用遗传算法优化燃料配方；5) 通过反应器模拟验证优化结果。

Result: 优化后的燃料候选在所有效能函数上均优于训练数据库中的燃料，成功打破了氮氧化物与CO之间的权衡，并最小化了起降循环中的污染物排放。

Conclusion: 该逆向设计框架可有效优化可持续航空燃料的配方，同时满足性能标准约束并降低污染物排放，为可持续航空燃料的理性设计提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: The Fuel Optimizer is an inverse design framework for sustainable aviation fuels that starts from a user-defined merit function and identifies the optimal combination of chemical species or hydrocarbon families that optimize a combination of targets. As a case study, a database of fuel blends meeting selected property standards was simulated in a reactor model to obtain pollutant emissions at cruise conditions. A surrogate model was developed to reduce the computational cost of evaluating candidate blends, taking fuel composition as input and predicting emissions as output. A genetic algorithm was used to optimize fuel formulation according to two merit functions designed to break the nitrogen oxides - CO trade-off, and minimize pollutant emissions over the landing-and-take-off cycle. Constraints included selected property standards (including seal swelling) and composition limits. Fuel candidates from the framework outperformed the training database across all merit functions, and the optimal candidates were validated through reactor simulations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Mechanistic Analysis of Alignment Algorithms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09850)
*Aarush Sinha,Ishan Garg,Veeraraju Elluru,Arth Singh,Kushal Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种偏好优化方法（PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO、KTO）在三种开源模型家族上的内部计算机制进行系统分析，发现不同方法在潜空间中引发质上不同的几何变换，其中KTO和GRPO提升线性可分性，DPO和ORPO降低可分性，PPO和SimPO基本保持基线几何结构。研究表明行为对齐并不等同于统一的内部重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练对齐算法主要作为黑箱评估，缺乏对其如何重塑语言模型内部计算机制的理解。作者旨在通过机械可解释性方法揭示不同偏好优化方法对模型内部表征的具体影响。

Method: 整合层间线性探针（linear probing）、稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）和交叉编码器（crosscoders）三种分析工具，对PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO和KTO六种方法在三种开源模型家族上进行系统机制分析，定位偏好表征并量化对齐诱导的潜空间几何变换。

Result: 偏好信号集中在早期-中期或中期-后期层，但不同优化目标引发质上不同的表征迁移。KTO和GRPO通过建设性特征共享和高显著性稀疏招募增强线性可分性；DPO和ORPO通过非建设性几何旋转和特征衰减降低可分性；PPO和SimPO基本保持基线几何结构。这些变换表现出架构依赖的变异性。

Conclusion: 行为对齐并不等同于统一的内部重构，对齐是一种异质性干预。研究建议建立标准化的特征级审计机制用于安全性和可解释性，并呼吁开发机制感知的优化目标。

Abstract: Post-training alignment algorithms are predominantly evaluated as black boxes, obscuring how they reshape language models' internal computations. We present a systematic mechanistic analysis of six preference-optimization methods: PPO, DPO, SimPO, ORPO, GRPO, and KTO across three open-weight model families. By integrating layer-wise linear probing, Sparse Autoencoders, and crosscoders, we localize preference representations and quantify alignment-induced geometric transformations in latent space. We find that preference signals consistently concentrate in early--mid or mid--late layers, but different objectives induce qualitatively distinct representational shifts. KTO and GRPO enhance linear separability through constructive feature sharing and sparse, high-salience recruitment. In contrast, DPO and ORPO degrade separability via non-constructive geometric rotation and feature attenuation, while PPO and SimPO largely preserve baseline geometry. These transformations exhibit architecture-dependent variability, demonstrating that behavioral alignment does not imply uniform internal restructuring. Our findings establish alignment as a heterogeneous intervention, motivate standardized feature-level auditing for safety and interpretability, and highlight the need for mechanism-aware optimization objectives.

</details>


### [147] [SynIB: Informational Bottleneck for Maximizing Synergy in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09853)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Teodora Gagaleska,Thomas Strypsteen,Christos Chatzichristos,Matthew Blaschko,Maarten De Vos,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB)，一种通过信息论直接优化多模态学习中协同作用（synergy）的训练目标。核心思想是在标准任务损失之外，对任意模态被遮蔽时仍保持高置信度的模型施加惩罚，从而迫使模型学习跨模态交互而非单模态或冗余信息。在合成数据上SynIB能恢复标准训练失败的真实协同信号，在五个真实基准上提升协同依赖样本准确率最高7.8%，整体准确率最高3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习的核心目标之一是捕捉协同作用（synergy）——即只有联合使用多种模态才能获得的、单一模态无法提供的任务相关信息。现有方法主要通过设计更大或更复杂的融合架构来实现，但作者提出另一个互补方向：直接塑造训练目标本身。标准训练往往强调单模态或冗余信息，在需要跨模态推理的样本上表现不足。

Method: 通过信息论形式化多模态协同作用，提出Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB)可扩展目标。SynIB在标准任务损失之外，额外运行一次遮蔽一个模态的前向传播，并对模型在此情况下仍保持高置信度施加惩罚——高置信度表明模型依赖的是单模态线索而非跨模态交互。通过这种方式直接优先学习协同信息。

Result: 在合成XOR任务上，标准训练无法恢复真实协同信号而SynIB成功。在五个真实基准上（包括MultiBench三个情感任务、Hateful Memes用CLIP-ViT和DeBERTa主干、以及作者引入的可控讽刺扩展CREMA-D），SynIB在协同依赖样本上准确率提升最高7.8%，整体准确率提升最高3.8%。

Conclusion: 通过修改训练目标而非网络架构，可以有效地引导模型学习跨模态协同信息。SynIB作为一种轻量级、可扩展的插件式方法，在多个多模态任务上一致提升了协同依赖样本和整体性能。

Abstract: A central objective in multimodal learning is to capture synergy: task-relevant information that arises only from the joint use of multiple modalities, and is not available from any single modality alone. While most approaches operate at the architectural level through larger or more complex fusion models, we propose a complementary axis: shaping the training objective itself. Standard training often emphasizes unimodal or redundant information, falling short on examples that require cross-modal reasoning. We formalize multimodal synergy through information theory and introduce the Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB), a scalable objective that targets synergy directly. To prioritize learning synergy, SynIB motivates the model to predict accurately from all modalities while penalizing confidence when information from any modality is withheld. Alongside the standard task loss, the model runs forward passes with one modality masked at a time and is penalized for remaining confident, which would indicate reliance on unimodal cues rather than cross-modal interactions. We validate SynIB in two regimes. On synthetic XOR tasks where the ground-truth synergy is known by construction, standard training fails to recover it while SynIB does. On five real-world benchmarks, including three MultiBench affective tasks, Hateful Memes with CLIP-ViT and DeBERTa backbones, and a controllable irony extension of CREMA-D we introduce, SynIB improves accuracy on synergy-dependent examples by up to 7.8% and overall accuracy by up to 3.8%.

</details>


### [148] [Uncertainty-aware Multi-fidelity Closure via Conditional Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09857)
*Jice Zeng,Shady E. Ahmed,David Barajas-Solano,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件归一化流的非确定性多保真度框架，用于降阶模型的闭合建模，通过学习低保真度系数到高保真度系数的概率映射提升预测精度，并量化闭合不确定性。残差学习策略优于直接学习，并在二维涡旋合并问题中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型在预测精度上常因截断误差和未解析尺度与解析尺度间相互作用表示不足而受限，即闭合问题。现有方法缺乏对闭合建模不确定性的量化，亟需一种能够同时提升精度并评估预测置信度的框架。

Method: 将ROM闭合建模形式化为多保真度学习问题，基于条件归一化流构建概率映射，从低保真度ROM系数预测高保真度系数。探索直接学习（直接预测HF系数）和残差学习（学习LF与HF间残差）两种校正策略，并提供不确定性量化。

Result: 两种校正策略均显著提升ROM精度，残差学习一致优于直接学习。同时，基于深度生成模型的方法能够为校正后的ROM系数提供不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该非确定性多保真度框架有效解决了ROM的闭合问题，提升了预测可靠性，并通过不确定性量化增强了模型在实际应用中的可信度。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) provide an efficient surrogate for complex multiscale systems, but their predictive accuracy is often compromised by truncation errors and the inadequate representation of interactions between resolved and unresolved scales. The missing effect of truncated (unresolved) scales on ROM (resolved) scales is often denoted as the closure problem. In this work, we formulate ROM closure modeling as a multi-fidelity (MF) learning problem and propose an uncertainty-aware MF framework based on conditional normalizing flow to enhance ROM predictive accuracy. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic mapping from low-fidelity (LF) ROM coefficients to high-fidelity (HF) coefficients, thereby improving predictive fidelity while quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned closure. Two correction strategies are investigated: direct learning, in which HF coefficients are predicted directly from LF inputs, and residual learning, which learns the discrepancy between LF and HF coefficients and uses it to recover the corrected HF solution. The framework is demonstrated on a vortex merging problem governed by the two-dimensional Navier Stokes equations. Results show that both correction strategies improve ROM accuracy over uncorrected ROM, with residual learning achieving consistently better performance than direct learning. Moreover, the two proposed deep generative model-based strategies provide uncertainty quantification for the corrected ROM coefficients, which is critical for assessing prediction confidence and supporting the reliable use of ROMs in practical applications.

</details>


### [149] [Mitigating Manifold Departure: Uncertainty-Aware Subspace Rectification for Trustworthy MLLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09859)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Jingxiao Yang,Miao Pan,Cheng Tan,Yuxiang Cai,Siwei Tan,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MGAP方法，通过几何感知的自适应投影策略选择性抑制语言先验中的有害部分，在减少多模态大模型幻觉的同时保持语义结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free解码策略盲目抑制语言先验，忽视了语言先验的双重性（既可能有益也可能有害），导致模型语义流形被破坏（Manifold Departure），造成性能下降。

Method: 首先通过SVD从盲态隐状态构建语言先验子空间；解码时，将多模态隐状态投影到该子空间，并利用一致性感知门控自适应地衰减投影的先验分量，实现子空间选择性更新，保留正交语义成分。

Result: 在POPE和CHAIR基准上，MGAP优于现有解码基线，在有效抑制幻觉的同时保持了生成文本的连贯性。

Conclusion: MGAP通过几何感知的自适应投影策略，在抑制幻觉和保护语义流形之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: MLLMs frequently hallucinate objects inconsistent with visual inputs. This issue is typically attributed to the over-reliance on language priors, which can override the visual context. Recent training-free decoding strategies address this by penalizing language priors. However, these methods overlook the dual nature of language priors, where they can be both helpful and harmful depending on the alignment with visual evidence. In particular, blindly suppressing language priors often disrupts the model's semantic manifold, leading to performance degradation, a phenomenon we term Manifold Departure. To address this, we propose Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP), a geometry-aware, training-free decoding method that mitigates hallucinations while preserving representation structure. MGAP first constructs a language-prior subspace from blind hidden states via SVD. During decoding, MGAP projects each multimodal hidden state onto this subspace and applies a consistency-aware gate to adaptively attenuate only the projected prior component, yielding a subspace-selective update that largely preserves the orthogonal semantic components. Extensive experiments on POPE and CHAIR show that MGAP outperforms prior decoding baselines, achieving stronger hallucination suppression without sacrificing coherence.

</details>


### [150] [Conformal Risk Prediction for Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease Using Gradient Boosting with Distribution-Free Coverages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09860)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度提升决策树与共形预测相结合的机器学习框架Method，用于非酒精性脂肪肝（NAFLD）风险预测。该方法在广州多中心队列（内部2,187例，外部验证412例）上实现了0.912的内部AUROC和0.891的外部AUROC，在90%名义置信水平下达到91.3%的经验覆盖率，并识别出腰围、ALT、GGT等关键代谢风险因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAFLD影响约25%的全球成年人，带来严重的肝脏和心血管风险，但现有的人群水平筛查工具不足，亟需更准确、可解释且具有统计保证的风险预测方法。

Method: 将梯度提升决策树与共形预测相结合，实现校准的、分布自由的覆盖率保证；采用基于互信息的稳定性选择，通过bootstrap重采样筛选紧凑且临床可解释的特征子集；构建预测集，其边际覆盖率可证明超过用户指定的置信水平。

Result: 内部AUROC 0.912，外部AUROC 0.891，优于深度神经网络、TabNet、SVM和逻辑回归；共形预测集在90%名义水平下达到91.3%经验覆盖率；高风险亚组12个月进展率是低风险组的4.7倍。

Conclusion: 该框架在NAFLD风险预测中表现出高准确性、可靠性及可解释性，筛选出的特征（腰围、ALT、GGT、甘油三酯、空腹血糖、BMI）与已知代谢风险因素一致，具有生物学合理性，为人群筛查提供了有前景的工具。

Abstract: Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) affects roughly 25% of global adults, posing substantial hepatic and cardiovascular risks. Yet, population-level screening tools remain inadequate. We present Method, a machine-learning framework for NAFLD risk prediction coupling gradient-boosted decision trees with conformal prediction to yield calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees on individual risk estimates. It integrates a mutual-information-based stability selection procedure to identify a compact, clinically interpretable feature subset via bootstrap resampling, constructing prediction sets whose marginal coverage provably exceeds a user-specified confidence level. We evaluated Method on a multicenter cohort from Guangzhou, China (primary n=2,187; external validation n=412) using 78 candidate features across demographics, metabolic biomarkers, and lifestyle factors. Method achieves an AUROC of 0.912 internally and 0.891 externally, outperforming deep neural networks, TabNet, support vector machines, and logistic regression. Conformal prediction sets achieve 91.3% empirical coverage at the 90% nominal level. A three-tier risk stratification derived from these scores separates the population into distinct groups, with the high-risk subgroup showing a 12-month progression rate 4.7 times that of the low-risk tier. The selected features -- notably waist circumference, ALT, GGT, triglycerides, fasting glucose, and BMI -- align with established metabolic risk factors, providing biological plausibility.

</details>


### [151] [Time Series as Language: A Universal Tokenizer for General-Purpose Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09861)
*Yunhao Zhang,Ruiying Qi,Jiale Zheng,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniTok-FM 是一种基于向量量化自编码器（UniTok）的通用时间序列基础模型，通过将连续时间序列离散化为令牌后采用下一代标记预测（NTP）进行预训练。该模型支持零样本预测、提示增强预测以及无需训练即可进行的上下文学习（少样本生成和分类），在多个任务上超越统计基线和监督基线，性能与任务特定基础模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对下一代标记预测（NTP）在大语言模型预训练中取得巨大成功，但将其应用于无界、连续的时间序列数据仍存在挑战。现有时间序列基础模型缺乏像NTP这样统一的预训练范式，且难以同时支持预测、生成和分类等多样任务。

Method: 提出UniTok向量量化自编码器，包含前缀归一化（稳定尺度）、渐进分辨率因果架构（编码解码）和结构保持重建损失。UniTok-FM采用现成LLM架构，在具有相似模式的多个序列构成的上下文窗口上进行NTP预训练，以捕获共享动态。

Result: 在预测、生成和分类实验中，单一的UniTok-FM一致优于统计基线和监督基线，与任务特定基础模型性能相当，且独特地实现了跨任务的无需训练上下文推理。

Conclusion: UniTok-FM成功将NTP范式扩展到时间序列领域，作为一个通用基础模型统一了多种时间序列任务，为时间序列基础模型研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: While Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has unified LLM pretraining, its adaptation to unbounded, continuous time series (TS) remains open. To bridge the gap, we introduce UniTok, a universal tokenizer that transforms TS into discrete tokens, and UniTok-FM, a foundation model pretrained via NTP on these tokens. UniTok-FM is a general-purpose foundation model that supports zero-shot and prompt-boosted forecasting, as well as few-shot generation and classification via training-free in-context inference--a capability not achieved by prior works. Technically, UniTok is a vector-quantized autoencoder incorporating prefix normalization for scale stabilization, a progressive-resolution causal architecture for encoding and decoding, and a structure-preserving reconstruction loss for training. UniTok-FM adopts an off-the-shelf LLM architecture without TS-specific modifications. Instead of pretraining on isolated TS, it performs NTP on context windows formed by multiple series with similar patterns, aiming to capture their shared dynamics. Experiments on forecasting, generation, and classification show that a single unified UniTok-FM consistently outperforms statistical and supervised baselines, achieves competitive performance with task-specific foundation models, and uniquely enables training-free in-context inference across tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Blurry Window Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09862)
*Axel Laborieux,Christos Sourmpis,Juan Gabriel Kostelec,Qinghai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为模糊窗口注意力（BLA）的新型有界记忆控制注意力方法，通过狄利克雷核插值从频率窗口重建模糊的KV历史，在保持线性复杂度的同时提升了状态效率，在合成检索任务上性能优于滑动窗口注意力和线性注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的Softmax注意力在长序列场景下存在二次复杂度和KV缓存状态规模过大的瓶颈；现有线性模型（如SSM、线性注意力、有界记忆控制注意力）虽然降低了复杂度，但在需要检索或回忆特定信息的任务上性能仍落后于Transformer。

Method: BLA是一种受SSM启发的有界记忆控制注意力方法，维护一个频率窗口，通过狄利克雷核插值从中重建模糊的KV历史。BLA可以视为滑动窗口注意力的推广（取决于狄利克雷核的分辨率），也可以视为门控槽注意力的特例（其中衰减因子由狄利克雷核实现）。

Result: 在MQAR合成任务上，BLA的状态效率比滑动窗口注意力提升8倍，且与主流线性注意力模型竞争力相当；在RegBench合成任务中，BLA和滑动窗口注意力是唯一随着状态规模增长性能提升的线性模型。

Conclusion: BLA通过频率窗口和狄利克雷核插值的设计，有效结合了滑动窗口注意力的检索能力和线性模型的高效状态管理，在长序列检索任务上实现了更好的性能-效率权衡。

Abstract: The Softmax Attention operation in Transformer language models has a quadratic complexity in the sequence length and a growing state size in the form of KV cache, which becomes a bottleneck in long context scenarios. To overcome this limitation, alternative architectures with linear complexity and finite state size have been introduced, such as State-Space Models (SSMs), Linear Attention (LA), and Attention with Bounded-memory Control (ABC).
