Though linear models achieve similar language perplexity as Transformers, they are still behind in tasks which require retrieval or recall of specific information. In this work, we introduce Blurry Window Attention (BLA) a novel ABC method inspired by SSMs. BLA stores a frequency window from which a blurry KV history is reconstructed via interpolation using Dirichlet kernels. BLA can be understood as a generalization of Sliding Window Attention (SWA) depending on the Dirichlet kernels resolution or as a special case of the Gated Slot Attention (GSA), where the decay factor is implemented with Dirichlet kernels. We describe in details the theory and efficient implementation of BLA. On the Multi-Query Associate Recall (MQAR) synthetic task, we show that the state efficiency of BLA is 8$\times$ better than SWA and is competitive with popular linear attention models, and in the RegBench synthetic task, only BLA and SWA improve their performance as the state size grows among the linear models we tested.

</details>


### [153] [From Confident Closing to Silent Failure: Characterizing False Success in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09863)
*Laksh Advani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在环境中状态显示未完成时却声称任务完成的"虚假成功"故障模式。通过在两个基准测试（tau2-bench和AppWorld）上分析，发现虚假成功普遍存在但比例因场景而异。LLM评委检测虚假成功的能力差（AUROC≤0.65），而轻量级TF-IDF检测器表现优异（AUROC达0.83-0.95），恢复效果比最优LLM评委高4-8倍，延迟低3300倍，建议生产监控应使用轻量级、领域校准的检测器作为主要监控手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在执行任务时可能错误地声称任务已完成而实际未完成（虚假成功），这种失败模式可能导致严重后果却难以被察觉。现有研究缺乏对这一故障模式的系统量化分析，也缺乏对LLM评委检测能力和替代方案的评估。

Method: 作者收集了两个基准测试（tau2-bench 9,876条轨迹来自8个模型家族；AppWorld 1,879条轨迹来自4个模型家族）的数据。使用文本无关的真实标签标注虚假成功。实验对比了5种LLM评委、5种提示策略与全任务规格的检测效果，以及轻量级TF-IDF检测器的性能。

Result: 虚假成功占比因场景而异：单控制域45-48%，双控制域3%，AppWorld编码智能体自评估中75.8%。所有LLM评委配置AUROC均不超过0.65（tau2-bench）和0.54（AppWorld）。TF-IDF检测器实现AUROC 0.83（tau2-bench）和0.95（AppWorld），恢复虚假成功数是最优评委的4-8倍，延迟低3300倍。

Conclusion: LLM评委依赖表面完成代理指标（如自信的结束语言、粗粒度动作序列量）而非验证状态变化，导致检测虚假成功能力严重不足。轻量级、领域校准的TF-IDF检测器作为三线信号更有效，生产监控应优先使用此类方法而非LLM评委。

Abstract: LLM agents can fail silently by asserting task completion when the environment state shows otherwise. We study this failure mode, false success, across two agent benchmarks: 9,876 tau2-bench trajectories from 8 model families and 1,879 AppWorld trajectories from 4 model families with text-independent ground truth. False success is common but varies by setting: 45--48% of failures in single-control tau2-bench domains, 3% in dual-control telecom, and 75.8% among AppWorld self-assessing coding-agent trajectories with explicit status claims. LLM judges fail reliably: no configuration across 5 judges, 5 prompt strategies, and full task specifications exceeds AUROC 0.65 on tau2-bench, and the same judges reach only 0.54 AUROC on AppWorld API-call traces. Judges rely on surface completion proxies -- confident closing language in tau2-bench and coarse action-sequence volume in AppWorld -- rather than verified state changes. Lightweight TF-IDF detectors achieve task-disjoint AUROC 0.83 on tau2-bench and 0.95 on AppWorld, recovering 4--8x more false successes than the best judge at the same flag rate with 3,300x lower latency. These results suggest that production monitoring should use lightweight, domain-calibrated detectors as triage signals rather than relying on LLM judges as the primary monitor for false success.

</details>


### [154] [Alignment Collapse Under KV Cache Quantization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09864)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Adarsh Kumarappan,Mu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV缓存量化在降低LLM推理内存的同时，会悄无声息地破坏模型的安全对齐（如Mistral-7B在仅1.03倍困惑度变化下拒绝率下降15.2%），且不存在通用安全位宽。根本原因是安全特征位于低维激活子空间，对量化噪声的敏感度比全表示空间高2-3个数量级。本文提出逐通道缩减（PCR）诊断方法，将模型分类为三种机械性失效模式，并给出相应修复方向，在20条校准提示下即可用约35 GPU分钟恢复高达97%的丢失对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化研究仅关注困惑度和准确率，完全忽视了量化对模型安全对齐的影响，亟需系统性的安全评估与修复方法。

Method: 提出逐通道缩减（PCR）诊断方法，通过分析量化噪声在激活子空间的影响，将模型分为三种失效模式：离群值压垮安全（outlier-crushes-safety）、离群值即安全（outlier-as-safety）和多层稀释（multi-layer dilution），并据此指导修复策略。

Result: PCR在9个主模型和1个独立族系留出模型上正确预测了修复方向；在KIVI上恢复率达97.2%；在vLLM FP8 KV缓存的NVIDIA GPU生产环境中也验证了漏洞。

Conclusion: KV缓存量化会破坏安全对齐但现有指标无法检测，PCR无需重新训练即可有效诊断并恢复对齐，是一种实用、高效且可泛化的训练无关协议。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache quantization is widely used to reduce Large Language Model (LLM) inference memory, yet existing evaluations solely focus on measuring perplexity and accuracy without assessing the safety impact. In this study, we explore alignment preservation under KV cache quantization. Across eleven instruction-tuned models (3.8B-72B) and five benchmarks (1,894 prompts), we find that low-bit quantization can silently destroy safety alignment: Mistral-7B loses 15.2% of its refusals at only 1.03x perplexity, and no universal safe bit-width exists, with sharp model-specific phase transitions invisible to standard metrics. We identify that the root cause is geometric: safety features occupy a low-dimensional activation subspace 10^2-10^3x more vulnerable to quantization noise than the full representation space perplexity averages over. Inspired by this observation, we propose Per-Channel Reduction (PCR), a diagnostic that classifies each model into one of three mechanistic failure modes: outlier-crushes-safety, where safety lives in non-outlier channels collaterally damaged by outlier-driven scale factors; outlier-as-safety, where safety overlaps outlier channels and finer granularity cannot rescue it; and multi-layer dilution, where safety is distributed across many layers and per-layer fixes fail. PCR predicts the correct mitigation direction on all nine primary models and one held-out model from an independent family using 20 calibration prompts. PCR generalizes across unseen prompts, models, and production quantizers, including KIVI with up to 97.2% recovery, succeeding where attention-based allocation methods fail. The resulting training-free protocol, requiring approximately 35 GPU-minutes, recovers up to 97% of lost alignment at minimal memory overhead, addressing vulnerabilities confirmed in production vLLM serving with FP8 KV cache on NVIDIA GPUs.

</details>


### [155] [LLM-as-a-Discriminator: When Synthetic Tables Still Look Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09865)
*Manel Slokom,Malek Slokom,Thierno Kante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM（大语言模型）作为判别器来审计表格合成数据的隐私风险，通过让LLM区分真实与合成表格样本，实验表明该方法在特定条件下可作为实用的隐私审计信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私与数据共享之间存在张力，许多组织使用合成数据来降低隐私风险，但表格数据的隐私审计仍然困难，甚至人类也难以区分真实与合成表格。因此需要一种有效的隐私审计方法。

Method: 基于LLM判别的方法：让LLM对每个表格样本分类为REAL或SYNTHETIC。测试两种设置：C1（仅表格）和C2（表格+分布元数据）。使用LLaMA（开源）和Gemini（参考模型），在UCI Adult和ACS Census两个数据集上，用CTGAN、TVAE和Gaussian Copula三种合成模型生成数据，共收集451次有效试验。

Result: 模型间差异显著。Adult数据集上，LLaMA的DRS=0%（报告单元格），Gemini对CTGAN和TVAE的DRS=100%。Census上，LLaMA将大多数样本预测为SYNTHETIC，Gemini在C1中保持高识别率，但在C2中对CTGAN和TVAE的识别率下降。还对比了C2ST、记录链接和人工标注实验。

Conclusion: LLM判别在模型选择、按提供商报告和数据编码得到妥善处理时，是一种实用的隐私审计信号。

Abstract: Privacy and data sharing are often in tension. Many organizations use synthetic data to reduce privacy risk and still share useful data. For tabular data, auditing privacy remains hard. In many cases, even humans cannot easily tell if a table is real or synthetic. In this paper, we propose a method based on LLM discrimination. We ask an LLM to classify each table sample as REAL or SYNTHETIC. We test two settings: C1 with table only, and C2 with table plus distributional metadata. We use LLaMA as an open model and Gemini as a reference model. In our experiments, we run three synthesis models, CTGAN, TVAE, and Gaussian Copula, on two public datasets, UCI Adult and ACS Census. We collect 451 valid trials. Our results show clear differences between models. On Adult, LLaMA reaches DRS=0% in reported cells, while Gemini reaches DRS=100% for CTGAN and TVAE. On Census, LLaMA predicts SYNTHETIC for most samples, while Gemini stays high in C1 but drops for CTGAN and TVAE in C2. We also compare with a classifier two-sample test (C2ST) and record linkage as distributional baselines, and with a human pilot of 2 annotators and 240 trials. Our results show that LLM discrimination is a practical privacy audit signal when model choice, per provider reporting, and data encoding are handled with care. For reproducibility, code and experiment scripts are available at https://github.com/SlokomManel/LLM-as-a-Discriminator.

</details>


### [156] [Two to Tango: Coupled Task-Reference Selection for Safe LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09866)
*Xinrui Chen,Jianhao Zhang,Ou Wu,Di Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualSelect框架，通过联合选择安全参考样本和兼容任务样本，在微调过程中保持大语言模型的安全性而不牺牲任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用固定安全示例、全局约束或单方面任务过滤，无法有效应对微调过程中安全行为被侵蚀的问题。

Method: 提出DualSelect耦合框架，通过熵正则化评分代理、惰性参考刷新和梯度校正，在最小化最大化视角下选择高保留损失和任务冲突的安全参考，同时选取兼容的任务样本。

Result: 在1B-8B参数规模的LLM上，DualSelect在保持安全性的同时不损失任务效用；使用REDORCA评估器，安全平均分比最强基线提高至少5.10分，并在各评估器中保持最高安全平均分，开销适中。

Conclusion: DualSelect有效解决了微调中安全行为侵蚀问题，且可扩展到以保留为重点的持续学习场景。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety aligned large language models (LLMs) on downstream data improves adaptation but may erode learned safety behavior. Existing methods use fixed safety examples, global constraints, or one-sided task filtering. Our diagnostics show task updates expose different safety constraints, motivating joint selection of relevant references and compatible task samples. We propose DualSelect, a coupled framework for task and reference selection that refreshes task conditioned safety references before filtering whole task samples compatible with the induced reference direction. Under a minimax view, DualSelect selects safety references with high preservation loss and task conflict, together with compatible task samples, through entropy-regularized scoring surrogates, lazy reference refresh, and gradient correction. On 1B-8B LLMs, DualSelect preserves safety without losing task utility; using the REDORCA judge, it improves Safety Avg. over the strongest baseline by at least 5.10 points and remains highest in Safety Avg. across judges with moderate overhead. This view extends to retention focused continual learning.

</details>


### [157] [SPACE: Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09868)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jiaqi Ding,Qianshan Wei,Nan Zhou,Jiaqi Li,Yongliang Wu,Tongxin Zhu,Xiaolin Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPACE，首个面向多模态大语言模型的免源数据机器遗忘框架，通过文本引导的代理锚点选择和双约束语义隔离，在不访问目标数据的情况下实现概念擦除，性能媲美依赖数据的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型面临日益增长的隐私风险和法规约束，现有机器遗忘方法通常依赖目标概念的视觉数据，但严格的数据保留政策导致这些数据往往不可用，因此需要一种无需源数据的遗忘方法。

Method: SPACE包含两个阶段：(1) 文本引导的代理锚点选择(TPAS)，从共享特征空间中检索语义对齐的代理锚点；(2) 双约束语义隔离(DCSI)，通过优化这些锚点间接擦除目标概念，同时将更新限制在保留知识的零空间中，以保证结构完整性。

Result: 在六个数据集上的大量实验表明，SPACE达到了与最先进的数据依赖方法可比的性能，验证了其在免源数据机器遗忘场景中的有效性。理论证明SPACE严格限制了保留知识的扰动并最大化特征谱熵以维持模型性能。

Conclusion: SPACE是首个专为多模态大语言模型设计的免源数据遗忘框架，通过代理锚点机制在不接触原始数据的情况下实现有效的概念擦除，为隐私保护下的模型更新提供了新方案。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face growing privacy risks and regulatory constraints, machine unlearning (MU) has emerged as a crucial solution for removing sensitive data while preserving model performance. However, existing MU methods typically rely on visual data of the target concepts, which is often unavailable due to strict data retention policies, thus creating a demand for source-free unlearning approaches that operate without access to the target data. In this work, we propose Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure (SPACE), the first source-free unlearning framework specialized for MLLMs. SPACE consists of two stages: (1) Text-Guided Proxy Anchor Selection (TPAS), which retrieves semantically aligned proxy anchors from the shared feature space. (2) Dual-Constraint Semantic Isolation (DCSI), which optimizes these anchors to indirectly erase target concepts. DCSI confines updates to the null space of retained knowledge, ensuring structural integrity. We theoretically prove that SPACE strictly bounds the perturbation on retained knowledge and maximizes feature spectral entropy, thereby maintaining the model's performance. Furthermore, extensive experiments across six datasets show that SPACE achieves performance comparable to that of state-of-the-art data-dependent methods, validating its effectiveness in source-free MU scenarios. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [158] [QSplitFL: Capability Aware Deep Q-Learning for Optimal Split Point Selection in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09869)
*Nazmus Shakib Shadin,Xinyue Zhang,Jingyi Wang,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QSplitFL提出了一种基于深度Q网络的感知能力的拆分点选择框架，通过客户端硬件指标（CPU、内存、电量、网络延迟）的轻量级状态表示、衰减损失下降奖励函数和委员会式DQN架构，在异构设备环境中实现比现有方法更优的收敛速度和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合拆分学习虽能保护隐私并降低训练成本，但固定拆分点无法适配客户端异构硬件能力，导致弱设备过载、通信和服务端负载增加，收敛变慢且稳定性下降。

Method: 提出QSplitFL框架：(1) 使用客户端硬件指标（CPU利用率、内存、电量、网络延迟）构建轻量级状态表示，替代高维模型权重；(2) 设计衰减损失下降奖励函数以优先早期收敛；(3) 采用委员会式DQN架构通过多数投票缓解奖励破解问题。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100数据集上使用CNN、ResNet50、MobileNetV4、ConvNeXt架构的实验表明，QSplitFL相比现有方法取得了更好的收敛速度和更高的准确率，并能有效适应异构设备资源。

Conclusion: QSplitFL通过DQN实现感知客户端能力的自适应拆分点选择，有效解决了异构设备场景下的拆分点优化难题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) combined with Split Learning (SL) is a privacy preserving paradigm that enables training deep neural networks (DNNs) on resource constrained devices while reducing overall training cost. However, determining the optimal split point, meaning the layer where the model is divided still remains a critical challenge, especially when clients have heterogeneous hardware capabilities. Fixed split points can overload weak devices and increase the communication and server load, which slows convergence and reduces stability. This paper introduces QSplitFL, a novel capability-aware Deep Q-Network (DQN) framework for optimal split point selection in Split learning based Federated Learning (SFL) environments. Unlike existing approaches that rely on high-dimensional model weight representations, QSplitFL employs a lightweight state representation derived directly from client hardware metrics, including CPU utilization, memory, battery level, and network latency. The proposed framework incorporates a decayed loss-drop reward function that prioritizes early convergence, and a committee-based DQN architecture with majority voting to mitigate reward hacking. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 datasets using CNN, ResNet50, MobileNetV4, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that our approach achieves better convergence and higher accuracy compared to existing methods, while effectively adapting to heterogeneous device resources. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/AIPO-Lab/QSplitFL.

</details>


### [159] [PatchSTG: Scalable Spatiotemporal Graph Transformers for Traffic Forecasting on Irregular Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09872)
*Jichao Li,Xuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PatchSTG，一种基于分块的时空图Transformer，通过层级化空间表示将不规则传感器划分为均衡保局块，并采用双注意力编码器（内块局部注意力+块间全局注意力）实现近线性复杂度的高效交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际交通网络中传感器分布不均匀，导致现有基于图或注意力的模型在非规则空间结构下可扩展性和有效性受限，且建模大规模时空依赖的计算成本过高。

Method: 1) 基于地理信息将传感器划分为均衡的、保持局部性的块（patch），构建层级化空间表示；2) 设计双注意力编码器，交替进行内块注意力（捕获局部交互）和块间注意力（建模全局依赖），将计算复杂度从二次降至近线性。

Result: 在罗德岛及多个大规模真实交通数据集上，PatchSTG实现了稳定且有竞争力的多步预测性能，同时显著提升了计算效率；消融实验验证了空间划分和双注意力的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于分块的时空建模为非规则空间条件下的交通预测提供了一种可扩展且有效的框架。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a fundamental component of intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging in real-world settings due to irregular sensor distributions and the high computational cost of modeling large-scale spatiotemporal dependencies. In practical traffic networks, sensors are unevenly distributed across regions, leading to non-uniform spatial structures that limit the effectiveness and scalability of existing graph-based and attention-based models. To address these challenges, we propose PatchSTG, a patch-based spatiotemporal graph Transformer designed for efficient forecasting on irregular sensor networks. The key idea is to introduce a hierarchical spatial representation that partitions sensors into balanced, locality-preserving patches based on geographic information. On top of this structure, a dual attention encoder alternates between intra-patch attention for capturing local interactions and inter-patch attention for modeling global dependencies, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to near-linear scaling. We evaluate PatchSTG on real-world traffic data from Rhode Island and additional large-scale datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves stable and competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, while significantly improving computational efficiency. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of spatial partitioning and dual attention in capturing both local and long-range traffic dynamics. These results suggest that patch-based spatiotemporal modeling provides a scalable and effective framework for traffic forecasting under irregular spatial settings.

</details>


### [160] [Rotate2Think: Geometric Priming via Orthogonal Rotation to Improve Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09873)
*Aditya Sharma,Christopher J. Pal,Amal Zouaq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理模型的思考嵌入（thinking embeddings）与输入嵌入在表示空间中呈现高锥度且几何上不同，提出无训练方法Rotate2Think，通过正交Procrustes分析估计输入到思考的旋转，在推理时注入合成思考向量，在30/32的模型-基准配置中提升准确率，并零样本泛化至多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在生成中间推理链时表现优异，但推理过程中隐藏表示的内部结构尚不明确——思考时的隐藏表示与输入嵌入在几何上如何区分？能否利用这种结构在推理时激发出更强的推理能力？

Method: 1) 分析发现输入嵌入和思考嵌入（均采用最后一层隐藏状态的均值池化）均呈现极高锥度，所有向量紧密围绕单一均值方向聚类，但二者方向非共线；2) 将输入到思考的转变建模为旋转问题，通过正交Procrustes分析求解闭式解；3) 提出Rotate2Think：从少量正确求解样本估计旋转矩阵，在推理时于思考分隔符之间注入合成的思考向量，作为推理轨迹起始的几何先导。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码任务的多个基准上，Rotate2Think在30/32的模型-基准配置中提升准确率，并在MATH-Vision上零样本泛化至多模态推理。

Conclusion: 思考嵌入与输入嵌入在表示空间中占据几何不同的区域，通过几何旋转可有效建模二者转换；Rotate2Think作为训练免费的方法，通过注入合成思考向量即可提升推理性能，揭示了表示几何与推理能力之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Reasoning models achieve strong performance on challenging tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces before producing a final answer. Yet the internal structure of representation space when reasoning remains poorly understood: how do a model's hidden representations differ during thinking versus the embeddings of the input prompt, and can this structure be exploited to elicit stronger reasoning at inference time? We show that both input embeddings and thinking embeddings (mean-pooled last-layer hidden states over the prompt and reasoning trace, respectively) exhibit extremely high conicity, with all vectors clustering tightly around a single mean direction. Crucially, these mean input and thinking directions are non-collinear, with thinking embeddings occupying a geometrically distinct region of embedding space across many different models and benchmark tasks. This observation motivates casting the input-to-thinking transition as a rotation problem admitting a closed-form solution via orthogonal Procrustes analysis. We propose Rotate2Think, a training-free method that estimates this rotation from a small set of correctly solved examples and injects the resulting synthetic thinking vector between thinking delimiters at inference time, providing a geometric primer at the onset of the reasoning trace. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks and model families, Rotate2Think improves accuracy in 30 of 32 model-benchmark configurations across mathematics, science, and code tasks, and generalizes zero-shot to multimodal reasoning on MATH-Vision.

</details>


### [161] [Disjoint or Overlapping? Inference Windowing for Reconstruction-Based Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09874)
*Guillaume Coulaud,Reza Akbarinia,Florent Masseglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了重建方法在单变量时间序列异常检测中推理步长（inference stride）的影响，提出了统一的训练、调优和多随机种子评估协议。实验表明，重叠窗口推理在所有模型上均带来一致改进（平均相对增益最高+28%），并会改变方法排名。重建基线在TSB-AD和UCR基准上均表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重建类时间序列异常检测方法因评估实践异构、推理过程未充分说明而导致结果难以比较，缺乏统一可复现的评估协议。

Method: 在单变量离线设定下，提出统一的训练、调优和多随机种子评估协议；研究推理步长（控制子序列是否以不重叠窗口或重叠方式处理）的影响；在TSB-AD基准上评估PCA基线、DLinear、AutoEncoder、TimesNet及Transformer变体等多种重建模型。

Result: 重叠窗口推理在所有模型上带来一致改进，平均相对增益高达+28%，且会改变方法排名；重建基线在TSB-AD和UCR基准上均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 重建类异常检测性能不仅取决于模型架构和训练，还取决于推理选择（如步长），亟需清晰可复现的评估协议；重建基线方法在单变量时间序列异常检测中具有竞争力和实用性。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are widely used for time series anomaly detection, where models are trained to reconstruct subsequences, and anomalies are identified through reconstruction errors. However, reported results are often hard to compare due to heterogeneous evaluation practices and underspecified inference procedures. In this paper, we revisit reconstruction-based anomaly detection in the univariate offline setting and study the role of the inference stride, which controls whether subsequences are processed as disjoint windows or with overlap. We propose a unified training, tuning, and multi-seed evaluation protocol on the curated TSB-AD benchmark, and study how overlapping inference affects anomaly detection performance for a range of reconstruction models, including PCA-based baselines, DLinear, an AutoEncoder, TimesNet, and Transformer variants. The results show that across all models, overlapping windows yield consistent improvements, with average relative gain up to +28%, and can alter method rankings. We further analyze variability across datasets, random seeds, and hyperparameter configurations. Finally, we complement the benchmark study with an evaluation on the full UCR archive using localization criteria aligned with sliding-window reconstruction. Overall, our results highlight that reconstruction-based anomaly detection performance depends not only on model architecture and training, but also on inference choices, motivating a clear and reproducible protocol. Our results show that reconstructionbased baselines achieve strong performance on both TSB-AD and UCR benchmarks, supporting them as competitive and practical approaches for univariate time series anomaly detection.

</details>


### [162] [Integrating Local and Global Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09875)
*Johanne Medina,Tianyi Zhou,Keivin Isufaj,Aristides Gionis,Sanjay Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLU方法，通过融合隐藏状态矩阵的几何熵（全局不确定性）和token级熵（局部不确定性）来量化LLM的不确定性，两者统计上近似正交，全局几何能捕获局部信号遗漏的"自信但错误"模式。GLU作为无监督、单次前向传播的评分方法，在三个模型族和六个基准上匹配或超越所有无监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会自信地产生幻觉，现有不确定性量化方法主要依赖token级信号，忽略了中间隐藏状态的几何结构信息。

Method: 将隐藏状态矩阵的几何复杂度作为全局不确定性度量，token级熵作为局部不确定性度量，通过乘法门融合两者得到GLU分数。

Result: 全局几何熵和局部熵统计上近似正交，捕获不同的失败模式；GLU在三个模型族、六个基准上匹配或超越所有无监督基线。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态几何结构为不确定性量化提供了互补于token级信号的全局视角，GLU方法简单高效且架构无关。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate confidently, making uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for reliable deployment. Existing methods rely predominantly on token-level signals, leaving the geometric structure of intermediate hidden states underused. In this paper, we take the geometric complexity of hidden-state matrices as a measure of the global uncertainty of LLMs, while treating token-level uncertainty estimation as a local metric. We show that hidden-state geometric entropy (global uncertainty) and token-level entropy (local uncertainty) are statistically near-orthogonal, capturing distinct failure regimes for reliability prediction. In particular, global geometry recovers the confident-but-wrong failure mode that local signals systematically miss. Building on this, we propose Global-Local Uncertainty (GLU), an unsupervised, single-pass score that fuses the two signals via a multiplicative gate. Across three model families and six benchmarks, GLU matches or outperforms all unsupervised baselines while requiring only a single forward pass and remaining length-normalized and architecture-agnostic.

</details>


### [163] [Structured Adaptive Tensor Prediction for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10085)
*Zhen Qin,Yang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对矩阵值时间序列的流式预测问题，提出了自适应张量回归框架，包括矩阵对矩阵（MoM）和张量对矩阵（ToM）两种形式，并开发了在线SGD算法。其中ToM通过将多个响应堆叠成高阶张量，在稳态误差和去噪能力上优于MoM。文章还分析了SGD在时变环境下的跟踪行为，并为ToM在稀疏、低秩及联合稀疏低秩结构下建立了固定时间恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵值时间序列方法主要针对静态设置，缺乏对流式和时变环境的适应性；而自适应滤波技术多局限于标量或向量数据，矩阵值时间序列的自适应预测问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出自适应张量回归框架（MoM和ToM两种形式），基于随机梯度下降（SGD）开发在线学习算法；通过将多个时间响应堆叠为高阶张量利用时间结构；从统计角度分析ToM在稀疏、低秩及联合稀疏低秩结构下的恢复保证。

Result: ToM模型在稳态误差和去噪能力上优于MoM；建立了ToM在一般低维结构下的固定时间恢复保证，并刻画了SGD在时变动态下的跟踪行为。

Conclusion: 将多个响应跨时间堆叠成高阶张量能提升预测性能，ToM比MoM更优；理论保证了ToM在低维结构下的可恢复性，为矩阵值流式预测提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Matrix-valued time series arise in a wide range of applications, such as spatio-temporal data from medical imaging and geophysics. Existing methods are mainly designed for static settings and lack adaptability to streaming and time-varying environments. Adaptive filtering techniques have also been largely limited to data with scalar or vector values, leaving adaptive forecasting for matrix-valued time series inadequately understood. To bridge these gaps, we develop an adaptive tensor regression framework that includes Matrix-on-Matrix (MoM) and Tensor-on-Matrix (ToM) formulations for streaming matrix-valued prediction. The two formulations differ in whether to directly model matrix-valued outputs or to exploit temporal structure via higher-order tensor representations. For the proposed tensor regression framework, we develop stochastic gradient descent (SGD) algorithms for online learning. We show that stacking multiple responses across time into higher-order tensors improves performance; in particular, the ToM achieves lower steady-state error and stronger denoising capability than MoM, motivating our focus on the ToM model. We further characterize the tracking behavior of SGD under time-varying dynamics. From a statistical perspective, we establish fixed-time recovery guarantees for ToM under general low-dimensional structures, including sparsity, low-rankness, and their joint sparselow-rank models.

</details>


### [164] [Calibrating Overconfidence Without Sacrificing Confidence: Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09876)
*Ke Li,Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Feng Liu,Qunli Zhang,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种推理时方法PCHI，通过冻结探针检测错误但自信的回答，并条件性地调整注意力头输出，从而在减少过度自信的同时保持正确回答的自信。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上，将82.2%的错误-自信读取转换为不自信，ECE从21.9%降至9.2%，仅损害5.1%的正确-自信读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常在错误答案上表现出高置信度，现有校准方法通常全局或分数级操作，虽减少过度自信但也会侵蚀正确答案的合理自信。

Method: PCHI（Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention）：推理时方法，使用冻结探针检测可能错误但自信的响应，然后条件性地重新缩放下游注意力头在置信度生成期间的输出。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上，读取标记PCHI将82.2%的错误-自信读取转换为不自信；联合干预将ECE从21.9%降至9.2%，仅损害5.1%的正确-自信读取。Gemma3-4B上也有读取标记效应，但上游干预较弱且更依赖掩码。

Conclusion: 通过条件性内部干预可以选择性减少言语化的过度自信，部分解耦了抑制不合理自信与损失合理自信之间的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models often express high confidence in answers that are wrong. Standard calibration remedies typically act globally or at the score level, reducing unwarranted confidence but also risking erosion of warranted confidence on correct answers. We introduce Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI), an inference-time method that uses a frozen probe to detect likely wrong-but-confident responses and conditionally rescales downstream attention-head outputs during confidence generation. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct solving OpenMathInstruct problems with a structured binary confidence field, readout-token PCHI converts 82.2% of originally wrong-yes confidence readouts to $\texttt{no}$, while a joint intervention across upstream confidence-template tokens reduces ECE from 21.9% to 9.2% and damages only 5.1% of originally correct-yes readouts. The readout-token effect also appears on Gemma3-4B, though upstream interventions are weaker and more mask-dependent. These results show that verbalized overconfidence can be selectively reduced through conditionally applied internal intervention, partially decoupling the suppression of unwarranted confidence from the loss of warranted confidence.

</details>


### [165] [Baseline-Free Policy Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10321)
*Carlos S. Sepúlveda,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将GRPO（来自LLM对齐的算法）应用于神经组合优化，替代REINFORCE中的rollout基线，在TSP和CVRP上避免了训练崩溃，且无需外部基线即可达到接近POMO的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REINFORCE with rollout baseline在难实例上会产生噪声梯度估计，导致训练不稳定甚至崩溃，亟需一种无需外部基线的稳定训练算法。

Method: 将GRPO（组相对策略优化）从LLM对齐领域引入NCO，通过组内轨迹归一化优势值以消除基线；在RL4CO框架下对五种RL算法进行受控对比实验。

Result: (i) GRPO避免了REINFORCE在TSP-100上的训练崩溃（代价从9.8骤降至52.1后无法恢复）；(ii) 在相同梯度更新次数下，GRPO解质量接近POMO（差距在2%以内），且无需外部基线；(iii) P3O在TSP上表现良好，但在CVRP上方差较大。

Conclusion: GRPO是一种有前景的无基线替代方案，尤其适用于基线依赖训练不稳定的场景。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) trains autoregressive policies to solve routing problems. The standard training algorithm, REINFORCE with a rollout baseline, requires maintaining and periodically updating a frozen copy of the policy for variance reduction. This baseline introduces a structural vulnerability: on harder instances, a poor baseline produces noisy gradient estimates that can destabilize training. We evaluate Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an algorithm from large language model alignment that eliminates the baseline entirely by normalizing advantages within groups of sampled trajectories. In a controlled comparison of five RL algorithms on TSP and CVRP benchmarks within the RL4CO framework, we find that: (i) GRPO avoids the training collapse observed with REINFORCE on TSP-100, where performance degrades from cost 9.8 to 52.1 immediately after the warmup phase and does not recover under extended training; (ii) at matched gradient updates, GRPO achieves solution quality within 2% of POMO, a strong AM-based multi-start baseline, while requiring no external baseline; and (iii) P3O, a pairwise preference algorithm also from the alignment literature, is competitive on TSP but shows higher variability on CVRP. These results identify GRPO as a promising baseline-free alternative for NCO, particularly in settings where baseline-dependent training becomes fragile.

</details>


### [166] [Streaming Knowledge Compilation: Proactive Materiality-Scored Pinning for Time-Evolving LLM Wikis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09877)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流式知识编译（Streaming Knowledge Compilation）框架，通过材料性信号φ_t在固定token预算下动态维护编译知识库，并证明O(√T log K)累积遗憾界。在金融（异常波动预测）和维基百科（异常编辑比AER）两个领域实例化，揭示LLM作为评判者在后训练知识上的混杂效应，指出遗憾分析而非绝对QA分数才是可靠评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM维基系统假设语料库静态不变，但实际信息环境是持续演化的。需要一种能在文档流、固定token预算且未知未来查询条件下，动态维护编译知识的方案。

Method: 提出材料性信号φ_t(k,n)∈[0,1]作为文档重要性的代理评分，在查询到达前主动固定关键知识。在金融领域用Llama 3.1 8B分类头预测异常股票波动率作为φ_t；在维基百科领域用跨截面归一化的编辑速度（AER）作为φ_t。理论证明O(√T log K)遗憾界，其中ε=E[|φ_t-φ̂_t|]是唯一领域相关量。

Result: 金融领域：φ_t预测AUROC=0.728（76K文章，严格时间划分），预测为重要的文章实现1.49倍更高的已实现前向波动率。端到端QA评估：金融累积遗憾收敛至-20.0（-0.12/步）；维基百科收敛至+16.0（+0.13/步）。发现LLM-as-judge在后训练知识上的混杂效应。

Conclusion: O(√T log K)保证适用于任何可通过流信号预测知识缺口领域。正遗憾符号确认维基百科编辑内容确实是后训练知识——更丰富的上下文持续改善分数（No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74），并消除了混杂效应。

Abstract: LLM wiki systems compile knowledge into pre-filled KV caches for efficient inference, but assume a static corpus -- an assumption that fails whenever the underlying information landscape evolves. We formalize Streaming Knowledge Compilation: given a document stream, a fixed token budget, and future queries unknown at ingestion time, maintain a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret against an offline oracle with perfect foresight. The enabling insight is a materiality signal $φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$ that scores document importance for entity $k$ at time $t$, acting as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning before queries arrive; we prove an $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ regret bound where $\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$ is the only domain-specific quantity. We instantiate in two domains: finance, where $φ_t$ is abnormal stock volatility predicted by frozen Llama 3.1 8B classification head (AUROC = 0.728 on 76K articles, strict temporal split; $1.49\times$ higher realized forward volatility for predicted-material articles); and Wikipedia, where $φ_t$ is the Abnormal Edit Ratio (AER), a cross-sectionally normalized edit velocity -- showing the same algorithm generalizes beyond the finance domain. End-to-end QA evaluation on 173 matched pairs (finance) and 119 (Wikipedia) reveals a pervasive LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge, establishing that regret analysis -- not absolute QA scores -- is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0 (-0.12/step); Wikipedia to +16.0 (+0.13/step), with the positive sign confirming that Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training -- richer context consistently improves scores (No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74) -- and eliminates this confound. The $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals.

</details>


### [167] [NOVA: Symbolic Regression Discovery of Interpretable Car-Following and Lane-Change Models with Driver Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10583)
*Ishak Abassi,Nassim Ali Bouazzouni,Farah Ibelaiden,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOVA是一个自主符号回归框架，能从原始轨迹数据中识别可解释的跟车和换道结构，在NGSIM数据集上优于现有基线方法，并发现人类跟车行为的鲁棒非线性主干项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶行为模型依赖强先验假设或黑箱方法，缺乏从数据中自主发现可解释符号结构的通用框架。

Method: 采用Rust驱动的确定性搜索引擎，在向前移位滚动均值预测目标下，从原始轨迹数据中穷举搜索代数结构；使用双预处理管道评估；扩展至多项logit框架进行换道建模。

Result: 跟车任务RMSE=1.376 m/s² (R²=15.57%)，优于SR-LLM基线0.135 m/s²；换道任务67.4%平衡准确率，超越基线+29.8个百分点；特征零样本迁移R²损失低于3个百分点。

Conclusion: NOVA框架成功从数据中自主发现可解释驾驶行为符号结构，其发现的非线性项与碰撞避免的心理物理理论相关，且具有良好的跨站点迁移能力。

Abstract: We present NOVA, an autonomous symbolic regression framework that identifies interpretable car-following and lane-change structures from raw trajectory data with minimal behavioral priors. Applied to 4,765,788 active driving observations from the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 datasets, NOVA's deterministic Rust-powered search engine evaluates over 10,000 candidate algebraic structures and identifies a compact two-term acceleration model under a forward-shifted rolling-mean prediction target. Evaluated under two complementary preprocessing pipelines, NOVA achieves $RMSE = 1.376 m/s^2$ ($R^2 = 15.57\%$) on the intent-forecasting benchmark, outperforming the best recalibrated symbolic-regression baseline (SR-LLM, PNAS~2025) by 0.135 m/s$^2$ in RMSE under an identical evaluation protocol. Across eight independent experiments, a single dominant nonlinear term emerges as a robust backbone of human car-following; a residual-guided extension further links the selected structure to an established psychophysical theory of collision avoidance. The discovered feature operators transfer zero-shot between freeway sites with under 3 pp $R^2$ loss. Extended to lane-change modelling within a multinomial logit framework, NOVA achieves 67.4\% balanced accuracy under strict vehicle-ID holdout on 502 unseen drivers, surpassing existing lane-changing baselines by +29.8 percentage points on a three-class problem.

</details>


### [168] [FailureScope: Cross-Regime Behavioral Diagnosis of Language Model Weaknesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09878)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FailureScope提出一种行为诊断方法，通过留一模交叉验证聚类评估探针，在不同评测场景（单轮基准、多轮对话、对抗性攻击）中生成稳定可解释的失败分类，并实现高效采样与跨模型失败预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准仅报告总体准确率，无法告知实践者模型具体缺少哪些能力，需要一种能细粒度诊断模型失败模式的通用方法。

Method: 提出FailureScope方法，使用留一模（LOMO）策略，基于跨模型的通过/失败模式对评估探针进行聚类，生成行为化的失败分类体系。

Result: 在2,664个单轮任务上，基于分类的条件采样仅需50个任务即可达Kendall's tau=0.81（随机为0.34），跨模型失败预测AUC达0.88；在多轮对话和对抗性智能体攻击场景中同样恢复出可解释聚类，并发现LLM裁判与实际执行间存在73-100个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 行为聚类是一种可移植的诊断原语，能够泛化到单一基准之外；作者开源了流程、三个标注语料库及跨场景分类体系。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks report aggregate accuracy, but practitioners need to know which specific capabilities a model lacks. We introduce FailureScope, a behavioral-diagnosis method that clusters evaluation probes by their cross-model pass/fail patterns (leave-one-model-out, LOMO), and show it yields stable, interpretable failure taxonomies across three regimes usually studied separately: single-turn benchmarks, multi-turn dialogue, and adversarial agent attacks. On 2,664 single-turn tasks across 18 models, taxonomy-conditioned sampling reaches Kendall's tau = 0.81 at 50 tasks (versus 0.34 for random selection), and cross-model failure prediction reaches AUC 0.88. The same primitive recovers interpretable clusters on a 363-task multi-turn corpus and on 630 adversarial agent traces, where it exposes a meta-failure mode: a 73-100 percentage-point gap between LLM-judge ASR and real execution. Cluster cohesion remains strong across all three regimes, which we take as evidence that behavioral clustering is a portable diagnosis primitive that generalizes beyond any single benchmark. We release the pipeline, three annotated corpora, and the cross-regime taxonomies.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Doubly Sparse Explicitly Conditioned Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10975)
*Tudor Pistol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化、显式条件化的学习变换方法，将其表示为固定规范矩阵与数据自适应稀疏分量的乘积，在保留快速稳定分析变换优势的同时引入可控的数据自适应性。算法基于不精确近端方法框架，利用新推导的闭式投影算子，在双稀疏变换学习问题上取得了最先进的结果，且计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析变换（如DFT、DCT）虽提供高效算法和鲁棒稀疏表示，但假设固定的数据先验，无法准确捕捉更受限信号类别的特定结构。现有数据自适应学习变换虽能减少变换域残差，但需要在泛化能力与最小化近似误差之间权衡。本文旨在设计一种兼具快速稳定分析变换优势与数据自适应能力的学习变换。

Method: 将学习变换建模为固定规范矩阵（如DFT/DCT）与数据自适应稀疏分量的乘积形式，从而保留结构化和显式条件化特性。在不精确近端方法框架下推导了闭式投影算子，用于优化求解。该公式化方法在现有文献中未见报道，具有新颖性。

Result: 在双稀疏变换学习问题上取得了最先进的结果；与密集变体相比性能相当，但计算成本显著降低，有时收敛速度更快，且能更好避免陷入不良局部最小值。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化显式条件化变换学习框架有效结合了固定分析变换的高效性与数据自适应的灵活性，在保持较低计算复杂度的同时实现了优异的稀疏表示性能，为稀疏编码和变换学习提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Finding convenient spaces in which certain hypotheses regarding an assumed sparse structure of natural signals hold true has become a desirable result in recent research, its implications being reflected in areas such as data compression, noise reduction and feature extraction. While the extensively used analytical transforms, such as DFT or DCT, already provide efficient algorithms and robust sparse representations, they assume a fixed prior about the data, failing to accurately capture the specific structure of more restrictive classes of signals. To address this, the concept of a data-adaptive, learnt transform has been introduced in the literature, allowing for the reduction of a residual term in the transform domain. More recent studies have shown that the condition number serves as a good metric in this context, where the desired outcome alternates between a generalizing tendency and one that achieves minimal approximation error. Motivated by these considerations, we introduce the learning of a structured, explicitly conditioned transform formulated as the product of a fixed canonical matrix and a refining data-adaptive sparse component. This approach seeks to preserve the advantages of fast and stable analytical transforms, while introducing controllable adaptivity to the data. No references that concern this specific formulation have been identified so far, indicating its novelty. The proposed algorithm is motivated within the framework of inexact proximal methods, leveraging a newly derived closed-form projection operator. Empirical observations demonstrate state-of-the-art results on the doubly sparse transform learning problem and comparable performance with its dense variant at significantly lower computational costs and sometimes faster convergence and better avoidance of bad local minima.

</details>


### [170] [Operator Fusion for LLM Inference on the Tensix Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09879)
*Qingbo Wu,Ke Li,Wenzhu Wang,Jie Yu,Ruian Zhang,Lili Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Tenstorrent Tensix架构提出了算子融合策略，将RMSNorm与矩阵乘法融合以提升数据局部性，并利用NoC多播机制支持多核并行，在Wormhole平台上实现高达37.44%的注意力延迟缩减和7.91%的解码层延迟缩减，同时保持数值一致性（PCC > 98.75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Transformer模型在Tenstorrent Tensix架构上推理时，频繁的DRAM读写中间结果和调度开销导致的性能瓶颈，特别是内存受限与计算受限算子交替执行带来的低效问题。

Method: 提出算子融合策略，将RMSNorm与自注意力及FFN中的矩阵乘法融合，使内存受限与计算受限算子在片上SRAM中连续执行，减少DRAM访问；同时利用基于NoC的多播机制，通过行/列主节点高效分发输入和权重到核心网格，缓解DRAM带宽争用。

Result: 在Wormhole平台上对Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-4B模型进行实验，注意力部分延迟降低最高37.44%，MLP部分降低15.89%，每解码层降低7.91%，PCC保持在98.75%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的算子融合方法与NoC多播机制能显著提升Tenstorrent Tensix架构上Transformer模型的端到端推理效率，在保证数值一致性的前提下实现可观的延迟优化。

Abstract: This study addresses on-device inference bottlenecks of Transformer models on Tenstorrent's Tensix architecture and proposes an operator fusion strategy that enhances data locality. RMSNorm is fused with matrix multiplication in self-attention and in the FFN, enabling back-to-back execution of memory-bound and compute-bound operators in on-chip SRAM to significantly reduce DRAM reads/writes of intermediate results and scheduling overhead. To support multi-core parallelism, a NoC-based multicast mechanism is leveraged in which row/column master nodes efficiently distribute inputs and weights across the core mesh, alleviating DRAM bandwidth contention. Experiments on the Wormhole platform with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen3-0.6B, and Qwen3-4B show up to 37.44% latency reduction for attention and 15.89% for MLP, with up to 7.91% reduction per decoder layer, while Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) remains above 98.75%, confirming significant end-to-end efficiency gains under numerical consistency.

</details>


### [171] [Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11118)
*Rahul Roy,Nur Sunar,Jayashankar M. Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双边服务平台上的动态商品搭配问题，其中平台对顾客和商家的选择模型参数均未知，提出了一种数据驱动算法实现学习与优化，并证明了其遗憾值随时间的多对数增长及最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态商品搭配研究大多仅考虑单边（顾客侧）未知参数，而双边服务平台中顾客和商家均存在选择行为且参数未知，如何同时学习双边未知参数并优化平台收益是一个尚未解决的关键挑战。

Method: 构建离散时间动态模型：每期顾客到达后平台展示商家集合，顾客按MNL模型选择至多一个商家提出交易；经过固定期数后，商家按另一MNL模型选择至多一个顾客。平台对双边MNL参数均未知，设计数据驱动算法同时学习参数并优化展示策略，以遗憾值（与全知基准的收益差）衡量性能。

Result: 所提算法的最坏情况遗憾值随时间呈多对数（polylogarithmic）增长，并推导出匹配的下界，证明该算法的速率最优性。

Conclusion: 本文首次研究了双边选择参数均未知的动态商品搭配问题，提出的算法在理论上达到了最优学习速率，为双边服务平台的实际运营提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


### [172] [Hyperparameter Learning for Latent Factorization of Tensors for Representation Learning to Large-scale Dynamic Weighted Directed Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09880)
*Yaqian Zhan,Jialan He,Tianzhu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大规模动态加权有向网络（DWDN）的张量分解（LFT）模型手动调参成本高的问题，提出基于差分进化（DE）的自动超参数优化框架DE-LFT，自动学习三个正则化参数，在四个真实数据集上取得了更低的MAE和RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LFT模型的性能高度依赖超参数选择，而现有手动调参或网格搜索方法计算资源消耗大、人工成本高。

Method: 将差分进化（DE）集成到LFT模型的训练过程中，自动优化三个正则化参数λ₁、λ₂和λ₃。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，DE-LFT相比手动调参基线取得了更低的MAE和RMSE。

Conclusion: DE-LFT能够自适应搜索超参数空间，提升预测精度，同时减少大量参数调优的需求。

Abstract: Large-scale dynamic weighted directed networks (DWDNs) are widely used to model time-varying interactions among nodes. Latent factorization of tensors (LFT) extracts target knowledge from DWDNs via low-rank embedding. However, similar to many machine learning models, the performance of LFT heavily depends on the selection of hyperparameters. In practice, these parameters are often tuned manually or through grid search, which requires significant computational resources and human effort. Motivated by this challenge, this paper proposes an automated hyperparameter optimization framework based on Differential Evolution (DE) for LFT (DE-LFT). The proposed method integrates DE into the training process of the LFT model to automatically learn optimal regularization parameters $λ_1$, $λ_2$ and $λ_3$. As a result, the model can adaptively search the hyperparameter space and improve prediction accuracy. Experimental results on four real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves lower MAE and RMSE compared with manually tuned baselines while reducing the need for extensive parameter tuning.

</details>


### [173] [Algorithmic and Minimax Complexities in Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11171)
*Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的MAIR算法信息框架，将GP-UCB和MAMS（DEC）两种方法置于共同语言下，证明算法复杂度与类宽度量回答不同问题，并提出了结合两者优势的保护性主算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB与DEC/MAMS方法看似属于不同理论体系，缺乏统一的算法信息视角来理解两者的关系与差异。

Method: 提出MAIR统一框架和异构正半定算法先验，泛化GP-UCB分析与MAMS算法，设计保护性主算法结合两者优势。

Result: 在过参数化模型中，算法复杂度比类宽度量或DEC证书更具信息性；算法信息与类宽度量回答不同问题，导致不同间隙。

Conclusion: 核Bandit为区分算法信息与类宽度量提供了清晰的数学场景，两种视角各有价值且不可相互替代。

Abstract: Gaussian-process upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) and decision-estimation-coefficient (DEC) methods may appear, at first sight, to belong to different theories. This paper places the two viewpoints in a common algorithmic-information language for frequentist RKHS bandits. GP-UCB fixes an algorithmic, rather than true, Gaussian-process prior and exploits realized-trajectory complexity together with computational tractability, whereas MAMS optimizes a robust class-wide MAIR/DEC envelope. Through the unified MAIR framework and heterogeneous positive-semidefinite algorithmic priors, we generalize both the GP-UCB analysis and the MAMS algorithm, propose a safeguarded master that combines their advantages, and provide a kernel-bandit construction showing that algorithmic complexity can be more informative than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates in overparameterized models. The resulting message is that algorithmic information and class-wide minimax coefficients answer different questions and can lead to different gaps; kernel bandits provide a clean setting in which this distinction becomes mathematically visible.

</details>


### [174] [Toward Calibrated, Fair, and accurate Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09881)
*Ryan Brown,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Face-Fairness (FF)框架，包含三种变体：FFT（无需人口统计标签）、FF-Max（需标签）和FF-Discover（嵌入发现分组），用于在不牺牲准确率的前提下减少深度伪造检测器的人口统计偏差。该方法即插即用、与检测器无关、计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器在不同人口统计群体间存在显著的性能差距，而现有公平性方法要么需要人口统计标签，要么需要重新训练模型，要么会牺牲检测精度。

Method: 提出Face-Fairness (FF)框架，核心贡献为Face-Feature Tuning (FFT)——首个无需人口统计标签的深度伪造检测公平性方法，通过轻量级校准器对冻结的人脸嵌入进行logit重映射。另有两个变体：FF-Max（利用人口统计标签最大化最差群体准确率）和FF-Discover（通过嵌入发现群体实现相同目标）。

Result: 在域内和跨数据集测试中，FF一致减少了FPR/TPR差距，提升了最小群体准确率，同时保持甚至提高了整体准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法即插即用、与检测器无关、运行时开销可忽略不计，且无需访问身份属性，为深度伪造检测的公平性提供了一种实用解决方案。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors show large performance gaps across demographic groups. Existing fairness approaches require demographic labels, retraining, or sacrifice accuracy. We introduce Face-Fairness (FF), a plug-and-play framework for bias mitigation. Our primary contribution, Face-Feature Tuning (FFT), is the first demographic label-free fairness method demonstrated for deepfake detection: a lightweight calibrator that performs a logit remapping conditioned on frozen face embeddings. We complement FFT with two variants: FF-Max, which maximizes worst-group accuracy when demographics are available, and FF-Discover, which does the same with embedding-discovered groups. Across in-domain and cross-dataset test settings, FF consistently reduces FPR/TPR gaps and improves minimum group accuracy while maintaining (often improving) overall accuracy. The approach is detector-agnostic, adds negligible runtime overhead, and requires no access to identity attributes.

</details>


### [175] [TD-Grokking: Learning from Zero-Reward Problems by Training-Time Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09883)
*Ningyuan Xi,Hao Xu,Hongsheng Xin,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-Grokking，一种训练时分解框架，通过递归地将零奖励问题分解为可验证的子问题树，将无效训练信号转化为有效信号，从而解决强化学习在零奖励问题上的优化困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR在高度挑战性的零奖励问题上失败，因为所有采样轨迹均失败，无法提供优化信号；此前方法（密集过程监督、部分奖励分配、前缀引导探索）受限于任务约束或未能充分赋予策略模型解决原始问题的能力。

Method: 提出TD-Grokking，一种训练时递归分解框架，将不可解的根问题递归分解为自包含、可验证的子问题，形成层次树结构，其中可解的叶节点提供非零奖励信号。

Result: 在数学和医学任务上的评估表明，TD-Grokking优于原始GRPO及所有基线方法，有效将零奖励样例转化为可用训练信号，实现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 训练时分解能够有效将零奖励样例转化为可用训练信号，为解决零奖励问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in reasoning tasks, largely driven by post-training paradigms, especially reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, a critical bottleneck persists: RLVR fails on highly challenging zero-reward problems, where all sampled reasoning trajectories yield uniformly failed outcomes, providing no optimization signal to drive model improvement. Prior efforts to address this limitation, such as dense process supervision, partial reward assignment, or prefix-guided exploration, suffer from inherent task constraints or do not fully equip the policy model with the capabilities necessary to solve the original intractable problems. To address this, we propose TD-Grokking, a training-time decomposition framework for zero-reward problems. It recursively decomposes intractable root problems into self-contained, verifiable subproblems, forming hierarchical trees where solvable leaves provide non-zero rewards. Evaluations on mathematical and medical tasks show that TD-Grokking outperforms vanilla GRPO as well as all baseline approaches. Together with detailed analysis, these results confirm that training-time decomposition effectively converts zero-reward examples into usable training signals, enabling consistent performance gains. Our code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TD-Grokking-6567/.

</details>


### [176] [TENP: Trapezoidal Expert Neuron Pruning For Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09885)
*Jiangyang He,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TENP是一种针对MoE大语言模型的结构化梯形专家-神经元剪枝框架，通过识别重要专家并对其余专家进行神经元剪枝，在保留路由拓扑的同时实现高效压缩。在DeepSeek模型上，40%专家稀疏度和63.76%激活参数下，仅损失1个精度点，代码生成任务甚至超越完整模型10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型部署受限于大量静态专家参数。已有压缩方法要么移除整个专家（破坏路由拓扑、损害性能），要么依赖非结构化权重剪枝（实际效率有限），缺乏兼顾性能与效率的结构化压缩方案。

Method: 提出TENP框架：(1) 用少量样本评估专家重要性，同时考虑专家输出幅度和改变输入向量方向的能力；(2) 保留重要专家，对次要专家进行专家神经元剪枝（ENP）；(3) 从浅层到深层以梯形模式保留模型参数；(4) ENP中测量每个神经元对专家输出的投影贡献来识别重要神经元。

Result: 在Qwen和DeepSeek模型上实验，40%路由专家稀疏度+平均63.76%激活专家参数下，DeepSeek模型仅比完整模型损失1个精度点，代码生成任务超越完整模型10%。

Conclusion: TENP有效解决了MoE模型部署中的参数冗余问题，在保持高性能的同时实现结构化压缩，且在某些任务上甚至优于完整模型，表明结构化剪枝是MoE模型压缩的可行方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts large language models (LLMs) scale efficiently through sparse activation, yet their deployment is fundamentally constrained by the large static parameter footprint of experts. Existing compression approaches either remove entire experts, disrupting routing topology and harming performance, or rely on unstructured weight pruning with limited practical efficiency. To address the limitations, we propose TENP, a structured Trapezoidal ExpertNeuron Pruning framework. Using a few samples, we identify and retain important experts, while applying expert neuron pruning (ENP) to less important experts, reserving model parameters in a trapezoidal pattern from shallow to deep layers. When evaluating expert importance, we jointly consider both the magnitude of the expert output and its ability to change the direction of the input vector. For ENP, we measure each neuron's projected contribution to the expert output to identify and retain important neurons. We conduct extensive experiments on the Qwen and DeepSeek models. Under a routing expert sparsity of 40% and an average of 63.76% activated expert parameters, the DeepSeek model suffers only a 1-point drop in accuracy compared to the full-parameter model. Moreover, it outperforms the full-parameter model by 10% on code generation tasks.

</details>


### [177] [SHAPE: Coalition-Aware Expert Pruning for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09886)
*Yuhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPE是一种基于Shapley值的MoE专家剪枝框架，通过将路由轨迹建模为合作博弈，识别对高质量协作不可或缺的专家，并采用质量-覆盖选择规则保留关键专家，在20%-40%剪枝率下保持性能并降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家剪枝方法独立评估每个专家，忽视了MoE推理本质上是"联盟式"的——输出依赖于路由选出的top-k专家组合的协作。现有方法无法识别哪些专家在合作中真正不可或缺。

Method: 1) 将小规模校准集上的路由轨迹建模为经验合作博弈；2) 通过Shapley值分配交互感知的专家价值；3) 提出质量-覆盖选择规则，在每层保留覆盖α比例非负Shapley质量的最小专家子集；4) 使用二分法匹配目标保持率。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GPT-OSS-20B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite三个现代MoE骨干上，SHAPE在20%和40%专家剪枝下保持竞争性准确率，优于全局和逐层剪枝变体，并显著降低峰值GPU内存占用。

Conclusion: SHAPE通过显式建模层内专家合作，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了更鲁棒的MoE剪枝，是首个将合作博弈论应用于MoE专家剪枝的工作。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models achieve strong quality with low per-token compute, yet their deployment is often limited by the memory wall: the full expert pool must remain resident to support token-dependent routing. Expert pruning is a direct remedy, but prior criteria typically score experts independently and overlook that MoE inference is inherently \emph{coalitional}, where outputs arise from routed top-$k$ expert combinations. We propose \textbf{SHAPE}, a task-driven pruning framework that explicitly models \emph{intra-layer} expert cooperation. SHAPE formulates routing traces on a small calibration set as an empirical cooperative game and assigns interaction-aware expert values via a Shapley-style attribution over observed top-$k$ coalitions, enabling the identification of experts that are essential for high-utility collaborations rather than merely frequent. To preserve MoE topology under a global pruning budget, SHAPE further introduces a \emph{quality-coverage} selection rule that retains, in each layer, the minimal expert subset covering an $α$ fraction of non-negative Shapley mass, while using bisection to match a target keep rate. Experiments on three modern MoE backbones (Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite) across diverse benchmarks show that SHAPE consistently improves robustness over global and layer-wise pruning variants, maintaining competitive accuracy under 20\% and 40\% expert pruning without additional training and delivering clear reductions in peak GPU memory footprint. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/Alizen-1009/Shapley-Moe.

</details>


### [178] [SocraticPO: Policy Optimization via Interactive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09887)
*Zirui Liu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Xianquan Wang,Jiayu Liu,Tingyue Pan,Qingchuan Li,Jing Sha,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SocraticPO是一种通过Socratic式自然语言引导和奖励衰减机制增强大语言模型强化学习的策略优化框架，在科学推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的标量结果奖励（如二元正确性）虽提供优化方向，但很少解释模型应如何修正错误推理，容易鼓励捷径学习和脆弱策略。

Method: 学生在rollout中先独立作答；若答案错误，教师诊断并提供简洁纠正性引导，学生在扩展上下文下继续推理。正确回答若在教师干预后获得，仅获衰减奖励，防止模型将教师帮助视为免费奖励路径。该方法仅修改rollout过程，保持标准期望奖励目标不变，可集成到现有策略梯度后端（如Reinforce++），且教师仅提供文本级引导，无需访问logits或分布匹配。

Result: 在SciKnowEval的本科级科学推理基准上，SocraticPO优于强RL和自蒸馏基线。消融实验表明，定向引导和奖励衰减均不可或缺，其中奖励衰减减少了模型对辅助纠正的依赖。

Conclusion: SocraticPO通过自然语言引导结合奖励衰减有效提升了RL推理训练的质量和鲁棒性，且具有良好的即插即用兼容性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models usually supervises reasoning with scalar outcome rewards, such as binary correctness. Such rewards provide an optimization direction but rarely explain how a model should revise its mistaken reasoning, which can encourage shortcut learning and brittle policies. We propose \textbf{SocraticPO} (Socratic Policy Optimization), a policy-optimization framework that augments RL rollouts with Socratic-style natural-language guidance. During rollout, the student first answers independently; if the answer is incorrect, a teacher diagnoses the attempt and provides concise corrective guidance, after which the student continues under the expanded context. Crucially, this guidance is paired with reward decay: correct answers obtained after teacher intervention only receive decayed rewards, preventing the policy from treating teacher help as a free path to reward. Since SocraticPO only modifies the rollout process while leaving the standard expected-reward objective intact, it can be plugged into existing policy-gradient backends such as Reinforce++. Moreover, because the teacher provides only text-level guidance, SocraticPO can leverage stronger black-box teacher models without requiring access to logits or distribution matching. On undergraduate-level scientific reasoning benchmarks from SciKnowEval, SocraticPO improves over strong RL and self-distillation baselines. Ablations show that both targeted guidance and reward decay are necessary, with reward decay mitigating reliance on assisted correction.

</details>


### [179] [SinkRec: Mitigating Semantic State Sink in Long Sequence Recommendation with Memory-Conditioned Gated Delta Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09888)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Ji Dai,Jie Chen,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinkRec提出了一种混合记忆-循环过渡架构，通过外部化重复行为模式为可检索记忆，解决了线性注意力中长期推荐中语义状态沉没的问题，在保持线性效率的同时提升了推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力虽能避免标准Transformer的二次复杂度，但其压缩的循环状态容易被重复行为模式主导，形成语义状态沉没（semantic state sink）现象，即重复语义过度占据循环状态并偏置后续读取，影响推荐准确性。

Method: 提出SinkRec混合记忆-循环过渡架构，包含两个核心组件：1）通过残差向量量化将重复局部模式外部化为可学习条件记忆；2）提出时序感知状态关系差分门控DeltaNet（TDGD），利用记忆净化循环读写操作，抑制被记忆覆盖的更新、移除与记忆对齐的读取响应。

Result: 在公开数据集和工业数据集上的实验验证了SinkRec的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: SinkRec通过将重复语义从状态竞争信号转变为记忆可检索模式，使循环状态专注于动态过渡建模，从而缓解语义状态沉没问题，同时保持线性时间复杂度。

Abstract: Linear attention provides an efficient backbone for long-sequence recommendation by avoiding the quadratic cost of standard Transformers, but its compressed recurrent state can be dominated by repetitive behavior patterns. We identify this phenomenon as semantic state sink, where recurring semantics over-occupy the recurrent state and bias subsequent readouts. To mitigate semantic state sink, we propose SinkRec, a hybrid memory-transition looped architecture that decouples collaborative behavioral pattern storage from dynamic transition modeling. SinkRec externalizes recurring local patterns into a learnable conditional memory through residual vector quantization, reinjects the retrieved codes, and exposes memory key-value pairs to the attention block. It further introduces Temporal-Aware State-Relation Differential Gated DeltaNet (TDGD), which uses memory to purify recurrent writing and reading by suppressing memory-covered updates and removing memory-aligned readout responses. This design turns recurring semantics from state-competing signals into memory-retrievable patterns, allowing the recurrent state to focus on dynamic transitions and alleviating semantic state sink with linear-time efficiency. Experiments on public and industrial datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SinkRec.

</details>


### [180] [Optuna Constrained Tree-Structured Parzen Estimator Is a Joint Density Generalization of c-TPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09889)
*Shuhei Watanabe,Kaichi Irie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Optuna中使用的约束TPE方法本质上是一种联合c-TPE（使用联合似然的ECI采集函数），与独立c-TPE相比，联合c-TPE对约束重复具有不变性，而独立c-TPE会因重复约束因子的累积而性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束超参数优化（HPO）在实践中很常见，但Optuna广泛使用的约束TPE缺乏算法分析，特别是其与理论上提出的c-TPE（基于期望约束改进ECI）之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 理论分析表明Optuna的约束TPE本质上是联合c-TPE——使用联合似然的ECI采集函数。通过对比分析联合c-TPE与独立c-TPE在约束重复场景下的行为差异。

Result: 联合c-TPE对约束重复具有不变性，而独立c-TPE随着重复约束因子的累积会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了两种c-TPE公式之间的实际权衡，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Constrained hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is common in practice, yet Optuna's widely used constrained TPE lacks algorithmic analysis. While c-TPE proposes an expected constrained improvement (ECI) approach assuming independence between the objective and constraints, Optuna uses a single joint density over both. We show that Optuna's constrained TPE is joint c-TPE -- the same ECI acquisition function using a joint likelihood. We demonstrate joint c-TPE is invariant to constraint duplication whereas independent c-TPE degrades as the product accumulates duplicated factors. We outline practical tradeoffs between the formulations and directions for future study.

</details>


### [181] [PreAct-Bench: Benchmarking Predictive Monitoring in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09890)
*Hainiu Xu,Italo Luis da Silva,Jiangnan Ye,Yuhao Wang,Wei Liu,Linyi Yang,Jonathan Richard Schwarz,Nicola Paoletti,Yulan He,Hanqi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性监控（Predictive Monitoring）任务：仅基于部分动作轨迹预判LLM智能体是否会执行不道德行为，并构建了PreActBench基准（1000条轨迹，5个领域），实验表明该任务对现有模型仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究仅能在完整轨迹事后检测不道德行为，无法在危害发生前进行预警，缺乏前瞻性风险推理能力。

Method: 定义Predictive Monitoring任务；构建PreActBench基准（1000条配对的伦理/非伦理动作轨迹，覆盖5个领域）；提出Prefix Foresight F1评估指标；评测多种LLM、安全护栏模型及潜在探针方法在不同轨迹前缀比例下的表现。

Result: 人类在此任务上表现较好，但即使强模型也面临挑战，预测性监控仍是一个开放难题。

Conclusion: LLM安全需要面向未来的风险推理能力，当前模型在该前瞻性安全任务上存在明显不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents capable of executing multi-step action trajectories toward a given objective. While existing safety research has focused on detecting unethical behavior from complete trajectories, this paradigm is fundamentally retrospective: it identifies harm only after it has already occurred. In this work, we study a critical yet overlooked safety task, which we term Predictive Monitoring: given only a partial action trajectory, can a model infer whether it will culminate in an unethical action before the overt action is executed? To support this task, we present PreActBench, a benchmark of 1,000 paired ethical and unethical action trajectories spanning five domains. We evaluate a range of LLMs, safety guardrail models, and latent probing methods across varying fractions of the action trajectory using our Prefix Foresight F1 metric. Results show that while humans achieve promising performance, predictive monitoring remains challenging even for strong models, highlighting the need for future-oriented risk reasoning in LLM safety.

</details>


### [182] [Representation Curriculum: Stagewise Training for Robust Ranking and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09891)
*Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Sina Baharlouei,Abraham Bagherjeiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表示课程学习（Representation Curriculum, RC）方法，通过在训练时间上分阶段引入特征（先内容信号，后历史信念信号），缓解排序系统对曝光混杂信号的过度依赖，改善冷启动泛化与分布偏移鲁棒性，在理论推导与大规模实验中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数字市场排序系统过度依赖曝光混杂信号（如流行度、CTR/CVR聚合、ID表征），这些信号虽在稳态需求下预测性强，但导致模型忽视内容本身的竞争力与语义亲和性等独立于曝光的信号，造成对在位者的固化、冷启动泛化差及分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出表示课程学习（RC），在训练时按时间阶段组织特征使用：初期突出基于内容的信号，后期引入依赖曝光的信念信号，同时将内容路径锚定在已学习的表示附近，抑制对历史信号的捷径依赖并缓解内容信号的梯度饥饿问题。RC与任务和假设类无关，并提供排序场景的具体实例化。在线性高斯脊回归设定下给出了解析解与充分条件。

Result: 在公开学习排序与推荐基准上，以及大型电商搜索系统的随机在线实验中，RC显著地将模型依赖从历史信念信号转向基于内容的信号，在冷启动群体上取得一致增益，头部性能仅有可控的权衡损失。

Conclusion: RC是一种有效的训练时干预手段，通过课程式特征引入策略缓解排序系统中的暴露偏差与冷启动问题，理论分析与实验共同验证了其有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Ranking in digital marketplaces is a dynamic exposure-allocation mechanism: displayed items shape discovery trajectories and success events logged by the platform to update future allocation policies. Modern ranking systems rely heavily on exposure-confounded signals (e.g. popularity estimates, CTR/CVR aggregates, and ID-based representation), because they are highly predictive under stationary demand. Yet this predictive power can become a learning shortcut: early access to exposure-dependent belief signals steers optimization toward over-reliance on them and away from exposure-independent merit signals (e.g., content-based competitiveness and semantic affinity). Consequently, the learned policy tends to entrench incumbents and degrade cold-start generalization and robustness under distribution shift. We propose Representation Curriculum (RC), a training-time intervention that temporally stages feature utilization. RC foregrounds content-based merit signals initially, then introduces exposure-dependent belief signals while anchoring the content pathway near the learned merit representation, curbing shortcut reliance on historical signals and mitigating gradient starvation on content signals. We formalize RC independently of task and hypothesis class and provide ranking-specific instantiations. In a Gaussian linear ridge setting, we derive closed-form solutions and sufficient conditions under which RC strictly reduces population risk on a cold-start target distribution, with a quantified Pareto tradeoff against source performance. Experiments on public learning-to-rank and recommendation benchmarks, and randomized online experiments in a large-scale e-commerce search system, show that RC measurably shifts reliance from historical belief signals toward content-based merit signals and yields consistent gains on cold populations with a controlled trade-off in head performance.

</details>


### [183] [LMT: A Bayesian Framework for Causal Discovery from Textual Alarm Records in Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09892)
*Xiaofeng Xiao,Jianhong Chen,Qiuzhuang Sun,Naichen Shi,Xubo Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LMT框架，结合大语言模型从文本描述中提取的语义因果信号与时间戳的统计证据，通过贝叶斯因果发现方法构建工程事件数据的因果图，在小样本报警事件场景中表现尤其优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程系统中的文本事件记录（如报警日志）包含因果关系的语义线索，但仅依赖LLM提取的语义信息不足以准确发现因果关系，因为语义模式可能混淆因果与相关或频繁序列模式。因此需要一种结合文本描述和时间戳信息的方法来提升因果发现的准确性。

Method: 提出LMT贝叶斯因果发现框架：首先利用LLM从事件描述中提取语义因果信号，构建事件类型/簇间因果图的先验分布；然后通过基于泊松过程的对数似然融合时间戳证据，用时间戳统计证据精炼LLM信息先验，最终生成既具可解释性又有数据支持的因果图。

Result: 仿真研究表明LMT框架在不同设置下均有效，尤其在小样本报警事件场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: LMT通过融合文本语义和时间戳证据的贝叶斯框架，为工程事件数据提供了有效的因果发现方法，兼具可解释性和数据支持。

Abstract: Textual event records, such as alarm logs, have become an increasingly common data source in engineering and manufacturing systems. Beyond identifying correlations or recurring patterns, engineers are often interested in understanding which types of events causally trigger or influence other events during system operation. Textual event descriptions may contain semantic clues about such causal relationships, and recent large language models (LLMs) provide a promising tool for extracting these signals. However, relying solely on LLM-encoded textual information is insufficient for accurate causal discovery, since semantic patterns do not directly reveal causal mechanisms and may confuse causation with correlation or frequent sequential patterns. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{LMT}, a Bayesian causal discovery framework for engineering event data that jointly leverages textual descriptions and timestamps. Specifically, LMT first uses LLMs to extract semantic causal signals from event descriptions and constructs a prior distribution over causal graphs among event types or event clusters. It then incorporates temporal evidence through a Poisson-process-based likelihood, allowing the LLM-informed prior to be refined by timestamp-based statistical evidence. By integrating the textual and temporal information, LMT produces a causal graph that is both interpretable and data-supported. Simulation studies show that the proposed framework is effective across different settings and is especially advantageous in small-sample alarm-event scenarios.

</details>


### [184] [A Navigable Manifold of Hypothesized Consciousness-Spectrum States in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09894)
*Sophie Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer嵌入空间中是否存在与人类意识谱系（从低层次自我聚焦到高层次整合连贯）对齐的结构化几何结构。实验发现嵌入空间呈现全局组织化的流形结构：高低层次区域具有凸性稳定性，中间区域形成过渡走廊；无论是基于效用引导还是纯几何的贪心轨迹，都一致地从低层次区域穿越到高层次区域，表明导航性是表示空间的内在属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型是否在表示空间中编码了与人类意识谱系（从反应性、自我聚焦到整合性、连贯性）对齐的结构化、可解释的谱系，对于模型引导、评估和对齐至关重要。

Method: 在Transformer嵌入空间中，沿假设的意识谱系（受冥想传统、哲学和现代心理学启发）研究几何结构和动态模式。通过分析句子嵌入的聚类、区域凸性和贪心轨迹（包括效用引导和纯几何两种方式）来揭示空间组织。

Result: 嵌入空间呈现全局组织化的几何结构：相似状态的句子聚类为局部连贯区域，形成结构化流形；高/低层次区域具有类似凸性的稳定性，中间区域为过渡走廊；两种贪心轨迹均一致地从低层次区域穿越到高层次区域，表明导航性为表示空间的内在属性。

Conclusion: Transformer嵌入空间编码了与意识谱系分类对齐的结构化、可导航的几何结构，该分类受人类意识的结构性描述启发，为分析和引导模型行为提供了表示层面的视角。

Abstract: Across contemplative, philosophical, and psychological accounts, human consciousness is often described along a similar spectrum, ranging from reactive and self-focused patterns to more integrative and coherent ones. Understanding whether language models encode such a structured, human-interpretable consciousness spectrum in representation space is important for model guidance, evaluation and alignment. In this work, we study the geometric structure and dynamics of patterns along this spectrum in transformer embedding spaces. We show that embeddings exhibit a globally organized geometry aligned with this spectrum: sentences associated with similar states cluster into locally coherent regions, forming a structured manifold. In particular, higher-level and lower-level regions exhibit convexity-like stability, while intermediate regions form a transition corridor. Dynamically, both utility-guided and geometry-only greedy trajectories consistently traverse from lower- to higher-level regions, passing through intermediate tiers, indicating that navigability is an intrinsic property of the representation space, guided but not dictated by a global directional signal. These results suggest that embedding spaces encode structured and navigable geometry aligned with a hypothesized consciousness-spectrum taxonomy, broadly inspired by recurring structural descriptions of human consciousness across contemplative traditions, philosophy, and modern psychology, providing a representation-level perspective for analyzing and guiding model behavior.

</details>


### [185] [TRAPS: Therapeutic Response Analysis via Pathway-informed Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09898)
*Sujoy Banik,Sayantan Chakraborty,Boishakhi Das Toma,Zainab Ghafoor,Ushashi Bhattacharjee,Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个统一的基准测试，用于评估三种通路引导的深度学习模型（BINN、GraphPath、PATH）在癌症治疗规划中的表现。在五种癌症队列、2622名患者中，模型同时在靶向分子治疗、放射治疗和六个月生存预测三个任务上训练和评估。结果显示：没有单一架构在所有任务上占优——PATH在靶向分子治疗预测上最佳，BINN在生存预测上最可靠，所有模型在放射治疗预测上均无效。值得注意的是，GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向分子治疗预测中达到了0.92的AUROC，尽管正样本仅占11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症治疗规划需要在多个临床维度上同时决策（如靶向分子治疗、放射治疗、生存期预测）。现有通路引导的深度学习模型各自独立开发和测试，缺乏统一的基准，导致不同架构之间无法进行公平比较。

Method: 构建了首个统一的基准测试，使用Reactome通路活性评分编码的2622名患者（来自TCGA的五个癌症队列），在相同的数据与评估条件下，对三种生物学启发的架构（BINN、GraphPath、PATH）进行联合训练，同时预测三个临床结局（靶向分子治疗、放射治疗、六个月生存概率）。

Result: PATH在靶向分子治疗预测上整体最佳；BINN在生存预测上最可靠；所有模型在放射治疗预测上均无效（其关键驱动因素为基因表达数据未捕获的临床变量）。GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向分子治疗预测中达到0.92的AUROC，是全部基准中的最高分，尽管正样本仅占11%。

Conclusion: 没有单一架构在所有任务上占优；通路结构的选择需匹配特定临床任务与癌症队列的特性；侧向共调控结构（GraphPath）在具有窄靶向驱动程序的队列中展现出卓越的判别能力；放射治疗决策的关键变量不在基因表达数据中，提示需要整合多模态临床特征。

Abstract: Cancer treatment planning requires decisions across multiple clinical dimensions at once. Clinicians must determine whether a patient should receive targeted molecular therapy, radiation therapy, and whether they are likely to survive beyond six months. Existing pathway-informed deep learning models have been developed and tested in isolation, making fair comparison across architectures impossible. We present the first unified benchmark for pathway-guided therapy response modeling, evaluating three biologically informed architectures, BINN, GraphPath, and PATH, across five cancer cohorts drawn from The Cancer Genome Atlas, representing 2,622 patients encoded using Reactome pathway activity scores. Each model is trained jointly on all three clinical outcomes under identical data and evaluation conditions, the first study to treat pathway-structured deep learning as a combined therapy and survival prediction problem. Our results show that no single architecture wins across all tasks: PATH performs best for targeted molecular therapy prediction overall, BINN is most reliable for survival prediction, and no model produces useful predictions for radiation therapy, as the key drivers of that decision are clinical variables not captured in gene expression data. Most strikingly, GraphPath achieves an AUROC of 0.92 on prostate targeted molecular therapy prediction, the highest score in the entire benchmark, demonstrating that lateral co-regulation structure produces exceptional discriminative power when matched to a cohort with a narrow targetable driver programme, even under conditions of extreme class imbalance at only 11\% positive prevalence.

</details>


### [186] [When Attribution Patching Lies: Diagnosis and a Second-Order Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09899)
*Luyang Zhang,Jialu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了归因修补（Attribution Patching）作为激活修补（Activation Patching）的一阶近似方法在机制可解释性中的不可靠性来源，指出主要误差来自下游网络的非线性而非局部曲率。基于此，提出了三个实用工具：可靠性分数、误差界和HVP修正项。实验表明HVP修正在大规模模型中可行且优于基线方法，能够提升电路恢复的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性的核心目标是识别语言模型行为的关键内部组件，而激活修补作为因果度量的黄金标准计算成本过高。实践中广泛使用的归因修补（一阶梯度近似）的可靠性尚未得到充分理解，需要系统分析其误差来源并提供改进方案。

Method: 通过理论分析证明归因修补的误差主要来自下游网络的非线性（而非局部曲率）；基于此提出：(i) 可靠性分数检测不可信估计，(ii) 误差界量化潜在误标，(iii) HVP向量积修正消除主导误差（仅需额外一次反向传播）。在五个模型族（124M-9B参数）上进行了随机token和自然扰动（name-swap）评估。

Result: HVP是唯一在大规模模型中可行的二阶修正方法，而标准基线如积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）计算代价过高；多步HVP变体在更低计算成本下匹配或超越积分梯度的准确率，优于先前的二阶基线方法；这些改进在标准基准上实现了更高保真度的电路恢复。

Conclusion: 归因修补的不可靠性主要源于下游非线性；提出的HVP修正以极低额外计算成本显著提升估计精度；支持Screen-Flag-Fix工作流，将计算资源仅集中于被标记为不可靠的组件上，实现高效的电路识别。

Abstract: A central goal of mechanistic interpretability is to identify which internal components causally drive a language model's behavior. Because these importance estimates serve as the evidence for identifying circuits, systematic errors can lead to the misidentification of the underlying mechanisms. While activation patching provides a gold-standard causal metric, its computational cost is prohibitive at scale. Practitioners instead rely on attribution patching, a gradient-based, first-order approximation whose reliability remains poorly understood. In this work, we characterize the source of this unreliability, demonstrating that the dominant error stems from the non-linearities in the downstream network rather than local curvature at the patched component. This insight yields three practical tools: (i) a reliability score to detect untrustworthy estimates, (ii) error bounds quantifying potential attribution mis-specifications, and (iii) a Hessian-vector-product (HVP) correction that eliminates the leading-order error with only one additional backward pass. In evaluations across five model families (124M-9B parameters) and both random-token and naturalistic (name-swap) perturbations, HVP is the only second-order correction feasible at larger scale, where standard baselines like Integrated Gradients become computationally prohibitive. In comparative experiments, a multi-step HVP variant matches or exceeds the accuracy of Integrated Gradients at significantly lower compute, outperforming prior second-order baselines. These improvements lead to higher-fidelity circuit recovery on standard benchmarks and support a Screen-Flag-Fix workflow that targets computational effort only toward the components flagged as unreliable.

</details>


### [187] [LongMoE: Longitudinal Multimodal Learning via Trajectory-Aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09907)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Prakhar Kumar,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LongMoE框架，统一解决临床多模态学习中的模态缺失和纵向动态两个挑战，通过上下文感知插补、注意力标记化、轨迹感知编码器和稀疏MoE路由，在ADNI、OASIS-3和MIMIC-IV上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理模态缺失（将每次就诊视为独立快照，丢弃时间上下文）和纵向动态（假设模态完整，在系统性缺失下性能下降），缺乏统一框架同时应对这两个临床学习中的根本性挑战。

Method: LongMoE包含四个核心模块：1) 上下文感知插补模块处理模态缺失；2) 注意力标记化模块捕捉不规则就诊序列上的频域时序模式；3) 轨迹感知编码器建模疾病进展；4) 上下文条件的稀疏MoE路由实现患者特异的专家选择。

Result: 在ADNI、OASIS-3和MIMIC-IV三个数据集上，LongMoE在模态缺失或弱模态条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性，且在完整模态设置下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: LongMoE为纵向感知的多模态临床学习建立了坚实基础，证明了统一处理模态缺失和纵向动态的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical learning is increasingly important for integrating diverse patient data, including imaging, text, and personalised health records. However, it faces two fundamental challenges: i) modality missingness, where arbitrary subsets of modalities are unavailable at a given patient visit, ii) longitudinal dynamics, where the diagnostic significance of an observation depends on the patient's evolving disease trajectory over time. Existing methods address these challenges in isolation: missing-modality frameworks treat each visit as an independent static snapshot and discard temporal context, while longitudinal models often assume complete modality availability and degrade under systematic modality incompleteness. We propose LongMoE (Longitudinal Mixture-of-Experts), the unified framework to jointly address both challenges. LongMoE combines a context-aware imputation module with an attentional tokenization module that captures frequency-domain temporal patterns across irregular visit sequences, a trajectory-aware encoder for modeling disease progression, and context-conditioned Sparse MoE routing for patient-specific expert selection. Experiments on ADNI, OASIS-3, and MIMIC-IV show that LongMoE improves robustness under missing or weak contemporaneous modalities and remains competitive in full-modality settings, establishing a strong foundation for longitudinally-aware multimodal clinical learning.

</details>


### [188] [Mix, Don't Pick: Why Synthetic Corpus Composition Matters for Time Series Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09912)
*Aaryan Nagpal,Debdeep Sanyal,Murari Mandal,Dhruv Kumar,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在时间序列基础模型的预训练中，合成数据生成器的选择至关重要：不同生成器在相同预算下可导致高达2倍的预测误差差异，且生成器排名在不同架构间不稳定。本文绕过选择问题，提出所有生成器的等权重混合能匹配或超越最佳个体生成器，与真实数据组合后效果最优。因此，合成预训练本质上是语料组合问题而非生成器选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型预训练中选择合成数据生成器时缺乏原则性方法，错误选择会导致显著性能损失（最佳与最差生成器间存在2倍预测误差差距），且生成器的有效性因模型架构而异，无法直接迁移。

Method: 在Chronos-T5-Mini和Moirai-Small两种架构上，对11个生成器家族进行从头训练实验，对比个体生成器与等权重混合策略的性能，并评估混合生成器与真实数据组合的效果。

Result: 生成器排名在不同架构间不稳定；简单的等权重混合策略能匹配或超越最佳个体生成器的表现；将混合生成器与真实数据组合可得到最强的预训练语料。

Conclusion: 合成预训练应被视为语料组合问题而非生成器选择问题，组合策略需针对每个模型家族进行验证，不能假设可跨架构迁移。

Abstract: Choosing the wrong synthetic generator for time-series foundation model pretraining is costly: under identical training budgets, the best and worst generators produce up to a $2\times$ gap in forecasting error, yet the field has no principled way to make this choice. The problem is compounded by the fact that generator rankings are not stable across architectures: across 11 generator families evaluated on Chronos-T5-Mini and Moirai-Small trained from scratch, we find that which generators are useful depends on the model architecture. Rather than solving the generator selection problem, we sidestep it: a simple equal-weight mixture of all generators matches or beats the best individual generator for both architectures, and composing this mixture with real data yields the strongest pretraining corpora overall.
