Synthetic pretraining is therefore a corpus composition problem, not a generator selection problem, and composition choices should be validated per model family rather than assumed to transfer.

</details>


### [189] [IntentKV: Cross-Turn Intent-Aware KV Cache Pruning for Agent Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09916)
*Junjie Li,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntentKV 是一种面向多轮 LLM Agent 场景的 KV 缓存剪枝方法，通过跨轮次意图记忆和零初始化残差头实现精准剪枝，在保持模型精度几乎不变的前提下大幅降低 KV 缓存峰值和读取带宽（例如在 Qwen2.5-14B 上峰值请求 Token 减少 77.8%，原始 KV 读取减少 92.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮 LLM Agent 中，短查询演变为长轨迹，导致 KV 缓存（而非参数计算）成为主要服务瓶颈：KV 内存和读取带宽在单条轨迹内增长数个数量级，亟需有效的 KV 剪枝方法。

Method: 保持基础 LLM 冻结不变；维护跨轮次意图的会话级 QueryMemory，通过记忆-注意力规则对实时历史令牌打分；添加零初始化的残差头（含对当前查询 K 向量的交叉注意力）；剪枝采用槽位映射重定向（slot-map redirection），丢弃位置路由到哨兵死槽位，保留的 K/V 行、RoPE 相位和槽位身份保持不变，兼容前缀缓存。

Result: 在 8k KV 预算下，Qwen3-8B 平均峰值请求 Token 下降 23.9%，Qwen2.5-14B 下降 30.7%。在 BCP 最长 100 条查询上，IntentKV-8k 将最坏情况峰值请求 Token 从 92.3k 降至 20.5k（-77.8%），最坏情况原始 KV 读取从 411M 降至 31M（-92.6%）。

Conclusion: IntentKV 在极紧的 KV 预算下几乎无损匹配全缓存基线，有效解决了多轮 Agent 中 KV 缓存成为服务瓶颈的问题。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents fan short queries into long trajectories of tool calls, search results, and intermediate reasoning. Both KV memory and KV read bandwidth grow by orders of magnitude across a single trajectory, making the key-value (KV) cache, not parameter compute, the dominant serving bottleneck for long-horizon agents. We introduce IntentKV, learned KV pruning that keeps the base LLM frozen. IntentKV maintains a session-level QueryMemory of cross-turn intent, scores live history tokens with a memory-attention rule, and adds a zero-initialized residual head with cross-attention over current-query K-vectors. To stay composable with prefix caches, eviction is a slot-map redirection: dropped positions route to a sentinel dead slot while surviving K/V rows, RoPE phases, and slot identities stay in place. IntentKV matches the no-pruning full-cache baseline with almost no accuracy drop under tight KV budgets: at an 8k KV budget, mean peak request tokens drop 23.9% on Qwen3-8B and 30.7% on Qwen2.5-14B. On the 100 longest BCP queries that all methods complete on Qwen2.5-14B, IntentKV-8k further cuts worst-case peak request tokens from 92.3k to 20.5k, a 77.8% reduction, and worst-case raw KV reads from 411M to 31M, a 92.6% reduction.

</details>


### [190] [SPDM: Geometry-Modulated State Space Modeling with Manifold Constraints for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09917)
*Xingsheng Chen,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SPDM，一种几何感知的状态空间模型，通过在对称正定流形上建模跨变量相关结构作为连续轨迹，利用黎曼几何特征约束状态空间模型的选择性扫描动态，在保持Mamba并行扫描线性时间复杂度的同时，在11个真实世界基准数据集上实现了最先进的多元时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型在处理多元时间序列时通过扫描标记化的时间或空间序列，忽略了变量间不断演化的几何关联结构，导致无法有效捕捉持续变化的跨变量相关性。

Method: SPDM包含两个协同机制：1) 流形轨迹路径：将动态演化的协方差矩阵从SPD流形投影到欧几里得切空间；2) 几何门控机制：基于流形轨迹导出的几何信号直接调节SSM内部的选择性参数。整体参数化保持Mamba并行扫描的线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在11个真实世界基准数据集上取得最先进的预测性能，消融研究证实几何约束的状态空间动态是性能提升的主导架构因素。

Conclusion: 将流形约束引入状态空间建模是一种有效的原则性方法，能够在保持计算效率的同时嵌入丰富的结构约束，为多元时间序列预测提供了新的几何感知架构范式。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting requires capturing the continuously evolving correlation structure among interacting variables. Existing state-space models process time series by scanning tokenized temporal or spatial sequences, discarding the evolutionary geometric structure. We address this limitation by introducing manifold constraints into state-space modeling: treating the cross-variable correlation structure as a continuous trajectory on the symmetric positive definite manifold, whose Riemannian geometric features, tangent space linearity, and Frechet mean centrality act as a principled geometric regularizer that guides and stabilizes the selective scanning dynamics of SSMs. We propose SPDM, a geometry-aware SSM architecture that realizes this principle through two cooperating mechanisms: a manifold trajectory path that projects dynamically evolving covariance matrices from the SPD manifold to a Euclidean tangent space, and a geometric gating scheme that directly modulates SSM's internal selective parameters based on geometric signals derived from the manifold trajectory. The parameterization preserves the linear-time complexity of the Mamba parallel scan while embedding rich structural constraints, making the architecture preserve prediction accuracy and computational efficiency simultaneously. Extensive experiments on eleven real-world benchmark datasets establish state-of-the-art forecasting performance, and further studies confirm that geometrically constrained state-space dynamics are the dominant architectural factor behind its performance gains.

</details>


### [191] [Co-GLANCE: Uncertainty-Aware Active Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09919)
*Michal P. Podolinsky,Neel P. Bhatt,Pranay Samineni,Rohan Siva,Christian Ellis,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Co-GLANCE是一个实时感知与决策系统，通过将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力蒸馏为端到端模型，结合共形预测与选择性放弃机制，为异构机器人团队提供具有统计保证的不确定性量化，在遮挡分割和机器人分配任务上分别比基于云的VLM基线提升25%和36%，同时推理延迟降低350倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队在非结构化户外环境中面临感知不确定性的核心挑战，不同视角下的遮挡等问题表现各异，而现有视觉语言模型虽具备语义推理能力但计算开销大、缺乏校准的不确定性量化，无法用于机载实时推理。

Method: 将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力蒸馏为端到端遮挡分割与机器人分配模型；结合共形预测与选择性放弃机制，提供分割、分配和检测输出的统计有效覆盖保证；利用校准后的不确定性估计触发主动感知，调度最优机器人获取信息视角。

Result: 在真实场景中，遮挡分割准确率提升25%，机器人分配准确率提升36%，每帧推理延迟降低350倍，并发布了空地数据集。

Conclusion: Co-GLANCE通过模型蒸馏和统计校准的不确定性量化，实现了异构机器人团队的实时机载感知与不确定性消解，在性能上超越基于云的VLM基线，同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Perceptual uncertainty is a central challenge for heterogeneous robot teams operating in unstructured outdoor environments, where no single viewpoint affords reliable scene understanding. Perceptual uncertainty, arising from sources such as occlusions, manifests differently across robot viewpoints depending on scene structure. Detecting and resolving sources of perceptual uncertainty requires both scene-based contextual reasoning and capability-aware robot allocation. While vision-language models provide strong semantic priors for both, they are computationally prohibitive for onboard inference and lack calibrated uncertainty quantification. We introduce Co-GLANCE, a real-time onboard perception and decision-making system for uncertainty resolution in heterogeneous robot teams. Co-GLANCE distills the semantic reasoning capabilities of a vision-language model into an end-to-end model for occlusion segmentation and robot allocation, eliminating the need for cloud-based inference. To quantify perceptual uncertainty, Co-GLANCE combines conformal prediction with selective abstention to provide statistically valid coverage guarantees for segmentation, robot allocation, and detection outputs. These calibrated uncertainty estimates directly trigger active perception, dispatching the most appropriate robot to acquire informative viewpoints and resolve uncertainty. Across real-world scenarios, Co-GLANCE outperforms cloud-based vision-language model baselines in occlusion segmentation and robot allocation accuracy by 25% and 36%, respectively, while reducing per-frame inference latency 350x. We also release an air-ground dataset for future research. Code, videos, and dataset available at https://co-glance.github.io/ .

</details>


### [192] [Conformal Prediction for Neural Operators: Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification in Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09923)
*Michael Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将分裂共形预测应用于神经算子物理仿真，提出了一种归一化共形预测方案，在稳态热传导基准测试中实现了89.1%的经验覆盖率，并提供了不确定性分解框架（认知不确定性占68%，偶然不确定性占32%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在安全关键的工程应用（如电子元件热管理、电池系统）中缺乏具有正式覆盖保证的不确定性量化方法，现有的MC Dropout和Deep Ensembles等方法仅提供相对不确定性估计，没有形式化的覆盖率保证。

Method: 提出了分裂共形预测在神经算子物理仿真中的首次应用，提供无分布假设的预测区间和有限样本覆盖保证；引入归一化共形预测方案，利用MC Dropout不确定性生成自适应宽度区间；在3370万参数、800训练样本、5个集成成员的设置下进行全规模实验。

Result: 在目标水平α=0.1下实现89.1%的经验覆盖率，生成空间自适应的预测区间，认知不确定性占总体不确定性的68%，偶然不确定性占32%。

Conclusion: 该方法为神经算子在安全关键工程应用中的部署提供了严格的不确定性保证，不确定性分解结果可为数据收集和模型改进提供可操作的指导，且方法已实现为开源平台并包含REST API和交互式3D可视化。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) have emerged as powerful surrogates for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), achieving speedups of several orders of magnitude over traditional numerical solvers. However, deploying these models in safety-critical engineering applications -- such as thermal management of electronic components and battery systems -- requires not only accurate point predictions but also rigorous uncertainty guarantees. Existing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods for neural operators, including Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles, provide only relative uncertainty estimates without formal coverage guarantees. In this work, we propose the first application of split conformal prediction to neural operator-based physics simulation, providing distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage guarantees. We further introduce a normalized conformal prediction scheme that leverages MC Dropout uncertainty to produce adaptive-width intervals, yielding tighter intervals in regions of low uncertainty and wider intervals where the model is less certain. Full-scale experiments (33.7M parameters, 800 training samples, 5 ensemble members, NVIDIA V100) on steady-state heat conduction benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves 89.1% empirical coverage at the target level of alpha=0.1, while producing spatially adaptive prediction intervals that reflect the underlying physical uncertainty structure. We also provide an uncertainty decomposition framework that separates epistemic uncertainty (68% of total) from aleatoric uncertainty (32% of total), offering actionable guidance for data collection and model improvement. Our method is implemented in an open-source platform with REST API endpoints and interactive 3D visualization.

</details>


### [193] [Sigma-Branch: Hierarchical Single-Path Network Reconstruction for Dynamic Inference with Reduced Active Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09924)
*Kohga Tanaka,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sigma-Branch (SigmaB) 是一种将预训练稠密网络重构为层次化二叉树的框架，通过激活值驱动的球形k-means聚类分配权重，推理时仅激活单条根到叶路径，在CIFAR-100/ResNet-50、ImageNet-1K/ResNet-50和ModelNet40/PointNet++上实现58-60%的活跃参数减少，且Top-1精度损失不超过1.72个百分点，在可比精度下活跃参数减少量超过FPGM和HRank等静态结构化剪枝方法14-23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的边缘加速器上部署深度神经网络时，每次推理的片外权重传输成为瓶颈而非计算量：稠密网络无法完整存放在片上，每个输入必须加载所有参数。现有模型压缩虽能减少传输但以永久性容量损失为代价。

Method: 将预训练稠密网络重构为层次化二叉树结构，包含共享主干、层次化路由器和专用叶子节点。通过激活值驱动的球形k-means聚类将预训练权重分配到树中，联合初始化路由器权重和每分支通道分配；再通过软路由微调使每个叶子与其路由的输入子集对齐。推理时仅执行单条根到叶路径，减少活跃参数足迹，同时完整稠密参数集仍存储在内存中。

Result: 在CIFAR-100/ResNet-50、ImageNet-1K/ResNet-50和ModelNet40/PointNet++上，SigmaB-Net实现58-60%的每推理活跃参数减少，Top-1精度损失不超过1.72个百分点。在ImageNet-1K可比Top-1精度下，活跃参数减少量超过FPGM和HRank等静态结构化剪枝方法14-23个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨模态评估（2D视觉和3D点云骨干网络）验证了该框架的核心主张：将每推理内存流量与总参数量解耦。SigmaB在保持高精度的同时显著降低推理时的内存传输需求，适用于内存受限的边缘加速场景。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on memory-constrained edge accelerators is bottlenecked by per-inference off-chip weight transfer rather than computation: the dense network cannot be retained on-chip, and every parameter must be loaded for every input. Existing model compression reduces this transfer only at the cost of permanent capacity loss. We propose Sigma-Branch (SigmaB), a framework that restructures a pretrained dense network into a hierarchical binary tree composed of a shared backbone, hierarchical routers, and specialized leaves. Pretrained weights are distributed across the tree via activation-based spherical k-means clustering, which jointly initializes router weights and per-branch channel allocations; soft-routing fine-tuning then aligns each leaf with its routed input subset. At inference, the resulting network executes only a single root-to-leaf path, reducing the active-parameter footprint while storing the complete dense parameter set in memory. Across CIFAR-100 / ResNet-50, ImageNet-1K / ResNet-50, and ModelNet40 / PointNet++, SigmaB-Net reduces per-inference active parameters by 58-60% while remaining within 1.72 percentage points (pp) of the dense baseline Top-1. At comparable ImageNet-1K Top-1, the active-parameter reduction exceeds static structured pruning (FPGM, HRank) by 14-23 pp. The cross-modal evaluation, spanning 2D vision and 3D point-cloud backbones, substantiates a framework-level claim that decouples per-inference memory traffic from the total parameter count.

</details>


### [194] [Sample Where You Struggle: Sharpening Base Model Reasoning via Entropy-Guided Power Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09926)
*Hong Guo,Nianhui Guo,Christoph Meinel,Haojin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Entropy-Guided Power Sampling (EGPS)，一种无需训练和验证器的采样方法，通过利用 token 级熵信息将 MCMC 采样聚焦于高熵决策点，在多个基准上达到最优性能并实现最高 12.6 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Metropolis-Hastings (MH) 在序列级幂分布 $p^α$ 上采样时昂贵且混合慢，原因在于 $p^α$ 与 $p$ 的差异仅集中在稀疏、空间聚集的高熵决策点，而 MH 均匀地沿前缀提议重采样，浪费计算在近退化条件分布上，同时在模式分叉处欠混合。

Method: 提出 EGPS：从前向传播中获取 token 级熵，跳过确定性块，将每个 MCMC 移动定位到高熵邻域，并在决策点应用 Multiple-Try Metropolis，使采样成本按"熵质量"而非序列长度缩放。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 上，EGPS 在 MATH500 (75.8%)、HumanEval (62.2%)、GPQA (42.4%) 三个基准上达到最佳或并列最佳准确率，相较于 MH 基线实现最高 12.6 倍墙钟加速。

Conclusion: EGPS 通过熵引导的感知结构感知采样，有效解决了 MH 在序列级幂分布采样中的计算效率与混合速度问题，实现了无需训练和验证器的高效推理增强。

Abstract: Sampling from the sequence-level power distribution $p^α$ elicits RL-level reasoning from base language models without any parameter updates, but the standard Metropolis--Hastings (MH), a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampler, is both expensive and slow-mixing. We trace both to a structural mismatch: $p^α$ mainly departs from $p$ at a sparse, spatially clustered set of high-entropy decision points, yet MH proposes resampling positions uniformly along the prefix -- wasting compute on near-degenerate conditionals while under-mixing precisely where modes diverge. We propose Entropy-Guided Power Sampling (EGPS), a training-free and verifier-free sampler that re-derives its proposal from token-level entropy already in the forward pass. EGPS skips deterministic blocks, localizes each MCMC move to a high-entropy neighborhood, and applies Multiple-Try Metropolis at decision points -- making sampling cost scale with \emph{entropy mass rather than sequence length}. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B, EGPS reaches best or tied-best accuracy on all three benchmarks (MATH500 $75.8\%$, HumanEval $62.2\%$, GPQA $42.4\%$) at up to a $12.6\times$ wall-clock speedup over the MH baseline.

</details>


### [195] [Trainable Smooth-Rotation Transforms with Learned Channel Scales for LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09927)
*Patrik Czakó,Gábor Kertész,Sándor Szénási*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合分位数鲁棒缩放策略和约束梯度优化的后训练量化方法，通过改进SmoothRot类变换中的激活统计量（用高分位数替代最大值），显著降低了大语言模型激活量化误差。在LLaMA-3.2-1B的W4A4量化下，联合搜索方法将选定层误差降低12%，全层均方误差降低19.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化中激活量化误差大，主要因离群值主导通道导致。本文探究SmoothRot类缩放变换中的过度迁移问题，并提出更鲁棒的缩放策略来缓解量化退化。

Method: 1) 用高分位数替代最大值作为激活统计量，设计分位数鲁棒缩放策略；2) 结合约束梯度优化对通道缩放系数进行轻量级学习；3) 在SmoothRot框架下进行联合(α, q)搜索优化。

Result: LLaMA-3.2-1B W4A4量化：仅分位数策略降低选定层误差11.1%；联合(α, q)搜索降低12%；训练方法降低18.5%。全层均方误差从97.51降至78.08（降幅19.9%）。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的迁移控制和轻量级缩放学习在保持等效变换框架的前提下，能够持续优于基于最大值的固定策略，为激活量化提供了一致且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is one of the most practical ways to reduce the serving cost of Large Language Models (LLMs), but activation quantization remains difficult because outlier-dominated channels lead to large quantization errors. This paper investigates whether part of this degradation is caused by over-migration in scaling-based equivalent transformations. We introduce a quantile-robust scaling policy for SmoothRot-style transforms by replacing max-based activation statistics with high quantiles, and we complement it with constrained gradient-based optimization of channel scales. On LLaMA-3.2-1B under W4A4 quantization, quantile-only policy search improves selected-layer error by 11.1% over the SmoothRot baseline, joint (alpha, q) search improves it by 12%, and training reaches 18.5%. Replaying the best selected-layer policy on all decoder-block down-projection layers reduces the corresponding full-layer mean error from 97.51 to 78.08 (19.9%). The results show that robust migration control and lightweight scale learning provide consistent gains over max-based fixed policies while preserving the equivalent-transform framework.

</details>


### [196] [Forward-Only Convolutional Neural Networks with Learnable Channel-Class Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09928)
*Mohammadnavid Ghader,Saeed Reza Kheradpisheh,Bahar Farahani,Mahmood Fazlali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可学习通道-类别分配机制和损失感知层贡献策略，改进了前向-前向算法在CNN中的性能，在多个数据集上达到FF方法的新SOTA，缩小了与反向传播的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向-前向算法在CNN中采用静态通道-类别划分，难以应对复杂任务，需要更灵活的自适应机制提升前向学习的表征能力。

Method: 1) 可学习通道-类别分配机制（含熵正则化和正交化正则化）实现数据驱动的自适应通道特化；2) 损失感知层贡献策略，根据验证性能自适应加权中间层预测。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet上持续优于现有前向方法，达到FF模型新SOTA，显著缩小与反向传播的差距。

Conclusion: 引入可学习通道特化和层贡献加权能显著增强前向学习在深度CNN中的表征能力。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a biologically inspired alternative to backpropagation by replacing gradient-based credit assignment with local, forward-only objectives. While recent extensions have adapted FF to convolutional neural networks (CNNs), existing formulations rely on static channel-class partitions and struggle to perform effectively in complex tasks. In this work, we introduce a learnable channel-class assignment mechanism that enables adaptive, data-driven specialization of convolutional channels, supported by entropy and orthogonality regularization to promote learning performance. We further propose a loss-aware layer contribution strategy that adaptively weights intermediate-layer predictions based on their validation performance, enhancing the effectiveness of forward-only inference. Integrated into residual CNNs, the proposed method achieves consistently superior performance across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet compared to existing similar forward-only methods. Notably, it establishes new state-of-the-art performance among FF-based models, substantially narrowing the gap with backpropagation. These findings demonstrate that introducing learnable channel specialization and layer contribution weighting significantly enhances the representational capacity of forward-only learning in deep CNNs.

</details>


### [197] [Between Amnesia and Chaos: A Memory Stability Expressivity Trilemma for Trainable Dissipative Oscillator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09929)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了物理储层计算中训练衬底（而非仅训练读出层）的一个三难困境：记忆跨度、梯度稳定性和动力学表达能力无法同时最大化，三者均受阻尼控制。通过二十个非线性振子网络的实验，验证了理论边界的存在，并发现可训练衬底仅在短记忆跨度下优于固定衬底，在临界点附近优势消失甚至逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理储层计算假定衬底不可训练（仅训练线性读出层），本文质疑这一前提，探究端到端训练非线性振子网络衬底的可行性及其根本限制。

Method: 采用辛积分器对非线性振子网络的质量、阻尼和刚度进行端到端学习；从理论上推导阻尼与记忆跨度、梯度稳定性、李雅普诺夫指数之间的关系，提出三难困境；在二十个振子的网络上通过阻尼扫描、计算量匹配的延迟召回任务进行实验验证。

Result: 阻尼单调降低最大李雅普诺夫指数并在明确稳定性下限处过零；可训练衬底在短记忆跨度下显著优于固定衬底，但在约十一步的跨度处优势消失并反转；训练后的模型自发靠近稳定性下限（混沌边缘）；理论边界与经验交叉点存在约五倍的差距，表明可检测梯度与可学习梯度之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 证实了训练物理衬底何时优于固定衬底的理论边界，为物理储层计算中是否以及何时训练衬底提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing harnesses nonlinear mechanical dynamics but, by convention, freezes the substrate and trains only a linear readout, presuming the substrate is not usefully trainable. We revisit that premise for networks of nonlinear oscillators whose mass, damping, and stiffness are learned end-to-end through a symplectic integrator. Our central result is a trilemma: memory horizon, gradient stability, and dynamical expressivity cannot be simultaneously maximized, because all three are governed by the damping. The backward gradient decays at a rate set by the damping, capping how far back credit can propagate, while forward sensitivities grow exponentially in the largest Lyapunov exponent, so usable gradients require damping above a stability floor. Since the Lyapunov exponent falls as damping rises while the memory ceiling falls as the horizon grows, stable training is confined to a band that contracts with horizon and closes at a critical point. We test every step on a twenty-oscillator network. A damping sweep finds the largest Lyapunov exponent monotone and crossing zero at a well-defined stability floor, confirming the theorem's key assumption. A compute-matched comparison of learned versus frozen substrate on delayed recall across nine horizons shows the learned substrate dominating at short horizons and the advantage closing and reversing near a horizon of eleven steps, the predicted signature of band closure; trained models settle near the stability floor, seeking the edge of chaos unprompted. The analytic ceiling overestimates the empirical crossover roughly fivefold, a gap between detectable and learnable gradient that we report rather than tune away. The contribution is a confirmed account of when training a physical substrate beats freezing it.

</details>


### [198] [When RL Fails after SFT: Rejuvenating Model Plasticity for Robust SFT-to-RL Handoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09932)
*Runze Liu,Jiashun Liu,Xu Wan,Yuqian Fu,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了过度SFT会导致模型可塑性丧失，使得后续RL优化困难，并提出了一种名为Rejuvenation的方法（基于基础模型锚定的模型融合与目标神经元重置）来恢复可塑性，同时保留SFT获得的有用先验，在数学推理和智能体任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的SFT+RL训练流程中，过度的SFT会导致模型在后续RL阶段提升有限，作者将这一问题归因于模型可塑性的丧失（即SFT初始化的策略难以被RL有效重塑），因此需要深入理解该现象并寻求解决方案。

Method: 首先从参数变化、输出空间和RL优化动态等多个角度分析了过度SFT导致可塑性丧失的原因（过度自信的token分布和尖锐的参数景观）。然后提出Rejuvenation方法，通过基础模型锚定的模型融合（base-anchored model fusion）减少过度SFT引起的漂移，并结合目标神经元重置（targeted neuron reset）来缓解模型刚性。

Result: 在数学推理任务和智能体任务上，Rejuvenation方法能够持续提升过度训练的SFT模型在后续RL阶段的性能，同时增强对分布外任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 过度SFT会损害模型可塑性，Rejuvenation通过融合基础模型和重置神经元的方式有效恢复了可塑性，为SFT到RL的过渡提供了一种鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) followed by Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become a standard pipeline for Large Language Model (LLM) post-training. SFT is expected to provide a useful behavioral prior for RL to further enhance model capabilities. However, checkpoints with excessive SFT often show limited improvement during RL. We attribute this failure to the loss of model plasticity: the reduced ability of an SFT-initialized policy to be effectively reshaped by subsequent RL. To better understand this phenomenon, we conduct detailed analysis from multiple perspectives, including parameter changes, output spaces, and RL optimization dynamics. Our results show that models from excessive SFT tend to produce over-confident token distributions and exhibit sharp parameter landscapes, which make them harder to optimize in the RL stage. To enable a more robust SFT-to-RL handoff, we propose \texttt{Rejuvenation}, a simple yet effective method that restores plasticity while preserving useful SFT-acquired priors. Rejuvenation leverages base-anchored model fusion to reduce excessive SFT-induced drift with targeted neuron reset to mitigate model rigidity. Experimental results on both math reasoning tasks and agentic tasks demonstrate that our approach consistently improves RL performance on over-trained SFT models, while also enhancing generalization to out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [199] [nCMD: Benign-Anchored Feature Selection for Imbalanced Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09934)
*Abu Fuad Ahmad,Istiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为nCMD（以良性为锚点的类均值偏差）的特征选择方法，针对网络入侵检测系统中高维、极度不平衡的流量数据。该方法通过计算攻击类分布与良性类均值的偏差来评分特征相关性，而非使用全局对称统计量。在四个基准数据集上，nCMD在宏平均F1分数上匹配或超越传统过滤方法，尤其在特征预算紧张和类别严重不平衡时提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统过滤式特征选择方法使用跨类别对称计算的全局统计量对特征排序，无法捕捉入侵检测中攻击流量本质上是偏离主导良性流量这一非对称特性，导致在高维、高度不平衡的网络流量中特征选择效果不佳。

Method: 提出benign-anchored Classwise Mean Deviation (nCMD)，以良性类均值为锚点，计算各攻击类分布相对于该锚点的偏差程度作为特征重要性评分，无需额外计算成本。

Result: 在CICIDS2017、CICDDoS2019、NSL-KDD、UNSW-NB15四个数据集上，使用多种特征预算和三个下游分类器，nCMD在宏平均F1分数上匹配或超越经典过滤基线方法，在三个数据集上取得最佳结果，且在特征预算紧张和类别严重不平衡时改进最显著。

Conclusion: 以良性为锚点的特征排序方法是一种可扩展、可解释的预处理组件，适用于资源受限的网络入侵检测系统。

Abstract: Feature selection is critical for network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) operating under high-dimensional, highly imbalanced traffic, as found in operational and defense networks. Traditional filter methods rank features using global statistics computed symmetrically across classes and thus fail to capture the asymmetry of intrusion detection, where attacks are best characterized as deviations from dominant benign traffic.
  We propose benign-anchored Classwise Mean Deviation (nCMD), a lightweight and interpretable method that scores feature relevance based on the deviation of attack-class distributions from the benign-class mean, rather than a globally biased reference. This approach aligns feature selection with the operational semantics of NIDS at no additional computational cost.
  Across four benchmark datasets (CICIDS2017, CICDDoS2019, NSL-KDD, and UNSW-NB15), multiple feature budgets, and three downstream classifiers, nCMD matches or exceeds classical filter baselines in macro-averaged F1-score. It achieves the best result on three of the four datasets and under every classifier, with the strongest improvements observed under tight feature budgets and severe class imbalance. These results support benign-anchored ranking as a scalable and interpretable preprocessing component for resource-constrained NIDS.

</details>


### [200] [One Lens, Many Worlds : A Capability-Typed Interface for World-Model Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09936)
*Bhavith Chandra Challagundla,Sanskar Pandey,Param Thakkar,Rishikesh Mallagundla,Yugandhar Reddy Gogireddy,Wenhao Lu,Hindol Roy Choudhury,Shravani Challagundla,Mohamed Deraz Nasr,Spursh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldModelLens是一个开源可解释性工具，通过能力类型适配器统一了不同架构的世界模型（如PlaNet、Dreamer、IRIS、I-JEPA），使其共享钩子与缓存接口，避免为每种架构重复实现分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（探针、激活修补、稀疏自编码器、惊讶分析）在不同世界模型架构上被重复实现，因为现有钩子-缓存工具假设的是无动作、无环境步骤的Transformer语言模型。这种碎片化源于工具而非模型本身。

Method: 提出能力类型适配器接口：每个模型实现四个必需方法（encode, transition, initial state, sample）并通过显式能力描述符声明可选头（decode, reward, continue, actor, critic）。统一的钩子与缓存层暴露时间索引的激活、想象展开和干预回放。

Result: 使得强化学习和自监督世界模型在同一接口下成为一等公民，无需互相模仿；每种分析只需编写一次。

Conclusion: 世界模型的共享结构可以通过一个小的类型化接口捕捉，从而消除工具碎片化。

Abstract: World models are now built on substantially different computational substrates. Latent recurrent state-space models such as PlaNet and the Dreamer family compress observations into recurrent states; token-based models such as IRIS quantize observations into a learned codebook and predict autoregressively with a transformer; and joint-embedding predictive architectures such as I-JEPA predict in a learned latent space with no pixel decoder. The interpretability methods applied to these models, including probing, activation patching, sparse autoencoders, and surprise analysis, share a common set of primitives, yet they are re-implemented from scratch for each architecture because existing hook-and-cache tooling assumes a transformer language model with no notion of actions, environment steps, or imagined rollouts. We argue that this fragmentation reflects the tooling rather than the models, and that the shared structure of world models is captured by a small typed interface. We present WorldModelLens, an open-source interpretability substrate organized around a capability-typed adapter: every model implements four required methods (encode, transition, initial state, sample) and declares a set of optional heads (decode, reward, continue, actor, critic) through an explicit capability descriptor, so that reinforcement-learning and self-supervised world models are first-class without either imitating the other. A single hook and cache layer exposes time-indexed activations, imagination rollouts, and intervention replay over this interface, allowing each analysis to be written once.

</details>


### [201] [RKSC: Reasoning-Aware KV Cache Sharing and Confident Early Exit for Multi-Step LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09937)
*Anirudh Sekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RKSC是一种无需微调的推理框架，通过注意力相似性KV共享、置信度门控提前退出和选择性块缓存管理，在多分支LLM推理中实现平均3倍加速，且错误率仅0.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除多分支LLM推理管线中存在的两种结构性冗余：前缀KV缓存冗余和不必要的验证前向传播冗余。

Method: 提出三个组件：ASKS（基于隐藏状态余弦相似度共享前缀KV缓存）、CGEE（置信度门控提前退出，包含跳过验证前向传播和中间层提前终止）、RSBCM（基于注意力加权的深度优先缓存淘汰策略）。

Result: 在5个模型家族（7B-10B）、4个基准、1000个问题上，平均加速3.008x（峰值3.990x），比vLLM等效前缀缓存提升1.66倍，CGEE错误率仅0.37%（1616次验证调用中6次错误）。

Conclusion: RKSC无需微调或架构改动即可显著提升多分支LLM推理效率，且精度损失极小。

Abstract: We introduce RKSC (Reasoning-Aware KV Cache Sharing), a training-free inference framework that eliminates two structural redundancies in multi-branch LLM reasoning pipelines. ASKS (Attention-Similarity KV Sharing) computes the prefix KV cache once and broadcasts it to all semantically similar branches via hidden-state cosine similarity, strictly generalising the token-exact prefix caching used by vLLM and SGLang. CGEE (Confidence-Gated Early Exit) applies two complementary exit mechanisms: (1) it skips the verification forward pass entirely when generation confidence is decisive across branches, and (2) it terminates the verification pass at an intermediate layer when per-layer entropy stabilises, using lightweight hooks on the transformer backbone. RSBCM (Reasoning-Selective Block Cache Manager) prevents unbounded cache growth via attention-weighted depth-priority eviction. Across five model families (7B-10B), four benchmarks, and 1,000 evaluated problems, RKSC achieves a mean speedup of 3.008x over the No-KV baseline (peak 3.990x), a 1.66x mean improvement over vLLM-equivalent prefix caching, with a CGEE-induced error rate of only 0.37% (6 errors out of 1,616 verify calls). No fine-tuning or architecture changes are required. Code is available at https://github.com/AnirudhSekar/RKSC.

</details>


### [202] [Interactions Between Crosscoder Features: A Compact Proofs Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09940)
*Dmitry Manning-Coe,Thomas Read,Anna Soligo,Oliver Clive-Griffin,Chun-Hei Yip,Rajashree Agrawal,Jason Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化证明了字典学习方法（SAE和crosscoder）中特征交互会导致重构误差，并提出了交互度量及其在计算稀疏性、语义聚类和检测休眠代理方面的三个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习方法（如SAE和crosscoder）试图将模型激活分解为独立特征，但特征间的交互会导致重构误差，目前缺乏对这种交互的严格形式化理解和度量。

Method: 通过紧凑证明形式化特征交互误差，推导出MLP层中交互项的显式表达式，并将其作为可微损失惩罚项，实现计算稀疏的crosscoder。

Result: 交互惩罚使crosscoder在每数据点每神经元仅保留单个特征时保留60%的MLP性能（标准方法仅10%）；交互度量聚类产生语义有意义的特征簇；检测到休眠代理具有显著交互。

Conclusion: 特征交互度量不仅是解释交叉编码器重构误差的理论工具，还能有效提升计算稀疏性、发现语义结构并检测异常行为。

Abstract: Dictionary learning methods like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) and crosscoders attempt to explain a model by decomposing its activations into independent features. Interactions between features hence induce errors in the reconstruction. We formalize this intuition via compact proofs and make five contributions. First, we show how, \textit{in principle}, a compact proof of model performance can be constructed using a crosscoder. Second, we show that an error term arising in this proof can naturally be interpreted as a measure of interaction between crosscoder features and provide an explicit expression for the interaction term in the Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) layers. We then provide three applications of this new interaction measure. In our third contribution we show that the interaction term itself can be used as a differentiable loss penalty. Applying this penalty, we can achieve ``computationally sparse'' crosscoders that retain $60\%$ of MLP performance when only keeping a single feature at each datapoint and neuron, compared to $10\%$ in standard crosscoders. We then show that clustering according to our interaction measure provides semantically meaningful feature clusters, and finally that sleeper agents have significant interactions. Code is available at https://github.com/chainik1125/crosscoders-feature-interactions/tree/arxiv.

</details>


### [203] [Learning Where to Simulate: Generative Active Sampling for Online PDE Surrogate Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09949)
*Pierre Cesar,Sofya Dymchenko,Abhishek Purandare,Bruno Raffin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线生成式主动采样（OGAS）方法，通过并行训练扩散模型将代理模型的困难信号映射到配置参数，主动引导数据生成朝向高难度区域，有效提升代理模型在极端动态下的预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的PDE代理模型需要覆盖广泛的PDE配置（如初始条件、物理系数），但均匀采样难以充分代表具有挑战性动态的轨迹，导致高预测误差和大方差。

Method: OGAS：在线训练中，并行训练一个快速扩散模型作为条件采样器，将代理模型派生的困难信号（如损失或不确定性）映射到配置参数；通过从偏向高难度的先验中主动抽取目标信号，持续引导数据生成向困难区域倾斜。

Result: 在2D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、Navier-Stokes、Gray-Scott方程（最多308个参数）及多种代理架构上，OGAS一致显著降低99百分位以上的尾部分布误差和整体误差离散度，且几乎不增加训练时间开销。

Conclusion: OGAS通过主动在线采样有效保证了代理模型在最坏情况下的可靠性，虽以平均误差的轻微增加为代价，但在尾部分布性能上大幅优于均匀采样。

Abstract: Data-driven PDE surrogates are trained with data produced by numerical PDE solvers. However, when the surrogate's goal is to generalize across a wide range of PDE configurations (e.g., initial conditions and physical coefficients), generating a representative training set is non-trivial. Uniform sampling of configuration parameters often under-represents trajectories exhibiting challenging dynamics, leading to high prediction errors and large error variance in the trained surrogate. Online training, where data generation and surrogate training are coupled, offers a natural advantage by allowing solver parameters to be steered on-the-fly. To efficiently exploit this capability, we introduce Online Generative Active Sampling (OGAS), an active learning method that reactively learns the relationship between configuration parameters and surrogate performance to control the sampling distribution. OGAS trains a fast diffusion model in parallel to the surrogate to act as a conditional sampler, mapping a surrogate-derived difficulty signal (e.g., loss or uncertainty) to configuration parameters. By actively drawing target signals from a prior biased toward high difficulty, OGAS continuously steers data generation toward challenging regimes without delaying the training workflow. We evaluate OGAS across 2D PDEs with distinct challenging dynamics (Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, Navier-Stokes, Gray-Scott) and up to 308 parameters, using multiple surrogate architectures. Across all settings, OGAS consistently improves tail statistics, yielding substantial reductions in errors above the 99th percentile and overall error dispersion compared to uniform sampling. While prioritizing challenging trajectories introduces a trade-off with average error, OGAS effectively ensures worst-case reliability of trained surrogates with negligible wall-time overhead.

</details>


### [204] [Integrating Out, Twice:The Open-System Case That Neural-Network Ensemble Theory Is Missing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09950)
*Jin Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过核反应理论中的光学模型，证明神经网络参数平均（集成）与高斯边缘化在数学上等价于舒尔补操作，并严格区分了“封闭”（协方差可逆）与“开放”（非厄米有效生成器）两种情形。作者将开放框架应用于注意力图、token级传递算子和稀疏专家路由，但绝大部分结果为阴性——开放框架的有效性受限于学习对象缺乏连续谱与波动动力学，操作上有用的不确定性仍存于封闭对应（认知不确定性）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核反应理论中的光学模型为理解开放系统提供了成熟的数学框架（非厄米有效生成器、广义光学定理），而当前深度学习中对集成、NTK、GP等概念的认知局限于封闭系统视角。作者希望将开放系统的视角引入机器学习，检验其是否能够为模型不确定性建模提供新的工具。

Method: 仅使用分布的矩、高斯代数和分块矩阵求逆（舒尔补），无需场论工具。将神经网络参数平均（ensemble）等同于封闭情形的舒尔补，将核反应光学模型映射为开放情形的非厄米有效生成器。随后将开放框架实证检验于三种结构：截断注意力图、token级传递算子、稀疏专家路由器。

Result: 主要结果为阴性。在三种测试结构上，开放框架的守恒通量账目虽然可记录开放性，但其特有内容要么缺失，要么是所选分块的伪影，要么被训练目标压制在底限附近。操作上有用的不确定性仍是认知不确定性（封闭半区），而非开放半区提供的通量不确定性。

Conclusion: 开放情形要求被消除的扇区具有连续谱和波动（非耗散）动力学，而主流机器学习中的对象是有限维或纯耗散的，不满足该条件。本文的主要价值在于那张定位此阴性结果的地图，而非正面结果本身。

Abstract: Averaging a neural network over its random parameters and marginalizing a Gaussian sector are the same operation, the Schur complement of the eliminated block, and when that block is closed it returns a covariance and its inverse. That is all a network ensemble produces, the closed case. The open case is missing, and nuclear reaction theory has it worked out. Projecting a scattering problem onto a chosen set of channels, with the rest carrying probability irreversibly to a continuum, leaves a non-Hermitian effective generator that conserves and itemizes exactly what it loses: the nuclear optical model and its generalized optical theorem. I set the two cases side by side using only the moments of a distribution, the algebra of Gaussians, and block inversion, no field theory, and give the closed-case dictionary in full: the neural tangent kernel is the Fisher sensitivity kernel, the infinite-width Gaussian limit is the Gaussian-process emulator, and the lazy-to-feature transition is the validity boundary of a reduced-basis emulator. I then test the open export on a truncated attention map, a token-level transfer operator, and a sparse expert router, and report a mostly negative result. The conserved flux ledger ports wherever openness is genuinely present, but its distinctive content is absent, an artifact of the chosen partition, or pinned near a floor by the training objective, and the operationally useful uncertainty turns out to be epistemic, living in the closed half of the correspondence, not the open one. The negative has a structural reason this note makes precise: the open case needs an eliminated sector with a continuous spectrum and wave-like, not relaxational, dynamics, which mainstream learning's finite or dissipative objects do not supply. This is a note, not a result; its main finding is that negative one, and its value is the map that locates it.

</details>


### [205] [Hasse Diagrams for Attention: A Partial Order Framework for Designing Transformer Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09951)
*Chentao Li,Han Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于Transformer注意力掩码信息流结构的完整理论框架，证明了深层Transformer的信息流收敛到Hasse图（有向无环偏序图），并将并行训练任务设计转化为寻找Hasse图最小公共超图的问题，由此推导出两种新注意力掩码：Block Two-Stream Attention和Butterfly Attention。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前虽然有FlexAttention等算子支持任意注意力掩码，但缺乏对任意掩码所诱导的信息流结构的系统性形式化分析，亟需一个理论框架来理解和设计注意力掩码。

Method: 1. 证明多层Transformer在足够深度下信息流收敛到Hasse图（偏序关系的有向无环图）；2. 将并行训练任务设计重述为寻找Hasse图的最小公共超图问题，并建立最小公共超图的判定准则；3. 基于该框架构造性地从任务族推导注意力掩码。

Result: 提出了两种新注意力掩码：Block Two-Stream Attention（块生成注意力掩码，保证训练-推理一致性）和Butterfly Attention（全监督双向注意力掩码），展示了框架发现新结构的能力。

Conclusion: 该理论框架系统化了注意力掩码的设计方法，能够从任务需求出发构造性地导出掩码结构，具有发现新注意力模式的理论指导价值。

Abstract: During the training of large Transformer models, attention masks regulate the scope and direction of information flow across a sequence. Numerous mask variants exist, and operators such as FlexAttention already support arbitrary attention masks. Nevertheless, a systematic formal analysis of the information-flow structure induced by arbitrary masks has been missing. This paper develops a complete theoretical framework. We prove that, with sufficient depth, the information flow of a multi-layer Transformer converges to a Hasse diagram -- a directed acyclic graph representing a partial order. Building on this, we recast the design of parallel training tasks as the problem of finding a minimal common supergraph of Hasse diagrams, and we establish a criterion for the minimal common supergraph. This yields a constructive method to derive attention masks directly from a family of tasks. Applying the framework, we design two novel masks: a block-generation attention mask that ensures training-inference consistency (Block Two-Stream Attention), and a fully supervised bidirectional attention mask (Butterfly Attention). These results demonstrate the framework's capacity to discover new structures.

</details>


### [206] [Does Normalization Choice Matter for Causal Large Time-Series Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09954)
*Samy-Melwan Vilhes,Gilles Gasso,Mokhtar Z Alaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于Transformer的大规模时间序列模型中不同归一化策略的效果，重点关注因果自回归架构下的信息泄漏问题，发现归一化选择对训练收敛和预测性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中非平稳性严重影响模型性能，常用归一化方法在因果自回归设置下会导致未来信息泄漏，现有替代方案（因果归一化、初始观测统计量）的实际影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在基于Transformer的大规模时间序列模型上，采用patching和高效因果策略，系统评估不同归一化策略（包括传统归一化、因果归一化及基于初始观测的归一化）对训练收敛和预测性能的影响。

Result: 归一化选择对训练收敛速度和预测性能均有显著影响，不同策略在不同场景下表现差异明显。

Conclusion: 归一化策略是大规模时间序列模型设计中的关键因素，合理选择可有效缓解因果设置下的信息泄漏问题并提升预测性能。

Abstract: Large models for time-series forecasting have been emerged as a promising paradigm for training models on heterogeneous collections of signals. These models typically rely on causal autoregressive architectures, where each observation is sequentially predicted from past. In practice, real-world time-series exhibit non-stationarities, which significantly influence predictive performance. To mitigate this, normalization is commonly employed. However, in efficient causal settings it might induce information leakage from future observations during training. Recent alternatives, including causal normalization and statistics computed from initial observations, have been proposed to address this issue, but their practical implications remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we evaluate normalization strategies for transformer-based large time-series models trained with patching and efficient causal strategy. We showcase that normalization choice significantly influences both training convergence and forecasting performance.

</details>


### [207] [Temporal Context Conditioning for Seasonality-Aware Precipitation Nowcasting of High-Intensity Rainfall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09959)
*Gijs van Nieuwkoop,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TA-SmaAt-UNet，通过在SmaAt-UNet中加入时间条件层（昼夜周期和季节周期编码），在KNMI雷达降水数据上实验表明，时间条件能显著提升对高强度降雨事件的预报能力，改善季节变化表征和降雨强度分布预测，且参数成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习降水临近预报模型虽然能高效捕捉短期降水运动，但缺乏更广泛的能描述降雨发展条件的气象背景信息。本文探究轻量级时间上下文能否改善基于雷达的临近预报，尤其是对高强度降雨事件的预报。

Method: 在SmaAt-UNet骨干网络基础上加入时间条件层，使用昼夜时间编码和一年中的时间编码来调制中间特征表示，形成Time-Aware Small-Attention U-Net (TA-SmaAt-UNet)。

Result: 在KNMI雷达降水数据上：时间条件对罕见的高强度降水事件最有益，同时改善了季节变化表征和降雨强度分布预测。层传导分析表明，添加的时间条件层虽然参数成本很小，但被模型活跃使用。

Conclusion: 简单的、基于物理动机的时间上下文能够改善深度学习降水临近预报的真实性和可靠性。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting is increasingly being approached with deep learning models that learn directly from recent radar observations. Although such models can efficiently capture short-term precipitation motion, they often lack broader contextual information about the meteorological conditions under which rainfall develops. This paper investigates whether lightweight temporal context can improve radar-based nowcasting, particularly for high-intensity rainfall. We propose the Time-Aware Small-Attention U-Net (TA-SmaAt-UNet), which extends the core SmaAt-UNet model with temporal conditioning layers that use cyclical encodings of time-of-day and time-of-year to modulate intermediate feature representations. Experiments on KNMI radar precipitation data show that temporal conditioning is most beneficial for rare, high-intensity precipitation events, while also improving the representation of seasonal variability and predicted rainfall-intensity distributions. A layer conductance analysis further indicates that the added temporal conditioning layers are actively used by the model despite their small parameter cost. These findings suggest that simple, physically motivated temporal context can improve the realism and reliability of deep learning-based precipitation nowcasts. The implementation of our models and training setup is available on \href{https://github.com/gijsvn/TA-SmaAt-UNet}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [208] [HydraCIL: Decoupled Class-Incremental Learning through Prototype-Guided Multi-Head Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09960)
*Daniel Vila-Cruz,Laura Morán-Fernández,Verónica Bolón-Canedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HydraCIL是一种基于原型引导的多头分类器的解耦持续学习模型，专为资源和算力受限的嵌入式环境设计。它通过冻结骨干网络、按任务解耦特征提取与学习、创建轻量级任务专用分类头，并在推理时通过原型相似度选择合适头部，显著降低训练时间和碳排放，在多个数据集上达到或超越现有方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数类别增量学习方法依赖强大硬件和长训练周期，无法满足机器人或边缘AI设备等实际系统在资源受限条件下快速适应的需求。HydraCIL旨在填补这一空白，实现可持续部署的持续学习。

Method: 冻结骨干网络，解耦特征提取与学习过程；每个任务仅提取一次特征并创建轻量级任务特定分类器头，避免昂贵的骨干重训练；推理时通过原型相似度选择适当的分类头。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100、CoRe50和Flowers102数据集上，HydraCIL匹配或超越了现有最先进的CIL方法，同时显著减少了训练时间和碳足迹。

Conclusion: HydraCIL是一种实用且高效的持续学习解决方案，特别适用于能源效率和快速适应性至关重要的嵌入式及实际部署场景。

Abstract: We present HydraCIL, a decoupled continual learning model based on prototype-guided multi-head classifiers, targeting sustainable deployment in embedded and resource-constrained environments. While most Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) methods rely on powerful hardware and long retraining cycles, real-world systems, such as robots or edge AI devices, must adapt quickly with limited resources. HydraCIL addresses this gap by freezing the backbone and decoupling feature extraction from learning. For each task, features are extracted once and a lightweight, task-specific classifier head is created, avoiding costly backbone retraining. At inference, HydraCIL selects the appropriate head via similarity with prototypes. Experiments on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-100, CoRe50, and Flowers102 datasets show that HydraCIL matches or outperforms state-of-the-art CIL methods while significantly reducing training time and carbon footprint, making it a practical solution for continual learning in real-world and embedded settings, where energy efficiency and rapid adaptation are critical.

</details>


### [209] [3SPO: State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09961)
*Yu Han,Kailing Li,Yang Jiao,Yulin Dai,Yuqian Fu,Linhai Zhuo,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出3SPO算法，通过在每一步进行状态分数监督的策略优化，解决了多轮智能体场景中奖励稀疏、延迟和信用分配困难的问题。在ALFWorld和WebShop上，3SPO分别比GRPO提升了22.6%和15.6个点，同时实现2.4倍的状态探索和1.8倍的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习算法在轨迹级别进行策略优化，需要完整回合后更新，这在多轮智能体场景中存在奖励稀疏、延迟和单步信用难以分配的根本性挑战。

Method: 提出3SPO算法，基于历史成功率计算每个步骤的状态分数，实现逐步信用分配、自适应轨迹回滚和步后策略优化，无需价值函数估计或额外辅助模型。理论上通过每状态臂架抽象证明了对数级分配遗憾和样本复杂度保证。

Result: 在ALFWorld上比GRPO提升22.6%，在WebShop上提升15.6个点，使用类似资源实现2.4倍状态探索和1.8倍更快收敛。

Conclusion: 3SPO通过细粒度的逐步状态分数监督，有效解决了多轮智能体强化学习中的信用分配问题，在性能、探索效率和收敛速度上均显著优于现有轨迹级RL方法。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) as autonomous agents via reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled frontier models to achieve superhuman performance in long-horizon tasks. However, existing RL algorithms operate at the trajectory level, performing policy optimization only after collecting complete episode rollouts. This coarse-grained approach faces fundamental challenges in multi-turn agent settings where rewards are sparse, delayed, and credit assignment across individual steps is critical. In this work, we propose \textbf{State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization (3SPO)}, a novel RL algorithm that performs post-step policy optimization with dynamic state score supervision. At each step, 3SPO computes the state score based on historical success rates, supervising step-wise credit assignment, adaptive rollout and post-step policy optimization without requiring value function estimation or additional auxiliary models. Theoretically, under a per-state bandit abstraction, we show that the proposed score-supervised allocation mechanism achieves logarithmic allocation regret and provide sample-complexity guarantees for action identification, score distinguishability, and filtering stability. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct show that 3SPO consistently outperforms GRPO by $+22.6\%$ on ALFWorld and $+15.6$ points on WebShop, while using comparable resources to achieve $2.4\times$ more state exploration and $1.8\times$ faster convergence. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/3SPO.

</details>


### [210] [Optimality of FSQ Tokens for Continuous Diffusion for Categorical Data with Application to Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09962)
*Vadim Popov,Wenju Gu,Tasnima Sadekova,Georgii Aparin,Assel Yermekova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续扩散模型在离散数据（特别是文本到语音任务）中的潜在空间结构，发现FSQ分词方案在KL散度和预测准确率方面表现最优，基于FSQ的扩散模型在性能上超越了基于LLM的方法，且模型更小、速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望找到自回归大型语言模型在离散数据生成方面的合理替代方案，连续扩散模型为此提供了一个有前景的方向。

Method: 通过KL散度分析扩散路径测度下离散token潜在空间的结构性质，以及最优训练模型正确预测token的准确率，结合理论分析和数值实验验证。在文本到语音任务中使用语音token作为中间声学特征进行实际验证。

Result: FSQ分词方案在潜在空间结构上最适合连续扩散模型，基于FSQ的文本到语音扩散模型性能最优，且超越了强大的基于LLM的对应模型。

Conclusion: FSQ分词方案是连续扩散处理离散数据的最佳选择，基于该方案的模型在性能、模型大小和推理速度上均优于LLM基线。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion for categorical data is a framework belonging to the diffusion family and aiming at generating discrete data. The scientific interest to such models has been constantly increasing these days because researchers try to achieve a challenging goal of finding reasonable alternatives to autoregressive large language models. In this paper, we study the properties of the structure of the latent space corresponding to discrete tokens expressed in terms of Kullback-Leibler divergence on diffusion path measures and accuracy of the correct token prediction by the optimally trained diffusion model. We find that FSQ tokenization scheme has the latent space structure with the properties that make it best suited for continuous diffusion for categorical data as verified through rigorous theoretical analysis and numerical experiments. To validate our findings in real-life scenario, we train several text-to-speech diffusion models having speech tokens as intermediate acoustic features, and show that the one based on FSQ tokens indeed performs the best, and, moreover, it outperforms its strong LLM-based counterpart, at the same time being significantly smaller and faster.

</details>


### [211] [Interpreting and Steering a Text-to-Speech Language Model with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10029)
*Nikita Koriagin,Georgii Aparin,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在CosyVoice3的语言模型骨干上训练BatchTopK稀疏自编码器，并设计了模态感知的自动解释流程来标记特征。恢复的特征可解释，涵盖音素、笑声、口音提示和说话者性别。通过SAE潜在空间的干预证明这些特征是因果性的而非描述性的：目标干预将笑声概率从0.02提升到0.79，翻转感知的说话者性别，并控制语速同时保留内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地作为TTS系统的骨干，但我们对文本和生成的语音token共享单一残差流时模型所构建的表征知之甚少，因此需要探究其内部表征机制。

Method: 在CosyVoice3的LM骨干上训练BatchTopK稀疏自编码器，并设计模态感知的自动解释流程，根据特征激活的来源（文本前缀上下文、1秒语音片段或两者）对特征进行标记。

Result: 恢复的特征可解释，涵盖音素、笑声、口音提示和说话者性别。通过SAE潜在空间进行干预，可将笑声概率从0.02提升至0.79，翻转感知的说话者性别，并控制语速而不影响语义内容。

Conclusion: SAE特征既可作为可解释性对象，也可作为TTS合成的控制方向，证明了其因果性而非仅仅是描述性。

Abstract: Language models increasingly serve as the backbone of text-to-speech (TTS) systems, yet we understand little about the representations they build when text and generated speech tokens share a single residual stream. We train BatchTopK sparse autoencoders on the LM backbone of CosyVoice3 and introduce a modality-aware auto-interp pipeline that labels each feature from where it fires-text-prefix context, 1-second speech clips, or both. The recovered features are interpretable, spanning phonemes, laughter, accent prompts and speaker gender. Steering through the SAE latent space shows these features are causal rather than merely descriptive: targeted interventions raise laughter probability from 0.02 to 0.79, flip perceived speaker gender, and control speech rate while preserving spoken content. SAE features thus serve both as interpretability objects and as control directions for TTS synthesis.

</details>


### [212] [Bittensor Agent Arenas as a Trajectory Primitive: Distilling a Shopping Agent from ShoppingBench Subnet Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10064)
*Shardul Bansal,Seth Schilbe,Jarrod Barnes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过激励对齐的智能体竞技场（ORO SN15）生成高质量轨迹数据用于小模型后训练，解决了传统数据来源（前沿模型合成数据、生产日志）的偏差与污染问题。使用结构质量过滤器将原始数据流转化为可训练语料，在ShoppingBench任务上使Qwen3-4B的ASR从18.0%提升至42.7%，接近合成数据SFT基线（43.6%），并指出子任务数据流是缩小与SFT+GRPO（48.7%）差距的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型智能体后训练的主要瓶颈不在于算法，而在于所使用的轨迹数据底物。现有两种数据来源——前沿模型合成数据和生产日志——各有缺陷：合成数据继承生成器的偏见并坍缩长尾分布，未过滤的生产日志缺乏评判且被捷径行为污染。因此需要一种新的数据生成机制。

Method: 1) 构建ORO SN15竞技场：包含竞速机制、LLM推理评判器和旋转泄露簇防护问题集，生成具有激励对齐多样性、逐轨迹评判和反记忆保留评估属性的语料。2) 设计结构质量过滤器：保留自主智能体轨迹（模型自己发出工具调用），拒绝子任务轨迹（模型仅分类或叙述确定性搜索循环）。3) 采用SFT-then-GRPO训练流水线对Qwen3-4B进行后训练，并引入逐步骤的教师接地Dr. GRPO奖励。

Result: 在泄露簇防护的保留集上，生产严格评分下模型ASR从Qwen3-4B基线的18.0%提升至42.7%，仅略低于合成数据SFT基线的43.6%，且仅使用单日子网产出的一小部分。pass@8（53.3%）与pass@1（34.8%）之间存在显著差距；逐步骤Dr. GRPO奖励能将此差距转化为过程改进。

Conclusion: 激励对齐的智能体竞技场能够有效生成高质量训练轨迹数据，克服传统数据源的局限。子任务数据流是缩小与SFT+GRPO（48.7%）之间差距的主要杠杆。本文开源了过滤器、语料分割和竞技场机制。

Abstract: Small-model agentic post-training is bottlenecked less by the algorithm than by the trajectory substrate it consumes. Leading recipes (RLVR, group-relative RL, rejection-sampled re-SFT) all need multi-turn traces carrying per-trajectory supervision, and the two existing sources fall short: frontier-synthesised data inherits the synthesizer's biases and collapses the long tail, while unfiltered production logs are unjudged and contaminated by shortcut behaviour. We argue that an incentive-aligned agent arena can be engineered to manufacture such trajectories, and demonstrate this on ORO Subnet 15 (SN15), a Bittensor deployment of the ShoppingBench agentic-commerce benchmark. SN15's race mechanism, LLM reasoning judge, and rotating leak-cluster-guarded problem suite yield a corpus with three properties: incentive-aligned diversity, per-trajectory judging, and anti-memorised held-out evaluation. We introduce a structural-quality filter that converts the raw firehose into a trainable corpus by keeping agentic trajectories (the model itself emits the tool calls) and rejecting sub-task trajectories (the model only classifies or narrates over a deterministic search loop), then post-train Qwen3-4B with a recipe matched to the published ShoppingBench SFT-then-GRPO pipeline. On a leak-cluster-guarded held-out partition scored production-strict, the model lifts from the published Qwen3-4B base of 18.0% ASR to 42.7%, within single-problem noise of the synthetic-data SFT-only baseline (43.6%), while training on a fraction of a single day of subnet output. The supervised stack leaves a large pass@8 to pass@1 gap (53.3% vs 34.8%); a per-step teacher-grounded Dr. GRPO reward converts that headroom into process improvement, and we identify the sub-task firehose as the primary lever for closing the gap to the 48.7% SFT+GRPO bar. We release the filter, the corpus splits, and the arena mechanics.

</details>


### [213] [Importance-Aware Scheduling for High-Dimensional Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10068)
*Ruinan Wang,Ian Nabney,Mohammad Golbabaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GIF(Greedy Importance First)方法，通过小样本预热估计超参数重要性，基于重要性分组并分配试验预算，在高维HPO中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超参数优化器在高维空间中评估成本高，且进度被大量低影响变量稀释，效率低下。

Method: 使用小样本预热估计超参数重要性→形成基于重要性的分组→按比例分配试验预算→保留全空间回退机制。

Result: 在高维基准上，GIF在收敛速度和最终最优解上优于TPE、BOHB、Random Search和Sequential Grouping；低维场景下竞争力相当但优势较小。

Conclusion: GIF是一种简单且即插即用的方法，能有效提升高维HPO的样本效率。

Abstract: Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) is essential for building high-performing ML/DL models, yet conventional optimizers often struggle in high-dimensional spaces where evaluations are costly and progress is diluted across many low-impact variables. We propose Greedy Importance First (GIF), an importance-aware scheduling strategy that uses a small-sample warm start to estimate hyperparameter importance, forms importance-based groups, allocates trials proportionally, and retains a full-space fallback. We evaluate GIF under fixed evaluation budgets on five anisotropic analytic functions, Bayesmark, and NAS-Bench-301. On the higher-dimensional benchmarks, GIF reaches better incumbents with faster convergence than TPE, BOHB, Random Search, and Sequential Grouping. On Bayesmark, where the effective dimensionality is smaller, GIF remains competitive but the margins are smaller. Ablation studies show that importance estimation, proportional allocation, and the fallback step all contribute to the gains. We also verify that the HIA component recovers the intended anisotropy on the analytic benchmarks. These results suggest that GIF is a simple and plug-compatible way to improve sample efficiency in high-dimensional HPO.

</details>


### [214] [Spatiotemporal Seismic Hazard Assessment Using VQ-VAE and Seismic Statistical Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10069)
*Wei Quan,Denise Gorse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在地震预测研究中，将基于60个地震统计特征（SSFs）的XGBoost模型从全区域预测扩展到局部预测（半径24公里），并引入基于VQ-VAE自编码器从二维地震地图提取的新特征。局部SSFs预测表现优异（AUC与之前全区域研究相当），而VQ-VAE特征经SHAP分析排名最高，能进一步提升性能并几乎完全替代传统b值特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究已证明60个地震统计特征（SSFs）比428个通用时间序列特征具有更高的预测价值，本文旨在将此方法从全区域预测扩展到局部精细化预测，并探索如何将一维目录数据与二维地震地图信息结合，以进一步提升预测性能。

Method: 使用日本地震目录数据，将特征计算和预测区域限制在候选事件周围24公里半径的圆形区域内；训练VQ-VAE模型以重现二维地震地图，将其重构误差作为衡量局部地壳应力积累的新特征；采用XGBoost作为预测模型，通过SHAP分析进行特征重要性排序。

Result: 局部SSFs预测取得了与前期全区域研究相当的AUC值；新引入的VQ-VAE特征在SHAP分析中排名最高，能够进一步提升预测性能，并且几乎完全取代了传统计算得到的b值特征。

Conclusion: 基于一维目录数据的SSFs在局部地震预测中同样有效，而结合二维地震地图的VQ-VAE衍生特征能进一步增强模型性能，且该新特征与传统b值存在高度信息重叠，提示b值可能蕴含空间应力积累信号。

Abstract: In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.

</details>


### [215] [Temporal Sheaf Neural Networks with Dynamic Orthogonal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10071)
*Md Sadek Hossain Asif,Tanzila Khan,Md. Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时序束神经网络（TSNN），通过为每个节点配备时变正交坐标系并在进行节点状态比较前显式执行局部坐标系间的传输操作，构建了一个新颖的时序链接预测框架。该方法在多个基准测试中匹配或超越现有最强方法，尤其在图节点角色异质性强的场景下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时序图模型在共享全局嵌入空间中操作，无法有效建模节点特异且演化的交互语义。TSNN旨在通过动态局部坐标系捕获节点随时间变化的交互模式，解决全局嵌入空间表达能力不足的问题。

Method: TSNN使用高效低秩Householder积参数化每个节点的时变正交坐标系；在坐标系更新时精确保留存储的隐藏状态；采用几何残差解码器，基于传输距离进行预测并学习残差校正。所有计算严格遵循因果性，仅利用事件前历史。证明了对称度归一化束拉普拉斯与对称归一化图拉普拉斯正交相似，TSNN使用的全激活特征缩放扩散等价于组合束狄利克雷能量的度量梯度步，具有无度单调下降与非扩张保证。坐标系漂移仅线性扰动更新。

Result: 在TGB v2链接预测与时序异质排行榜以及DGB基准套件上，TSNN在大部分基准中匹配或超越最强先前方法，在图节点角色异质性强的图上改进最大。消融实验证实了动态坐标系、正交传输和几何残差解码的独特益处。

Conclusion: TSNN通过动态局部坐标系和显式传输机制有效建模节点特异演化交互语义，理论分析和实验验证了其优越性，尤其适用于节点角色异质性强的时序图。

Abstract: We introduce Temporal Sheaf Neural Networks (TSNN), a temporal link prediction framework that equips each node with a time-varying orthogonal frame and compares node states only after explicit transport between local coordinate systems. In contrast to existing continuous-time graph models that operate in a shared global embedding space, TSNN models node-specific and evolving interaction semantics through dynamic local frames. The model parameterizes per-node frames via efficient low-rank Householder products, preserves stored hidden states exactly under frame updates, and uses a geometric-residual decoder that anchors predictions on transported distances while learning residual corrections. All computations are strictly causal and use only the pre-event history. We show that the symmetric degree-normalized sheaf Laplacian is orthogonally similar to the symmetric normalized graph Laplacian, with the random-walk normalized form similar in the corresponding degree metric; the full-active, feature-scaled diffusion used by TSNN is exactly a metric-gradient step on the combinatorial sheaf Dirichlet energy, with a degree-free monotone-descent and non-expansiveness guarantee. Frame drift perturbs updates only linearly. Across TGB v2 link-prediction and temporal-heterogeneous leaderboards, together with the DGB benchmark suite, TSNN matches or surpasses the strongest prior methods on most benchmarks, with the largest improvements on graphs exhibiting strong node-role heterogeneity. Ablations confirm the distinct benefit of dynamic frames, orthogonal transport, and geometric-residual decoding.

</details>


### [216] [VFUSE: Virulent Feature Understanding with Sparse autoEncoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10080)
*Michael Yu,Matthew L. Olson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VFUSE方法，通过在扩散变换器激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），对蛋白质设计模型（RoseTTAFold3和RFDiffusion3）进行机械可解释性审计，发现SAE潜在空间中的线性探针比原始表示能更好地检测有害设计，并识别出仅对有害设计激活的单语义特征（AUROC最高0.84），这是首个全原子扩散模型的SAE训练和蛋白质设计模型的特征级毒性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在蛋白质设计等领域取得显著进展，但同时也带来了有害蛋白质不透明生成的潜在风险，因此需要对蛋白质模型进行可解释性审计，检测其是否编码了与毒性相关的特征。

Method: 提出VFUSE框架，在扩散变换器（RoseTTAFold3和RFDiffusion3）的激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），然后在SAE潜在空间中训练线性探针进行有害设计检测，并与原始模型表示上的探针性能进行对比。

Result: 在特定模块中，SAE潜在空间上的线性探针对有害设计的检测性能显著优于原始模型表示；识别出单语义特征，其仅对有害设计激活，AUROC达到0.84（q<10^{-13}）。

Conclusion: 这是首个在全原子扩散模型上训练SAE的工作，也是首个对蛋白质设计模型进行特征级毒性审计的研究，为安全且可解释的蛋白质设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Generative models have shown remarkable progress in a variety of domains such as protein design, but such power enables the opaque generation of hazardous proteins. In this work, we introduce VFUSE (Virulent Feature Understanding with Sparse autoEncoders), a mechanistic interpretability approach that trains SAEs on diffusion-transformer activations to audit protein models for hazard-aware features. We apply VFUSE to RoseTTAFold3 and RFDiffusion3, popular open-weight models for protein folding and synthesis. We find that for certain blocks, linear probes detect hazardous designs significantly better when fit in the SAE latent space over the original model's representations: improving interpretability without sacrificing model performance. Furthermore, we identify monosemantic features from the SAE that fire only on hazardous designs at up to AUROC $0.84$ ($q < 10^{-13}$). To our knowledge this is the first SAE trained on an all-atom diffusion model and the first feature-level virulence audit of a protein design model, paving the way towards safe and interpretable protein design.

</details>


### [217] [Divide-and-Conquer Modeling for the CTF-4-Science Lorenz Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10084)
*Shundong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分治建模策略，针对Lorenz混沌基准测试的五个场景（干净预测、噪声重建、噪声输入预测、少样本学习、参数泛化），为每个任务组匹配专用模型，最终得分79.63，证明有界的场景特定更新优于广泛的模型替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型类难以处理混沌系统预测中多样化的评估场景（干净/噪声/少样本/参数泛化），因此需要为不同任务组定制专用模型。

Method: 采用分治策略，将不同预测模块匹配到对应任务组：平滑重建用于噪声全轨迹去噪；NG-RC/NVAR模型用于噪声长时间吸引子预测；拟合Lorenz转移修正用于敏感的干净短时前缀；参数前缀混合用于插值任务。

Result: 最终公开得分79.63，表明有界的场景特定更新优于广泛的模型替换。

Conclusion: 在混合混沌预测基准上，为不同场景定制专用模型比使用单一通用模型更有效。

Abstract: This work presents a divide-and-conquer modeling strategy for the CTF-4-Science Lorenz benchmark, which evaluates chaotic-system prediction across twelve hidden scores and five scenario families: clean forecasting, noisy reconstruction, noisy-input forecasting, few-shot learning, and parametric generalization. Rather than forcing one model class to handle all regimes, the final system matched each prediction block to the evaluation behavior of its task group. The main contributions are: smoothing-based reconstruction for noisy full-trajectory denoising; NG-RC/NVAR models tuned for noisy long-time attractor forecasting; a fitted Lorenz transition correction restricted to the sensitive clean short-time prefix; and a parametric prefix blend for the interpolation task. The resulting system with final public score of 79.63 shows that bounded, scenario-specific updates can outperform broad model replacement on mixed chaotic forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [218] [A Theory on Flow Matching with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10089)
*Yihan He,Qishuo Yin,Yuan Cao,Jianqing Fan,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为神经参数化条件速度场的流匹配建立了理论基础，证明了过参数化双层ReLU网络下梯度下降的收敛性，推导了条件速度场匹配目标的泛化界，并提供了生成样本的Wasserstein距离保证。该分析基于多任务表示学习的泛化界，通过合成数据和真实图像基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为神经参数化条件速度场的流匹配方法建立严格的理论基础，填补该领域缺乏收敛性和泛化保证的理论空白。

Method: 采用过参数化双层ReLU神经网络分析梯度下降收敛性；通过多任务表示学习的泛化界（可处理无界损失）推导条件速度场匹配的泛化保证；基于这些结果进一步推导生成样本的Wasserstein距离上界。

Result: 证明了梯度下降在过参数化设置下的收敛性；得到了条件速度场匹配目标的泛化界；给出了生成样本的Wasserstein距离保证；在合成数据和真实图像基准上实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为流匹配模型建立了系统性的理论框架，所提出的多任务表示学习泛化界可作为独立工具应用于其他生成模型分析，实验验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we develop theoretical foundation for flow matching with neural-network-parameterized conditional velocity fields. We establish convergence guarantees for gradient descent in the over-parameterized 2-layered ReLU neural network regime. We derive generalization bounds for the conditional velocity-field matching objective. Building on these results, we provide Wasserstein-distance guarantees for the samples generated by the induced flow. Our analysis is based on generalization bound for multi-task representation learning with unbounded losses, which may be of independent interest beyond flow-based generative modeling. These theoretical results are validated through extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world image benchmarks.

</details>


### [219] [Decision-Making under Combinatorial Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10092)
*Yifan Hong,Hongmiao Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究引入投资分配任务研究组合风险下的决策，发现人们主要依据成功概率增量及初始概率进行选择。通过符号回归发现简洁描述模型，揭示显示诱导概率分布会显著改变行为，使决策更接近彩票估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统风险决策研究多聚焦于单次彩票选择，而现实决策常涉及多个风险成分的组合风险，其中结果分布是被诱导而非直接给定的，且精确评估成本高昂。现有研究缺乏对这一重要场景的深入理解。

Method: 设计投资分配任务（投资于某成分可提高其成功概率并重塑结果分布），通过行为实验收集数据，并采用符号回归方法发现简洁的描述性模型，而非依赖预定义基准或手工假设。

Result: 参与者倾向于选择概率增量更大的选项；增量相等时选择初始成功概率更高的选项。显示诱导概率质量函数后，参与者对组合风险特征的反应降低，选择方差减小。符号回归发现模型主要依赖组合风险特征（如投资后成功概率），而非完整诱导分布的精确评估。

Conclusion: 人们在组合风险决策中主要通过核心特征（概率变化）导航，仅当诱导概率分布被明确显示时才会转向彩票估值方式。显示PMF时的行为可通过组合风险模型加上前景理论残差模型很好地解释。

Abstract: Decision-making under risk is typically studied through single-shot lottery choices. Yet many real decisions involve combinatorial risk, where risk arises from multiple risky components, so the lottery over outcomes is induced rather than given outright and can be costly to evaluate exactly. We introduce an investment-allocation task to study decision under combinatorial risk, where investing in a component raises its success probability and thereby reshapes the outcome distribution. Participants favor the option with the larger probability increment, and, when increments are equal, the option with the higher initial success probability. Revealing the induced probability mass function (PMF) substantially changes behavior, making participants less responsive to combinatorial-risk features and reducing choice variance. To explain these patterns, we move beyond standard benchmarks and hand-crafted hypotheses with symbolic regression to discover compact descriptive models. The discovered models rely mainly on combinatorial-risk features, such as the after-investment success probability, rather than exact evaluation of the full induced distribution. Behavior under the displayed PMF is then well explained by augmenting this model with a prospect-theoretic residual model. The results show that people navigate combinatorial risk primarily through its core features, shifting toward lottery valuation only when the induced PMF is displayed.

</details>


### [220] [Unsupervised Style Representation Learning for AI-Text Detection via Paraphrase Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10099)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Barry Chen,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需作者标签的神经风格表征学习框架，通过重构人类文本与机器生成释义来学习判别性风格特征，在少样本和零样本检测场景中均表现出色，并泛化到作者验证等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于写作风格的检测器依赖作者标签进行训练，且仅限于少样本推理，需要可能不总是可用的分布内样本，限制了实际应用。

Method: 训练一个风格编码器，从机器生成的释义中重构人类作者撰写的文本；在训练过程中冻结语义编码器，迫使风格编码器仅捕获非语义特征以完成重构。利用学习到的表征通过少样本检测器和基于DeepSVDD的零样本检测器进行检测。

Result: 在少样本设置中匹配或超越所有基线；在零样本设置中与全监督分类器在分布内测试数据上具有竞争力，并对未见过的LLM具有更好的泛化能力。在作者验证和细粒度风格区分任务上也取得竞争性表现。

Conclusion: 无监督学习的风格表征能有效检测机器生成文本，且无需作者标签即可泛化到多种下游任务。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns about misuse such as plagiarism, misinformation, and automated influence operations, motivating the need for robust detectors. Recent work has shown that neural representations of writing style are effective for detection and, crucially, robust to adversarial attacks that defeat most existing detectors. However, current style-based detectors rely on authorship labels for training, and are limited to few-shot inference for detection, requiring in-distribution samples that may not always be available. We learn discriminative style features without authorship labels by training a style encoder to reconstruct human-authored text from its machine-generated paraphrase; freezing a semantic encoder during training biases the style encoder to capture only the non-semantic features needed for reconstruction. We evaluate the learned representations via two detection strategies: a few-shot detector and a zero-shot DeepSVDD-based detector. Across benchmarks, our method matches or outperforms all baselines in the few-shot setting and, in the zero-shot regime, is competitive with fully supervised classifiers on in-distribution test data while generalizing better to unseen LLMs. Beyond detection, the learned representations generalize to unseen tasks, achieving competitive performance on authorship verification and fine-grained style discrimination despite never being trained on either objective.

</details>


### [221] [Nonlinear Estimator: Dual Bayesian Affine Estimators for Parameter Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10111)
*Sasan Vakili,Daniël Woonings,Pradyumna Paruchuri,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Wiener型状态空间模型提出了一种非线性参数估计器，通过固定点架构耦合两个仿射最小均方误差估计器（一个估计未知参数，一个估计潜在变量），并引入了动态基统计量。两种实现方案中，双基-参数估计器性能接近纯仿射估计器，而双状态-参数估计器取得了最低的参数均方误差，优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wiener型状态空间模型中的参数估计问题具有非线性特性，传统的纯仿射MMSE估计器性能受限，需要设计能够更好利用非线性结构信息的参数估计方法。

Method: 提出固定点架构耦合两个仿射MMSE估计器，将动态基统计量（DBS）估计嵌入其中。设计了两种DBS构建策略：双基-参数估计器（联合仿射基估计器与仿射参数估计器）和双状态-参数估计器（先计算仿射状态估计及其协方差，再通过高斯DBS算子映射得到DBS估计）。两者均采用交替固定点迭代方式，利用前一轮的插件估计统计量更新另一组分的先验。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明：双基-参数估计器的参数均方误差与纯仿射参数估计器相当；双状态-参数估计器取得了最低的参数均方误差，优于双基-参数估计器、纯仿射参数估计器以及基于顺序蒙特卡洛的粒子吉布斯和期望最大化算法变体。

Conclusion: 所提出的双状态-参数估计器框架在Wiener型状态空间模型参数估计任务中表现出最优性能，验证了通过耦合仿射估计器并引入DBS统计量的非线性估计策略的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a nonlinear parameter estimator for Wiener-type state-space models obtained as a fixed-point architecture that couples two affine minimum mean-squared error (MMSE) estimators: one for the unknown parameters and one for latent variables. The architecture retains the functional structure of the optimal affine MMSE parameter estimator while incorporating Dynamic Basis Statistics (DBS) estimates that summarize nonlinear basis-function evaluations. Two DBS construction strategies are developed, leading to two nonlinear estimator frameworks. The dual basis-parameter estimator combines an affine basis estimator with the affine parameter estimator, whereas the dual state-parameter estimator first computes affine state estimates and their covariances, then maps these state-estimate statistics through a Gaussian DBS operator to obtain DBS estimates. Both dual estimators admit fixed-point characterizations that alternate between estimating each component using the updated prior of the other, obtained from that component's plug-in estimate statistics from the previous iteration. The efficacy of the proposed methods is examined via extensive Monte Carlo experiments, showing that the dual basis-parameter estimator attains parameter mean-squared errors comparable to those of the purely affine parameter estimator, while the dual state-parameter estimator achieves the lowest parameter mean-squared error, outperforming both the dual basis-parameter and purely affine parameter estimators, as well as sequential Monte Carlo variants of classical Particle Gibbs and Expectation-Maximization schemes.

</details>


### [222] [FedSteer: Taming Extreme Gradient Staleness in Federated Learning with Corrective Projections and Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10124)
*Haoran Zhang,Cainã Figueiredo Pereira,Marie Siew,Xutong Liu,Carlee Joe-Wong,Rachid El-Azouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSteer通过构建梯度子空间并利用选择性缓存策略，解决联邦学习中由于客户端参与不均导致的聚合方差和训练不稳定问题，有效将过期梯度"导向"当前全局目标，在多个场景下提升超过7%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端参与不均导致聚合方差增大，重用陈旧更新虽可减少方差，但严重的更新过时性会破坏训练稳定性，现有方法对此应对不足。

Method: 提出FedSteer方法：1) 从近期客户端梯度缓存构建低维梯度子空间，表征当前优化景观；2) 将活跃客户端真实梯度投影到子空间寻找最优坐标；3) 对非活跃客户端，利用已演化的子空间重用这些坐标，将过期梯度"导向"当前目标；4) 结合选择性缓存策略识别代表性客户端子集，降低服务器内存开销。

Result: FedSteer显著优于基线方法，在挑战性场景下防止性能崩溃，并在其他场景中取得超过7%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: FedSteer有效解决了非均匀客户端参与导致的梯度过时问题，通过子空间投影和导向机制提升了联邦学习的训练稳定性与模型性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is often subject to aggregation variance if clients do not consistently participate in training rounds. While reusing stale model updates from inactive clients is a common technique to reduce this variance, we find that with skewed client participation, the resulting update staleness can become severe enough to destabilize training. To remedy this, we propose FedSteer, a novel method that constructs a gradient subspace from a cache of recent client gradients to serve as a low-dimensional representation of the current optimization landscape. FedSteer projects an active client's true gradient onto this subspace to find a set of optimal coordinates. For an inactive client, FedSteer reuses these coordinates with the now-evolved subspace drifted by other active clients. This process effectively "steers" outdated gradients toward the current global objective. This is complemented by a selective caching strategy that identifies a representative client subset to form the subspace, reducing server memory. Experiments demonstrate that FedSteer significantly outperforms baselines, preventing performance collapse in challenging scenarios while delivering accuracy gains of over 7% in others.

</details>


### [223] [Discovering Interpretable Multi-Parameter Control Policies for Evolutionary Algorithms Using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10129)
*Tai Nguyen,Phong Le,Carola Doerr,Nguyen Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用深度强化学习克服进化算法中多参数控制的难题，以(1+($λ$,$λ$))-GA优化OneMax为案例。通过引入动作空间分解、奖励平移和长程折扣等改进，发现Double DQN优于PPO，并将学习到的行为蒸馏为可解释的符号控制策略，该策略在性能上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在进化算法参数控制中的应用日益增多，但严格的理论分析仍局限于单参数设置，缺乏有效的、可解释的多参数策略，难以进行形式化研究。

Method: 以(1+($λ$,$λ$))-GA优化OneMax为案例，首先引入算法无关的增强技术（动作空间分解、奖励平移、长程折扣），然后比较常见深度强化学习方法（DDQN、PPO），最后将神经网络学习到的行为蒸馏为透明的符号控制策略。

Result: 标准深度强化学习方法在多参数设置中难以收敛；Double DQN能避免PPO出现的策略崩溃问题，生成适合下游分析的轨迹；蒸馏得到的符号控制策略不仅可解释，而且在各种问题规模上持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习可以突破进化算法多参数控制的理论分析瓶颈，符号策略蒸馏为桥接黑盒学习与形式化理论分析提供了可行路径，Double DQN在此类多参数控制任务中优于PPO。

Abstract: While deep Reinforcement Learning (deep-RL) has been increasingly applied to parameter control in evolutionary algorithms, rigorous theoretical analysis of parameter control remains largely restricted to single-parameter settings, owing to the difficulty of deriving effective, interpretable multi-parameter policies amenable to formal study. We demonstrate how deep-RL can be leveraged to overcome this barrier, using the (1+($λ$,$λ$))-genetic algorithm optimizing OneMax, one of the few problems where a super-constant speedup of dynamic control has been formally proven, as a representative case study. We first show that standard approaches struggle to converge in this multi-parameter setting, and introduce algorithm-agnostic enhancements targeting action-space decomposition, reward shifting, and long-horizon discounting. With these in place, we compare common deep-RL methods and find that Double Deep Q-Networks uniquely avoid the policy collapse observed in Proximal Policy Optimization, yielding trajectories suitable for downstream analysis. Crucially, we move beyond the ``black-box'' nature of neural networks by distilling the learned behaviors into a transparent, symbolic control policy. This resulting policy does not only offer interpretability for future theoretical analysis but also yields exceptional performance, consistently outperforming existing baselines across a wide range of problem sizes.

</details>


### [224] [A Continuous-Time Markov Chain Framework for Insertion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10199)
*Dhruvesh Patel,Benjamin Rozonoyer,Soumitra Das,Tahira Naseem,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将插入式语言模型（ILM）的加噪过程建模为变长序列空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链，推导出扩散式去噪目标，并证明现有ILM公式是该框架的特例。实验表明该方法在合成规划任务上保留了插入式生成的优势，在语言建模上与从左到右生成和掩码扩散模型具有竞争力，同时提供更灵活的采样方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插入式语言模型的公式大多是临时性的（ad-hoc），缺乏统一的理论框架，本文旨在从基本原理出发推导出更严谨的扩散式去噪目标。

Method: 将插入式生成的加噪过程形式化为变长序列空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链，并由此推导出扩散风格的去噪目标函数，统一了先前插入式语言模型的各种公式。

Result: 在合成规划任务上，该方法保留了插入式生成相比从左到右生成和掩码扩散模型的优势；在语言建模上，该方法与从左到右生成和掩码扩散模型表现相当，且相比现有插入式语言模型提供更灵活的采样能力。

Conclusion: 基于扩散框架的插入式语言模型不仅在理论上统一了现有工作，还在实际任务中展现了插入式生成的优势和更灵活的采样特性，是一种有前景的生成范式。

Abstract: Insertion Language Models (ILMs) offer several advantages over left-to-right generation and mask-based generation. However, existing formulations of insertion-based generation have largely been ad-hoc. In this paper, we derive a diffusion-style denoising objective for ILMs from first principles by formulating the noising process as a continuous-time Markov chain on the space of variable-length sequences. We show that previous formulations of ILMs can be viewed as special cases of this denoising framework. Through empirical evaluation on a synthetic planning task, we show that the proposed approach retains the benefits of insertion-based generation over left-to-right generation and masked diffusion models. In language modeling, our diffusion-based approach is competitive with left-to-right generation and masked diffusion models, while offering additional flexibility in sampling compared to existing insertion language models.

</details>


### [225] [Ambiguous Strategic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10137)
*Ivri Hikri,Nir Rosenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在监管要求部分信息披露的场景下，学习器如何通过模糊策略（揭示一组可能的分类器而非单一分类器）来联合优化分类器及其不确定性，并开发了高效算法及实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统战略分类假设分类器完全公开，但实践中监管可能只要求部分披露。本文旨在探究系统为何选择完全披露，以及在部分披露约束下如何最优地设计分类器。

Method: 借鉴鲁棒机制设计中的模糊性概念，学习器公开一组可能的分类器范围，但实际选择其中某个实现。开发了计算最佳响应和训练的高效算法，并进行实证探索。

Result: 证明了模糊性策略在部分信息披露下的有效性，给出了高效算法，并通过实验展示了战略学习在这种新设置下的结果。

Conclusion: 在监管部分披露要求下，系统可以通过模糊分类器范围来获得优势，这为理解战略分类中的信息披露策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: A common assumption in strategic classification is that the classifier is public knowledge. However, it remains unclear whether, and why, a system would choose to commit to full disclosure. We study a setting in which regulation requires the system to disclose some, but not all, of the information.
