These properties make the proposed approach particularly suitable for applications where both predictive correctness and confidence are essential for trustworthy decision making.

</details>


### [261] [KG-SoftMAP: Soft Knowledge-Graph Priors for Bayesian Network Structure Learning from Sparse Discrete Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10358)
*Guoliang Xu,James E. Corter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏离散数据中贝叶斯网络结构学习困难的问题，提出KG-SoftMAP方法，将加权有向知识图谱编码为软先验，结合BDeu评分与logit形式先验进行MAP优化。在合成基准上（ρ=0.05时DF1从近零提升至0.14-0.29，ρ≥0.2时达0.46-0.96），在真实教育数据上作为诊断模型提供与KG一致的边和校准联合概率，但若无有意义KG则判别式逻辑回归更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏离散数据中变量对缺乏联合观测，导致基于数据的结构学习方法效果差；而领域知识（通常以加权有向知识图谱形式存在）虽不完美但可获取，如何有效融合数据与这类知识以提升BN结构学习性能是核心动机。

Method: 提出KG-SoftMAP方法，将加权有向KG编码为软、置信度加权、可被数据覆盖的边先验，通过最大化结合BDeu评分与logit形式先验的MAP目标来学习BN结构。KG可来自专家或LLM提取。

Result: 合成基准上，ρ=0.05时DF1从近零提升至0.14-0.29，ρ≥0.2时达0.46-0.96，且随KG质量下降性能优雅退化。真实教育数据上，作为诊断模型在SAF任务中F1_FAIL仅比逻辑回归低0.03，同时提供KG一致的边和校准联合概率。

Conclusion: KG-SoftMAP能有效融合不完美领域知识与稀疏数据恢复BN结构，适合需可解释性和推理的诊断场景；若无有意义KG，纯判别式逻辑回归更优先。

Abstract: Learning Bayesian network (BN) structure from sparse discrete data is hard: when each instance records only a few variables, most variable pairs lack the joint observations needed for reliable scoring, and data-only methods recover little structure. Imperfect domain knowledge, expressible as a weighted directed knowledge graph (KG), is often available. We propose KG-SoftMAP, which encodes such a KG as a soft, confidence-weighted, data-overridable edge prior and maximizes a MAP objective combining the BDeu score with a logit-form prior; the KG may be expert-curated or LLM-extracted. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, the only setting with ground-truth DAGs, KG-SoftMAP recovers partial directed structure at $ρ=0.05$ (DF1 $0.14$ to $0.29$, versus near-zero baselines) and substantially more once $ρ\geq0.2$ (DF1 $0.46$ to $0.96$), when paired with an informative but imperfect KG; recovery degrades gracefully as KG quality drops. On real sparse educational data, which has no ground-truth DAG, we evaluate deployment-facing measures only: prediction, calibration, and KG-consistency. The learned BN is best read as a diagnostic model: on SAF it trails logistic regression by $0.03$ F1_FAIL while providing KG-consistent edges, calibrated joint probabilities, and inference from arbitrary observed concept subsets; when no meaningful KG exists, discriminative logistic regression is preferable.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Absolute Imitation: Anchored Residual Guidance for Privileged On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10385)
*Wenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定残差在线策略蒸馏（AR-OPD），通过解耦特权监督中的局部可达推理步骤与未来导向的预言信号，解决传统特权在线策略蒸馏中存在的可达性失配和事后偏差问题，在多种推理任务上平均超越全特权OPD 2.3个点、超越SFT 7.9个点，并将事后泄漏降低21.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特权在线策略蒸馏方法将特权信息视为单一模仿目标，未能分离局部可达推理步骤与未来条件化的预言信号，导致学生模型被鼓励去匹配超出其局部预测支持的 hindsight-biased 分布，从而跳过有效的中间推理步骤，产生可达性失配问题。

Method: 提出锚定残差在线策略蒸馏（AR-OPD）框架，采用双视角方法解耦特权监督：建立局部兼容的锚点（使用部分特权教师），将预言性先见（oracle foresight）作为受控残差注入，提供目的地导向的指导，而非强制完全的全局模仿。

Result: 在多种推理任务上，AR-OPD超越全特权OPD 2.3个点、超越SFT 7.9个点；事后泄漏减少21.7%；在超过768 token的长程轨迹任务上，晚期漂移得到缓解，获得高达7.2个点的优势。

Conclusion: 通过锚定残差机制有效缓解了事后泄漏和晚期漂移问题，证明了将特权信息解耦为局部兼容锚点与受控残差的策略在复杂推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has demonstrated strong empirical gains in enhancing complex reasoning in LLMs by aligning a student model with a teacher's predictive distribution over the student's own trajectories. An emerging variant, Privileged OPD, further strengthens this paradigm by employing a self-teacher model augmented with privileged information, such as oracle traces, to mitigate teacher-student capacity gaps while providing dense, answer-directed supervision. However, current methods treat privileged information as a monolithic imitation target, failing to disentangle locally reachable reasoning steps from future-conditioned oracle signals. Consequently, the student is encouraged to match a hindsight-biased distribution that often falls outside its local predictive support. This reachability mismatch incentivizes the student model to skip valid intermediate reasoning in favor of locally unsupported shortcuts. To resolve this, we introduce Anchored Residual On-Policy Distillation (AR-OPD), a dual-view framework that disentangles privileged supervision. Rather than enforcing strict full-view imitation, AR-OPD establishes a locally compatible anchor using a partially privileged teacher, isolating and injecting oracle foresight as a controlled residual to provide destination-directed guidance. Across diverse reasoning tasks, AR-OPD outperforms full privileged OPD by 2.3 points and SFT by 7.9 points. Crucially, this anchored residual mechanism reduces hindsight leakage by 21.7% and mitigates late-stage drift, yielding up to a 7.2-point advantage on challenging long-horizon trajectories exceeding 768 tokens.

</details>


### [263] [Validation-Stage Combinatorial Fusion Analysis for Imbalanced Credit-Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10393)
*Xiao Han,Chenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测基准上评估了组合融合分析（CFA）的应用价值。通过从七个基分类器中构建480种融合配置，发现基于多样性加权的随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM得分融合（DEF WtScore）表现最佳（AUC-ROC=0.9405），CFA在AUPRC和F1上优于软投票和Stacking。CFA的主要价值不在于结合所有分类器，而在于在验证阶段选择小型互补子集并分配多样性感知权重。CTGAN数据增强实验结果为负面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈检测任务中欺诈交易稀少、成本高且分布不均，而强梯度提升树模型已在结构化交易数据上表现良好，因此需要探究组合融合分析（CFA）是否能在已有强基线基础上进一步带来性能提升。

Method: 采用无数据泄漏的60/20/20训练/验证/测试划分，从七个基分类器中构建480种融合配置，评估多种融合规则。进行CTGAN合成数据增强实验。

Result: 多样性加权得分融合（DEF WtScore）在测试集上取得AUC-ROC=0.9405、AUPRC=0.6699、F1=0.6373。Bootstrap置信区间显示三项指标均显著优于最强单模型。CFA在AUPRC和F1上优于软投票和Stacking。CTGAN合成欺诈样本反而降低了模型性能。

Conclusion: CFA的主要价值不在于融合所有分类器，而在于验证阶段选择小型互补子集并分配多样性感知权重；CTGAN数据增强在此场景下无效。

Abstract: Credit-card fraud detection is difficult because fraudulent transactions are rare, costly, and unevenly distributed. Strong gradient-boosted tree models already perform well on structured transaction data, so the value of another fusion method is not obvious. This paper examines whether Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (CFA), which searches over model subsets and rank-score fusion rules, can still add value on the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark. Using a leakage-free 60/20/20 train/validation/test protocol, we evaluate 480 fusion configurations built from seven base classifiers. The best test-set result comes from diversity-weighted score fusion of Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM (DEF WtScore), with AUC-ROC = 0.9405, AUPRC = 0.6699, and F1 = 0.6373. Bootstrap confidence intervals from 1,000 resamples show that the gains over the strongest single model exclude zero for all three metrics. CFA matches soft voting on AUC-ROC, improves AUPRC and F1, and outperforms stacking in this setting. A CTGAN augmentation experiment gives a negative result: synthetic fraud samples degrade both individual models and CFA. Overall, CFA is most useful here not as a way to combine every classifier, but as a validation-stage method for choosing a small, complementary subset and assigning diversity-aware weights.

</details>


### [264] [FOGO: Forgetting-aware Orthogonalization Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10406)
*Toan Nguyen,Yang Liu,Trung Le,Celso de Melo,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出遗忘是通用的优化现象，不仅在持续学习中存在，标准训练中也会发生。FOGO优化器通过谱正交化动量更新和紧凑码本记忆来解决梯度干扰，在多种任务上优于Adam和Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明遗忘不仅局限于持续学习，而是标准训练中普遍存在的优化现象——主导的小批量梯度会压制稀有但有用的更新方向，导致每一步都出现短期遗忘；当这类知识不再被 revisit 时，累积为长期遗忘。

Method: 提出FOGO优化器：(1) 谱正交化动量更新，防止主导方向垄断优化；(2) 基于随机投影构建紧凑码本记忆，存储代表性历史方向，且低维空间中成对距离可证明保持；(3) 每一步通过轻量正交校正解决当前更新与存储方向间的冲突，并通过近端步骤回升，无数据存储开销。

Result: 在类不平衡分类、领域/类别偏移下的持续视觉学习、LLaVA-7B持续微调、GPT-2预训练中，FOGO一致提升收敛速度和知识保持能力，优于Adam和Muon。

Conclusion: 遗忘是通用优化现象，FOGO通过正交化动量与码本记忆能同时在标准训练和持续学习中缓解梯度干扰，提升优化效果。

Abstract: We argue that forgetting is not confined to continual learning but is a general optimization phenomenon: during standard training, dominant mini-batch gradients suppress rare but useful update directions, causing short-term forgetting at every step. When such knowledge is never revisited, these losses compound into long-term forgetting-the classical failure mode of continual learning. We introduce FOGO, a scalable optimizer that continuously detects and resolves gradient interference across both regimes. FOGO spectrally orthogonalizes momentum updates to prevent dominant directions from monopolizing optimization, then stores representative past directions in a compact codebook memory built on random projection, where pairwise distances are provably preserved in low-dimensional space. At each step, conflicts between the current update and stored directions are resolved via lightweight orthogonal correction and lifted back through a proximal step, with minimal overhead and no data storage. Across class-imbalanced classification, continual visual learning under domain and class shifts, continual fine-tuning of LLaVA-7B, and GPT-2 pretraining, FOGO consistently improves convergence and knowledge retention, outperforming Adam and Muon.

</details>


### [265] [A Comprehensive Inference-Time Augmentation Framework in Physiological Signals: Application to PPG-Based AF Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10410)
*Davood Fattahi,Runze Yan,Saurabh Kataria,Zhaoliang Chen,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的推理时数据增强(ITA)框架，整合13种增强方法并通过贝叶斯优化超参数，在PPG信号房颤检测任务上显著提升了模型性能( AUROC最高提升8.5%)，为无法重新训练的部署场景提供了一种实用的模型无关鲁棒性提升方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理信号分类模型在实际部署中面临传感器噪声、运动伪影和分布偏移等挑战，而现有ITA方法在生理信号领域的应用范围狭窄，仅依赖有限的增强方法和固定的未优化参数，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出统一的ITA框架，包含13种覆盖时域、幅域、频域和伪影注入四种变换类型的增强方法，并使用贝叶斯优化对超参数进行优化。在5个数据集、400多名患者、约9800小时记录的PPG信号上，使用GPT-PPG和ResNet两种模型评估房颤检测性能。

Result: 标准ITA持续提升AUROC（GPT-PPG最高8.5%，ResNet最高0.7%）和AUPRC（GPT-PPG最高10.6%，ResNet最高0.8%）。选择性ITA进一步在非AF数据集上将平均FPR降低最多4.4%（GPT-PPG）和1.3%（ResNet）。

Conclusion: ITA是一种实用且模型无关的方法，能够有效提升PPG房颤分类在无法重新训练的部署场景下的可靠性，且具有更广泛的生理信号分析适用性。

Abstract: Objective: Accurate classification of physiological signals in real-world deployments is challenged by sensor noise, motion artifacts, and distribution shifts between training and deployment data. Inference-time augmentation (ITA), which applies augmentations during inference rather than retraining, offers a simple, model-agnostic mechanism to improve robustness. However, ITA application to physiological signals has remained narrow in scope, relying on limited augmentation methods with fixed, unoptimized parameters. This work proposes a unified ITA framework to address that gap.
  Approach: The framework incorporates 13 augmentation methods spanning time-domain, amplitude-domain, frequency-domain, and artifact-injection transformations, with hyperparameters optimized via Bayesian optimization. We evaluate on atrial fibrillation (AF) detection from 30-second PPG signals using GPT-PPG and ResNet across five datasets comprising more than 400 patients and ${\sim}$9,800 hours of recording.
  Main results: Standard ITA consistently improved AUROC (up to 8.5% for GPT-PPG and 0.7% for ResNet) and AUPRC (up to 10.6% for GPT-PPG and 0.8% for ResNet). Selective ITA further reduced average FPR by up to 4.4% (GPT-PPG) and 1.3% (ResNet) on non-AF datasets.
  Significance: These findings establish ITA as a practical, model-agnostic approach for improving PPG-based AF classification reliability in deployment settings where retraining is not feasible, with broader applicability to physiological signal analysis.

</details>


### [266] [Mitigating Bias in Low-SNR Financial Reinforcement Learning via Quantum Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10448)
*Zeyu Liu,Xuanzhi Feng,Sing Kwong Lai,Yuanchen Gao,Xiaoyi Pang,Hualei Zhang,Jingcai Guo,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FPQC-SAC，一种在Actor-Critic网络前嵌入紧凑参数化量子电路（PQC）的SAC变体，用于金融市场的低信噪比场景，通过表示层面约束特征传播来避免"金融熵陷阱"，在真实投资组合管理任务中实现累计收益66.89%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场是典型的低信噪比环境，离线策略的最大熵方法（如SAC）容易因噪声状态表示产生不可靠的Q值估计，经自举放大形成"金融熵陷阱"失效模式，需要更鲁棒的表示学习方法。

Method: 在Actor和Critic网络前放置一个紧凑且有界的参数化量子电路（PQC），在表示层约束特征传播，而非过滤原始输入或对自举后的Q值进行正则化；利用可训练的量子纠缠保留灵活的跨资产交互。

Result: 在真实投资组合管理任务中，FPQC-SAC相比标准无约束SAC实现累计收益66.89%的相对提升，并优于最优连续控制深度强化学习基线约27%。

Conclusion: FPQC-SAC通过量子电路在表示层的特征约束有效缓解了低信噪比环境下SAC的"金融熵陷阱"问题，显著提升了金融市场的样本外稳定性和累计收益。

Abstract: The financial market is a typical low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) setting, which often destabilizes off-policy maximum-entropy methods like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Specifically, noisy state representations may produce unreliable Q-value estimates, and bootstrapping amplifies these errors, forming a failure mode we call the "Financial Entropy Trap". In this paper, we propose FPQC-SAC, an efficient and plug-and-play SAC variant that places a compact and bounded Parameterized Quantum Circuit (PQC) before the actor and critic networks to constrain feature propagation at the representation level, rather than filtering raw inputs or regularizing Q-values after bootstrapping. Notably, FPQC-SAC reduces the impact of extreme market fluctuations on Bellman target estimation, while trainable quantum entanglement preserves flexible cross-asset interactions. Empirical evaluations on real-world portfolio management tasks demonstrate that FPQC-SAC substantially enhances out-of-sample stability and cumulative returns by achieving a 66.89% relative gain in cumulative return over standard unconstrained SAC and outperforms the best continuous-control deep reinforcement learning baseline by approximately 27%. Open-source code is available at https://github.com/ZeyuLIU-UST/FPQC-SAC-main.

</details>


### [267] [UPLOTS: A Unified Pretrained Language Model for Constrained Time-series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10466)
*Du Yin,Hao Xue,Jinliang Deng,Yang Yang,Shuang Ao,Arian Prabowo,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UPLOTS提出了一种统一的、基于提示引导的语言模型框架，用于跨领域的有约束时间序列生成，通过单预训练Transformer骨干网络和动态多数据集损失重加权，实现可泛化的条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成方法通常为每个数据集单独设计或训练模型，导致可扩展性差且无法跨领域共享时序结构信息。

Method: 提出UPLOTS框架，使用单个预训练Transformer骨干网络，结合可学习的约束提示（constraint prompts）实现按需生成；核心创新包括动态多数据集损失重加权和提示到模式映射（prompt-to-pattern mapping），使模型在训练时内化多种时序结构并在推理时条件生成。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集和多种约束设置（峰值周期、日历、负载水平、波动模式）上进行了评估；额外的留出约束组合实验和下游预测实验表明UPLOTS能够泛化到原始峰值模式设置之外，并在稀缺真实数据场景下有效改进数据增强效果。

Conclusion: UPLOTS证明了单一统一框架能够替代多个任务特定模型，实现跨域有约束时间序列生成的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: In time-series generation, existing approaches typically handcraft ortrain a separate model for each dataset, which hinders their scalability and fails to leverage shared temporal structures across domains. To address this fragmentation, we propose UPLOTS, a Unified, Prompt-guided Language model framework fOr constrained Time-Series Generation across diverse domains. Instead of building task-specific models, UPLOTS leverages a single pre-trained transformer backbone guided by learned constraint prompts, enabling on-demand generation with precise pattern control. One key innovation is our dynamic multi-dataset loss re-weighting and prompt-to-pattern mapping, which allows UPLOTS to internalize diverse temporal structures during training and conditionally generate them at inference. We evaluate UPLOTS on four real-world benchmarks and multiple constraint settings, including peak-period, calendar, load-level, and volatility patterns. Additional held-out constraint-combination and downstream forecasting experiments further demonstrate that UPLOTS generalizes beyond the original peak-pattern setting and improves data augmentation under scarce real-data regimes. Our code and baselines are available at anonymous github repo: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UPLOTS-6C36.

</details>


### [268] [Stop Early, Spend Less: Hidden-State Probes as a Practical Recipe for Streaming Moderation of LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10487)
*Huizhen Shu,Xuying Li,Piao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在大型语言模型的隐藏状态上训练轻量级token级探针，实现无需额外前向传播的逐token安全检测，可将后置审核的计算开销降低数个数量级，并支持流式场景下提前中断不安全输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后置安全审核方案需要额外的前向推理成本，且只能在生成完成后检测违规；本文观察到审核所需信号已存在于模型隐藏状态中，旨在利用该信号实现零额外计算开销的在线安全过滤。

Method: 在LLM中间层的隐藏状态上训练轻量级线性探针，为每个token生成安全分数；探针复用生成器的激活值，无需额外前向传播；通过聚合策略（如最大/平均）得到序列级安全判定，并支持在解码循环内以亚毫秒级延迟进行逐token干预。

Result: 单个中间层的探针可恢复强审核模型的大部分决策；计算开销比后置和流式审核模型低数个数量级；探针的线性成分对应残差空间中的安全方向，可用于检测和激活干预。

Conclusion: 基于隐藏状态的探针是一种低延迟、低成本的安全过滤替代方案，可在不增加推理开销的前提下实现连续token级安全监控，适用于对延迟敏感的用户交互场景。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in user-facing systems requires efficient output safety filtering. Existing approaches typically rely on a separate moderation model applied after generation, which doubles inference cost and only detects violations after generation completes. We observe that the signal needed for moderation is already present in the model hidden states. Based on this, we train lightweight token-level probes that operate directly on internal activations, producing per-token safety scores that can be aggregated for both offline evaluation and online intervention. The probe reuses activations from the generator and requires no additional forward pass, enabling sub millisecond per-token safety checks inside the decoding loop. A probe applied to a single mid layer recovers most decisions of a strong guard model, acting as a low cost surrogate optimized for latency rather than accuracy. In streaming settings, it can halt or modify unsafe outputs before they are fully generated, replacing end of sequence moderation with continuous token level monitoring. Compared to post hoc and streaming guard models, our method achieves orders of magnitude lower compute overhead with minimal latency cost. We also provide a practical deployment recipe, including layer selection, aggregation strategy, probing frequency, and triggering thresholds. Finally, we show that the probe linear component corresponds to a direction in residual space, enabling both detection and activation steering at negligible cost.

</details>


### [269] [MoE Enhanced Federated Learning for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10499)
*Zhehao Dai,Xiao Han,Zhaolin Deng,Zijian Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Guojiang Shen,Xiangjie Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoE-FedTP，一种基于轻量级混合专家网络（MoE）的个性化联邦跨城市时空预测框架，通过门控机制动态融合不同源城市的专家网络，在保护隐私的同时实现细粒度城市异质性建模，有效提升数据稀缺城市的交通预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多城市因传感器部署有限和城市发展不均衡面临交通数据稀缺问题，跨城市知识迁移受关注但集中式方法存在隐私风险，现有联邦方法难以应对跨城市显著的时空异质性。

Method: 首先使用时空神经网络提取源城市和目标城市的特征，然后通过部分参数共享从不同源城市派生出多个专家网络，利用门控机制动态融合专家以捕捉多样化的交通动态，实现隐私保护下的细粒度城市异质性建模。

Result: 在四个真实交通数据集上，MoE-FedTP持续优于现有的跨城市和联邦学习基线方法，有效提升了数据稀缺城市的预测准确性。

Conclusion: MoE-FedTP通过轻量级MoE网络与联邦学习结合，成功解决了跨城市时空预测中的隐私保护与异质性建模难题，为数据稀缺城市的交通预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is fundamental to intelligent transportation systems and urban computing, yet many cities continue to suffer from traffic data scarcity due to limited sensor deployment and uneven urban development. Cross-city knowledge transfer has thus attracted increasing attention, enabling data-rich cities to assist data-scarce ones. However, centralized approaches raise privacy concerns, while existing federated methods struggle with pronounced spatiotemporal heterogeneity across cities. To address these challenges, we propose MoE-FedTP, a personalized federated cross-city spatiotemporal prediction framework based on lightweight Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) networks. MoE-FedTP first employs spatiotemporal neural networks to extract features from both source and target cities, then introduces a set of expert networks derived from different source cities through partial parameter sharing. A gating mechanism dynamically fuses the experts to capture diverse traffic dynamics, achieving fine-grained modeling of urban heterogeneity while preserving privacy. Experiments on four real-world traffic datasets show that MoE-FedTP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city and federated learning baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing prediction accuracy for data-scarce cities.

</details>


### [270] [Machine Learning Methods for Studying Latent Neural Activity Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10530)
*Shufeng Kong,Fumei Deng,Xinyi Dong,Caihua Liu,Weiwei Chen,Yingheng Wang,Daniel Cao,Azahara Oliva,Antonio Fernandez-Ruiz,Carla Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了从早期状态空间模型到深度生成模型的潜变量模型（LVM）在神经数据分析中的发展轨迹，涵盖单区域潜动态、多区域通信和行为对齐建模三大领域，并讨论了大规模神经基础模型、基准测试及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑记录技术的最新发展推动了对能够解码大规模神经元群体潜在结构的机器学习工具的需求，亟需系统梳理潜变量模型在该领域的发展脉络。

Method: 按三个相关领域组织文献：单区域潜动态（线性动态系统→RNN→神经ODE）、多区域通信（概率/子空间方法研究跨脑区信息传递及突触延迟）、行为对齐建模（有监督/对比学习分离任务相关与内在状态的神经活动）；并纳入基于Transformer和扩散模型的大规模预训练基础模型。

Result: 系统梳理了LVM从经典到深度生成模型的演进路径，提出了三大研究领域的分类框架，并指出大规模预训练模型在多主体泛化中的优势。

Conclusion: 需要建立统一的基准和评估标准，解决因果链接识别、通信方向性等开放挑战，以桥接可解释脑动态与可靠神经解码。

Abstract: Recent developments in brain recording are driving a demand for machine learning tools capable of decoding the latent structure of large populations of neurons. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive survey that outlines the trajectory of Latent Variable Models (LVMs) from early state-space models to more recent deep generative models. We organize the literature into three closely related domains: (1) Single-Region Latent Dynamics, which includes models such as linear dynamical systems to more complex dynamics represented by Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) and Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs); (2) Multi-Region Communication, which employs probabilistic as well as subspace methods to study how information is transferred across different brain areas considering synaptic propagation delays and network connectivity; and (3) Behavior-Aligned Modeling, which seeks to disentangle neural activity related to task performance from other internal states via supervised or contrastive learning. This survey also includes large-scale neural foundation models, such as Transformers and diffusion models, that rely on large-scale pre-training for optimal performance across subjects. Finally, we conclude and discuss benchmarks, evaluation criteria, and open challenges, such as the ability to identify causal links or directionality of communication, to facilitate future research for bridging interpretable brain dynamics with reliable neural decoding.

</details>


### [271] [Flexible Flows for Biological Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10543)
*Yogesh Verma,Dani Korpela,Harri Lähdesmäki,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化耦合、基于编辑的潜变量参数化以及潜分类器无关引导的离散流匹配方法，用于功能性生物序列生成，在多种任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散流匹配方法使用生物学上无意义的耦合，且对可变长度序列生成和细粒度控制的支持有限。

Method: 1) 结构化耦合：编码领域特定的序列元素偏好，将源分布偏向合理区域；2) 潜变量编辑速率参数化：通过共享全局潜变量条件化的编辑操作建模变长序列生成；3) 潜分类器无关引导：在连续潜空间中实现连贯的条件生成；4) Dirichlet先验温度缩放：实现测试时的编辑操作控制。

Result: 在密度估计、无条件/条件DNA序列生成以及肽序列生成等任务上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在不修改流目标或训练过程的前提下，通过结构化的先验设计和潜空间控制，显著提升了离散生物序列生成的灵活性和可控性。

Abstract: Designing functional biological sequences requires navigating vast discrete spaces under strict evolutionary and biophysical constraints. Discrete Flow Matching (DFM) offers a generative framework over such spaces, but existing approaches rely on biologically uninformative couplings and offer limited flexibility for variable-length sequence generation and fine-grained control. We propose a structured coupling that encodes domain-specific preferences among sequence elements, biasing the source distribution toward plausible regions without modifying the flow objective or training procedure. Building on this, we introduce a latent edit-based rate parameterization that models variable-length generation via edit operations conditioned on a shared global latent, akin to a latent variable model, while remaining tractable. We further introduce a latent classifier-free guidance mechanism that steers generation coherently in continuous latent space, along with Dirichlet-prior temperature scaling for test-time control over edit operations. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse biological sequence tasks, including density estimation, unconditional and conditional DNA sequence generation, and peptide sequence generation.

</details>


### [272] [Convergence of Monte Carlo Optimistic Policy Iteration: Beyond Uniform State-Action Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10580)
*Octave Oliviers,Glenn Vinnicombe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格放宽了蒙特卡洛乐观策略迭代（MC-O-PI）收敛至最优性的条件，证明即使更新仅在每个状态的动作上均匀（而非整个状态-动作空间），算法仍能收敛到最优策略。通过均值场动力学和改进的锁入论证，解决了不同状态更新频率不一致时的收敛难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MC-O-PI的渐近行为长期悬而未决；实际环境中模型未知时，已知的收敛条件（要求整状态-动作空间均匀初始化）不切实际，亟需更宽松且可行的条件。

Method: 1. 证明当更新仅在每个状态内部动作上均匀时，MC-O-PI的均值场动力学产生单调改进的策略；2. 通过扩展组合稳定性-ODE方法的锁入论证，证明噪声无法持续阻止这种改进。

Result: 即使在状态以任意频率初始化、仅需每个状态内动作更新均匀的条件下，初始访问MC-O-PI仍能收敛到最优策略。

Conclusion: 放宽了经典Tsitsiklis分析中关于状态均匀更新的关键假设，为乐观策略迭代算法提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: The asymptotic behaviour of Monte Carlo optimistic policy iteration (MC-O-PI) is a long-standing open question. When the model of the environment is unknown, as is common in practice, the only known condition that guarantees convergence to optimality is impractical. In its canonical form, this condition requires that the episodes used for policy evaluation be initialised uniformly over the entire state-action space. This paper strictly relaxes that requirement. Specifically, we prove that initial-visit MC-O-PI converges to optimality even when updates are uniform only over the actions within each state. This allows episodes to start in different states at arbitrary frequencies; a realistic implementation when the state space is large or unknown but the action space in each state is manageable. The proof departs from the classical analysis of Tsitsiklis whose central commutativity argument no longer applies when states are updated at different frequencies. Instead, we first show that the mean-field dynamics of MC-O-PI generate monotonically improving policies when updates are uniform over the actions in each state, and then prove that noise cannot consistently prevent this improvement by extending the lock-in argument of the combined stability-ODE method. This approach suggests a new way to study optimistic policy-iteration algorithms in general.

</details>


### [273] [Drawing with Strangers: Population Scaling Drives Zero-Shot Mutual Intelligibility in Emergent Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10582)
*Jooyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从完全不相关社区独立训练的两个智能体群体之间能否实现零样本相互理解（ZMI）。以涂鸦式绘画作为视觉基础模态，发现扩大训练群体规模能显著提升ZMI：群体内交流变异性增加（防止过度适应），群体间变异性减少（结构趋同）。这种趋同源于对视觉感知的锚定——智能体越来越多地依赖目标图像的客观视觉相似性进行交流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往紧急通信中的泛化研究主要关注新输入或新语言结构，但来自完全不同社区（无共同训练经历）的智能体之间能否成功通信这一问题尚未被探索。

Method: 将零样本相互理解（ZMI）形式化定义为两个独立训练群体之间无需事先暴露即可成功通信的能力；以紧急涂鸦（emergent sketching）——智能体通过绘制笔画集合进行交流——作为视觉基础模态，通过扩大训练群体规模来观察ZMI的变化。

Result: 扩大训练群体规模显著提升ZMI；群体内交流变异性增加（防止同质化过度适应），群体间变异性降低（结构性趋同）；这种趋同通过感知锚定实现——规模扩大后智能体更依赖目标图像的客观视觉相似性。

Conclusion: ZMI是紧急通信中一个独立的泛化维度；该工作为构建社交可互操作的人工智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generalization in emergent communication has largely focused on novel inputs or linguistic structures, yet the capacity for agents to communicate with strangers from strictly disjoint communities remains relatively unexplored. In this work, we formalize this capability as \textit{zero-shot mutual intelligibility (ZMI)}: successful communication between independently trained populations without prior exposure. Leveraging emergent sketching -- in which agents communicate through sets of drawn strokes -- as a visually grounded modality, we find that scaling the training population substantially improves ZMI across independent groups. Crucially, as we scale the population size, in-group communicative variation increases, preventing co-adaptation into homogeneity. Simultaneously, cross-group variation decreases, indicating a structural convergence toward a certain type of universality. Further analysis reveals that this universality is achieved through perceptual grounding: scaled populations increasingly anchor their emergent sketches on the objective visual resemblance of the target images. Together, these results position ZMI as a distinct axis of generalization in emergent communication and suggest a route toward socially interoperable artificial agents.

</details>


### [274] [Towards Diverse Scientific Hypothesis Search with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10587)
*Haorui Wang,Parshin Shojaee,Kazem Meidani,Kunyang Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Teresa Head-Gordon,Jiajun He,Chandan K. Reddy,Chao Zhang,Yuanqi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受并行回火算法启发的进化框架，将假设搜索建模为采样问题，通过多温度级别搜索和信息交换，在不破坏收敛性的前提下提升假设生成的质量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化搜索方法在假设生成中偏重优化而忽视探索，导致多样性崩溃，而科学发现场景下需要一组高质量且多样化的候选假设来对冲下游验证的不确定性。

Method: 将假设搜索形式化为固定验证预算下的采样问题，提出受并行回火算法启发的进化框架，在多个温度级别下搜索假设，并通过跨温度的信息交换实现探索与收敛的平衡。

Result: 在分子发现、方程发现和算法发现等任务中，在相同验证预算下，该方法一致地提升了假设的质量和多样性，且生成的候选在更昂贵的下游计算验证中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将假设搜索重新定义为采样问题，并借鉴并行回火的多温度策略，能够有效解决进化搜索中的多样性崩溃问题，在科学发现场景中同时提升假设质量与多样性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are on the rise for accelerating scientific discovery, most recently in advanced tasks such as generating valid scientific hypotheses. Yet in many discovery settings, the goal is not to identify a single best hypothesis since validation can be noisy and expensive, and scientists benefit from a set of high-quality alternative hypotheses that hedge against downstream uncertainty for the best solutions. Nevertheless, commonly used evolutionary search recipes tend to prioritize optimization over exploration in hypothesis generation, and the resulting selection pressure during the search process leads to diversity collapse. Motivated by these limitations, we formulate hypothesis search as a sampling problem, where the objective is to efficiently produce diverse, high-quality hypotheses under a fixed validation budget. Building on this perspective, we propose \ours, an evolutionary framework inspired by the classical parallel tempering algorithm that searches hypotheses at multiple temperature levels and enables principled information exchange across temperatures to improve exploration without disrupting convergence. Across domains including molecular discovery, equation discovery, and algorithm discovery, our approach consistently improves both hypothesis quality and diversity under the same validation budget, and produces candidates that remain robust under more expensive downstream computational validations.

</details>


### [275] [Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting for Alleviating Over-smoothing in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10592)
*Xingyu Zhang,Jingyao Wang,Xin Yu,Zeen Song,Jianqi Zhang,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting (DGF)框架，通过显式建模多模态条件预测分布和Dirichlet引导的层次采样机制，解决了时间序列预测中的过平滑问题，在保持预测准确性的同时提升了动态一致性和模态多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中的过平滑问题导致预测结果丢失尖峰变化、振荡、转折点和机制转换等关键动态特征，尤其当未来动态呈现多模态分布时，现有方法倾向于将多个合理未来模式弱化、合并或平均化。

Method: 提出Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting (DGF)框架：1) 显式建模多个模态条件预测分布及选择概率的不确定性；2) 采用Dirichlet引导的层次采样机制；3) 基于奖励的优化策略，鼓励预测结果的准确性、动态一致性和模态区分性。

Result: 在真实世界预测基准上的大量实验表明，DGF在减少过平滑的同时，提升了预测准确性、多样性和动态一致性。

Conclusion: 从潜在动态模式压缩的角度重新审视了过平滑问题，DGF通过多模态显式建模有效保留了预测中的关键动态特征，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often suffers from over-smoothing, especially when future dynamics are multi-modal. Forecasts may follow the coarse trend of the observed future, but fail to preserve sharp changes, oscillations, turning points, and regime transitions that define plausible dynamic evolution. In this work, we revisit over-smoothing from the perspective of latent dynamical mode compression: under partial observation and single-realization supervision, multiple plausible future modes can be weakened, merged, or averaged during forecasting. Based on this view, we propose Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting (DGF), a mode-preserving forecasting framework that explicitly models multiple mode-conditioned predictive distributions and uncertainty over their selection probabilities. DGF uses a Dirichlet-guided hierarchical sampling mechanism and reward-based optimization to encourage forecasts that are accurate, dynamically consistent, and mode-distinct. Extensive experiments on real-world forecasting benchmarks show that DGF reduces over-smoothing while improving forecasting accuracy, diversity, and dynamical consistency.

</details>


### [276] [Embedding Hybrid Systems into Continuous Latent Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10596)
*Sangli Teng,Hang Liu,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明高维混合系统可嵌入到其两倍维度以上的欧几里得空间中，实现连续向量场表示，并据此提出一种隐空间神经常微分方程方法，通过潜在空间和状态空间的一致性损失有效学习混合系统的流，实验表明该方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合系统本质上是离散的，缺乏连续表示形式，这不利于可微优化。本文试图寻找一种连续的表示方法，使得混合系统可以嵌入到更高维的连续系统中，从而支持基于梯度的优化。

Method: 首先证明嵌入存在性定理：当m>2n时，n维混合系统可嵌入到m维欧几里得空间的连续向量场中。在此基础上，提出隐空间神经常微分方程（Latent Neural ODE），通过在潜在空间和状态空间同时施加一致性损失来学习混合系统的流。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在学习不同几何结构的混合系统时，仅从时间序列数据出发，即显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 混合系统虽然本身是不连续的，但可以在更高维空间中获得连续的表示，这种表示可用于可微优化，且通过隐空间神经常微分方程可以有效学习。

Abstract: This work proves that an $n$-dimensional hybrid system can be embedded into an $m$-dimensional Euclidean space equipped with a continuous vector field on its embedded image whenever $m>2n$. This result suggests that an intrinsically discontinuous hybrid system generically admits a continuous extrinsic representation that is well-posed for differentiable optimization. Building on this existence theorem, we show that a latent Neural ODE with consistency loss in both the latent and state space can accurately recover the flow of hybrid systems. Extensive experiments suggest the proposed method outperforms the existing method in learning hybrid systems with varying geometries from only time series data.

</details>


### [277] [Geometry-Aware Reinforcement Learning for 2D Irregular Nesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10611)
*Auguste Lehuger,Guillaume Henon-Just*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Polygons Transformer (PoT)架构，结合几何感知神经编码器和强化学习训练框架，用于解决2D不规则嵌套问题。实验表明，该方法在面积利用率上与最先进的启发式求解器Sparrow性能相当，证明了强化学习能够有效发现和利用几何先验知识进行精确空间任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式求解器存在根本性局限：它们对多边形几何结构"不可见"，依赖带引导的暴力搜索在连续放置空间中导航，缺乏几何指导。作者认为强化学习能够克服这一瓶颈，通过将优化策略与几何感知神经编码器配对，智能体可以自动从数据中发现丰富的几何先验。

Method: 提出了Polygons Transformer (PoT)架构，一种编码2D连续矢量几何同时允许跨多边形注意力的新型网络结构；将其与组合优化强化学习(CORL)训练框架耦合以寻找最优解；并发布了基于复杂地理轮廓的训练数据集和评估基准。

Result: 训练后的智能体在面积利用率上达到了与最先进启发式求解器Sparrow高度竞争的性能水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够成功发现并利用几何感知能力来完成精确的空间任务，为2D不规则嵌套问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Traditional heuristic solvers for the 2D irregular nesting problem share a fundamental limitation: they are blind to polygon geometry, relying on guided brute-force to navigate the continuous placement space with minimal geometrical guidance. In this paper, we argue that Reinforcement Learning is uniquely positioned to overcome this bottleneck. By pairing an optimization policy with a geometry-aware neural encoder, an agent can automatically discover rich geometric priors directly from data, utilizing these learned intuitions to strategically guide exploration. To realize this, we introduce the Polygons Transformer (PoT), a novel architecture that encodes 2D continuous vector geometries while allowing cross-polygons attention. We couple this novel architecture with a Combinatorial Optimization Reinforcement Learning (CORL) training framework to find optimal solutions. To support this paradigm, we release an open-source training dataset derived from complex geographic contours alongside a dedicated evaluation benchmark. Our empirical validation demonstrates that our trained agent achieves area utilization performance highly competitive with Sparrow, the state-of-the-art heuristic solver, proving that reinforcement learning can successfully discover and exploit geometric awareness for precise spatial tasks.

</details>


### [278] [Fast and Highly Expressive Policy Learning for Offline Reinforcement Learning via Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10613)
*Thanh Nguyen,Tri Ton,Hongbin Choe,Tung M. Luu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning (BFQ)框架，通过分治策略学习流路径上的位移向量，实现单步动作生成，无需多步去噪、辅助网络或蒸馏，在D4RL基准上显著降低计算成本的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的Q-learning面临多步去噪导致的训练和推理计算昂贵且脆弱的问题，而现有的加速方法（引入辅助网络、策略蒸馏或多阶段训练）往往牺牲了简洁性、稳定性或性能。

Method: 采用分治策略处理流路径上的位移向量：先学习可从Flow Matching边际速度准确估计的短程位移分量，再通过bootstrap方式组合这些分量，直接学习从噪声到动作的单步映射，无需多步去噪。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，BFQ在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算开销，证明单步动作生成足以实现高性能的离线强化学习。

Conclusion: BFQ通过消除多步去噪过程，实现了更快速、更简单、更鲁棒的学习流程，且无需辅助网络或蒸馏即可达到甚至超越多步扩散基线的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Q-learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for offline reinforcement learning, but its reliance on multi-step denoising makes both training and inference computationally expensive and brittle. Recent efforts to accelerate diffusion Q-learning toward single-step action generation typically introduce auxiliary networks, policy distillation, or multi-phase training, which frequently compromise simplicity, stability, or performance. To address these limitations, we introduce Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning (BFQ), a novel framework that enables accurate single-step action generation during both training and inference, without auxiliary networks or distillation procedures. BFQ adopts a divide-and-conquer view of the displacement vector along the flow path: it begins by learning short-range displacements that can be accurately estimated from the Flow Matching marginal velocity, and bootstraps these components to directly learn a noise-to-action mapping in a single step. This formulation eliminates multi-step denoising, resulting in a learning procedure that is substantially faster, simpler, and more robust. Extensive D4RL evaluations show that BFQ improves performance while significantly reducing computational cost compared to multi-step diffusion baselines, demonstrating that single-step action generation suffices for high-performance offline Reinforcement Learning.

</details>


### [279] [Is Fairness Truly Fair? Towards Reliable Lipschitz Fairness in Multi-Task Learning via Fixed-\texorpdfstring{$δ$}{delta} Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10632)
*Junbo Ding,Xin Zang,Chenchen Pan,Donghao Song,Jiaxin Zhu,Danhuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLiF框架，解决多任务学习中Lipschitz个体公平性评估时的阈值混淆问题，通过固定δ审计和违规率反馈控制实现可比较的公平性评估与正则化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多任务学习中，现有Lipschitz个体公平性评估因各模型表示尺度不同而导致阈值混淆——不同算法在语义不同的阈值下被比较，使得公平性排名不可靠。

Method: 提出ReLiF框架：1）评估阶段使用共享参考阈值（固定δ）进行可比较审计；2）训练阶段通过违规率反馈控制器保持Lipschitz代理项活跃但不过度主导随机训练；3）开发了阈值漂移分析、参考阈值选择理论以及Huberized训练代理与其非平滑正间隔版本的关系分析。

Result: 在临床时间序列基准和NYUv2密集预测任务上：固定δ审计揭示了方法依赖阈值可能掩盖的效用-公平性权衡；在NYUv2+ResNet50上，ReLiF在共享固定阈值下显著降低对齐偏差同时保持竞争性效用；在临床基准上实现可控的公平正则化权衡，同时固定δ审计揭示任务平衡基线有时能达到更低偏差。

Conclusion: 支持固定δ审计作为多任务学习中评估Lipschitz公平性的语义一致协议。

Abstract: Lipschitz-style individual fairness formalizes the idea that semantically similar examples should receive similar predictions, but its evaluation in multi-task learning (MTL) can be confounded by method-induced representation scales. This paper identifies threshold confounding: when the auditing tolerance is derived from each model's own representation distances, different algorithms are compared under different semantic thresholds. A threshold-drift analysis further shows how Bias rankings can change and identifies sufficient conditions for ranking preservation.
  We propose \textbf{ReLiF}, a reliability-aware framework that separates evaluation-time fixed-$δ$ auditing from training-time controlled regularization. ReLiF uses a shared reference tolerance for comparable auditing and a violation-rate feedback controller to keep the Lipschitz surrogate active without letting it dominate stochastic training. This work also develops supporting analysis for threshold drift, reference-tolerance selection, and the relationship between the huberized training surrogate and its unsmoothed positive-margin counterpart.
  Experiments on clinical time-series benchmarks and NYUv2 (NYU Depth V2) dense prediction show that fixed-$δ$ auditing exposes utility--fairness trade-offs that method-dependent thresholds can obscure. On NYUv2 with a ResNet50 backbone, ReLiF achieves competitive utility while substantially reducing aligned bias under shared fixed thresholds. On clinical benchmarks, ReLiF yields controlled fairness-regularized trade-offs, while fixed-$δ$ auditing reveals that task-balancing baselines can sometimes achieve lower bias and that genuine utility--fairness trade-offs persist. These results support fixed-$δ$ auditing as a semantically consistent protocol for evaluating Lipschitz fairness in MTL.

</details>


### [280] [PhysMetrics.Weather: An Evaluation Framework for Physical Consistency in ML Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10642)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Timo Maier,Axel Lauer,Veronika Eyring,Pierre Gentine,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，从守恒性、谱特性和动力学三个维度量化机器学习天气预测模型的物理真实性，以弥补传统像素级误差指标无法保证物理一致性的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习天气预测模型虽然计算效率高，但本质上是数据驱动的，且仅用RMSE等像素级误差指标评估，无法保证预测结果符合已知物理定律，因此需要一套能够量化物理真实性的评估工具。

Method: 构建PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，包含三类物理度量指标：守恒性指标（如质量、能量守恒）、谱特性指标（如能量谱分布）和动力学指标（如地转平衡等物理约束），对MLWP模型的输出进行多维度物理一致性检验。

Result: 该框架能够量化MLWP模型的物理真实性，指导物理信息架构的研发，并帮助评估模型是否适合业务化运行。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: PhysMetrics.Weather填补了MLWP模型物理一致性评估的空白，为提升模型可靠性和推动物理-informed架构发展提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Machine learning weather prediction (MLWP) models have achieved impressive forecasting performance at a small fraction of the computational costs required for traditional physics-based methods. However, they are primarily (1) data-driven and (2) evaluated using pixel-wide error metrics (e.g., RMSE), so there are no guarantees that their forecasts are consistent with known physical laws. We introduce PhysMetrics.Weather, an evaluation framework that assesses the physical realism of MLWP models across three types of metrics: conservation, spectral, and dynamical. By quantifying physical realism, this tool guides the development of physics-informed architectures and helps evaluate whether MLWP models are reliable for operational use. Our framework is available on Github at https://github.com/Emmakast/PhysMetrics.Weather.

</details>


### [281] [In Defense of Information Leakage in Concept-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10669)
*Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了概念模型中信息泄漏必然有害的传统观点，提出存在"良性泄漏"，并在概念不完整的现实场景下，通过优化训练目标，可以主动利用这种泄漏来提升模型准确性和可干预性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为概念模型中的信息泄漏会导致模型不可解释，作者指出这一观点存在两个问题：一是泄漏导致模型不可解释的证据并不充分；二是在现实世界中概念不完备是常态，此时完全消除泄漏会导致模型不实用。因此需要重新审视泄漏在概念模型中的作用。

Method: 重新构建了概念模型的训练目标，通过优化目标函数使模型能够鼓励并利用"良性泄漏"——即在概念不完备场景下，泄漏有利于提升模型准确性和可干预性的那部分信息。

Result: 证明了存在良性泄漏，并且通过优化重新定义的训练目标，概念模型可以在不牺牲准确性和可干预性的前提下，主动利用这种泄漏。

Conclusion: 概念模型中的信息泄漏并非全然有害，在现实世界概念不完备的约束下，适当保留和利用泄漏反而能构建更准确、更实用的可干预模型。

Abstract: Concept-based models (CMs), deep neural networks that ground their predictions on representations aligned with human-understandable concepts (e.g., "round", "stripes", etc.), have been shown to learn representations that leak concept-irrelevant information. As the traditional narrative goes, this leakage is undesirable and should be eradicated as it leads to uninterpretable models. In this paper, we posit that this conventional view of leakage in CMs is not only ill-posed, as the evidence of how leakage makes a model less interpretable is often inconclusive, but also bound to lead to impractical CMs under common real-world constraints. Specifically, we argue that in real-world settings where concept incompleteness is the norm, some leakage is often necessary for constructing accurate and intervenable CMs. To this end, we propose that there is such a thing as benign leakage and show that, by optimizing a reframing of the typical CM training objective, CMs can encourage and exploit this form of leakage without sacrificing accuracy or intervenability.

</details>


### [282] [One Step Closer to Ground Truth: A Multi-Scale Residual-Aware Representation Learning Pipeline for Predicting Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10678)
*Amrijit Biswas,Mustafa Kamal,Robin Krambroeckers,M. M. Lutfe Elahi,Sifat Momen,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段模型无关框架，将预测与残差学习解耦：基础Transformer首先生成初始预测，专用元校正器动态建模结构化误差模式并迭代修正残差偏差，在8个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段Transformer预测架构存在系统性残差偏差，源于结构不匹配、未建模随机成分或多尺度时间表征不足，而现有方法将残差视为不可约噪声，无法自适应修正结构化误差模式。

Method: 提出两阶段模型无关框架：第一阶段基础Transformer生成初始预测；第二阶段元校正器（meta-corrector）在多变量通道间动态建模结构化误差模式，保持跨变量依赖性，并迭代优化基础Transformer的残差偏差。将该流程形式化为假设空间扩展。

Result: 在8个主流基准数据集上，采用标准评估协议，该方法在MSE和MAE指标上取得显著改进，达到SOTA性能，有效缓解系统性偏差并增强对复杂时间动态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 两阶段残差学习框架通过假设空间扩展解决了单阶段架构的逼近局限性，无需依赖严格假设，能够端到端学习复杂误差动态，提升了Transformer预测模型的实用适用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have emerged as leading paradigms in time-series forecasting in recent years, employing self-attention mechanisms to capture long-range dependencies. Despite their success, these single-stage forecasting architectures exhibit persistent systematic residual biases arising from structural discrepancies, unmodeled stochastic components, or inadequate multi-scale temporal representations. This limitation persists when residuals are treated as irreducible noise, precluding adaptive correction of structured error patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce a two-stage, model-agnostic framework that explicitly decouples forecasting and residual learning into distinct stages of representation learning. A base transformer first generates the initial predictions. Subsequently, a dedicated meta-corrector dynamically models structured error patterns across multivariate channels, preserves cross-variable dependencies, and iteratively refines the residual bias of the base transformer. By formalizing this pipeline as a hypothesis space expansion, our framework addresses approximation limitations inherent in single-stage architectures, removes reliance on restrictive assumptions, and enables end-to-end learning of complex error dynamics. Evaluated on eight popular benchmark datasets using established protocols, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in standard metrics (MSE, MAE). The results demonstrate the framework's ability to mitigate systematic biases and enhance robustness to complex temporal dynamics, advancing the practical applicability of transformer-based forecasting models.

</details>


### [283] [PL-KKT-hPINN: Enforcing Nonlinear Equality Constraints on Neural Networks via Piecewise-Linear Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10682)
*Fateme Mohammad Mohammadi,Hector Budman,Joshua L. Pulsipher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PL-KKT-hPINNs框架，通过分段线性投影严格强制非线性等式约束，在CSTR案例中实现与标准神经网络相当的预测精度，同时大幅降低约束违反，且在低数据场景下具有更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)仅在训练时将物理方程作为软约束，无法保证推理时约束满足，需要一种能严格强制非线性等式约束的方法。

Method: 基于KKT条件的分段线性投影方法，将神经网络输出正交投影到约束可行域，扩展了线性KKT-hPINN框架以处理非线性等式约束。

Result: 在CSTR案例（单输入和双输入）中，PL-KKT-hPINN保持与标准神经网络相当的预测精度，显著降低约束违反；在有限训练样本的低数据场景下，RMSE低于无约束神经网络。

Conclusion: PL-KKT-hPINN为非线性化工系统的代理建模提供了一种计算高效且物理一致的框架。

Abstract: While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have shown strong potential for process modeling, physical equations are only enforced as soft constraints during training, and thus, they do not guarantee constraint satisfaction at inference. We propose a framework, called piecewise-linear Karush--Kuhn--Tucker hard-constrained PINNs (PL-KKT-hPINNs), that strictly enforces nonlinear equality constraints through piecewise-linear projection. This extends the KKT-hPINN framewor, which exactly enforces linear equalities through the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions associated with orthogonally projecting neural network outputs onto the constraint feasible region. The method is demonstrated on a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) case study for both one and two inputs. Results show that PL-KKT-hPINN preserves predictive accuracy comparable to that of a standard neural network while achieving substantially lower constraint violations. In addition, the proposed model shows improved robustness in low-data regimes, yielding lower RMSE than the unconstrained neural network for limited training sample sizes. These results demonstrate that PL-KKT-hPINN provides a computationally efficient and physically consistent framework for surrogate modeling of nonlinear chemical engineering systems.

</details>


### [284] [Divide and Cooperate: Role-Decomposed Multi-Agent LLM Training with Cross-Agent Learning Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10684)
*Jaewan Park,Solbee Cho,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAC框架，将智能体搜索分解为两个协作子任务（搜索者和生成者），通过角色分解的多智能体训练解决信用分配问题，在QA基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将证据获取和答案生成耦合在单一策略中，导致策略空间组合爆炸、探索效率低，并存在信用分配问题（搜索动作即使获取足够证据也可能因生成失败而受到惩罚）。

Method: DAC框架将智能体搜索分解为两个协作子任务：(1) 搜索者负责获取证据；(2) 生成者同时承担答案生产和证据充分性验证的双重角色，在证据不足时弃权。弃权信号被纳入搜索者奖励，形成结构化的跨智能体学习信号；同时通过难正例证据增强提升生成者鲁棒性。基于共享骨干网络使用LoRA模块实现。

Result: 在通用和多跳QA基准上，基于参数高效LoRA模块的DAC优于依赖全参数微调的整体模型方法。

Conclusion: 角色分解的多智能体训练框架能有效解耦搜索与生成任务，通过跨智能体信用分配信号提升训练效率，参数高效方法即可超越全参数微调的整体模型。

Abstract: Modern language agents which perform multi-step reasoning have shown strong performance in knowledge-intensive question answering. However, existing approaches typically couple evidence acquisition and answer generation within a single policy. This forces a single model to play multiple potentially conflicting roles, inducing a combinatorial explosion in the policy space and hindering efficient exploration. It also introduces a credit assignment problem during training: a search action that retrieves sufficient evidence may still be penalized when generation fails, and vice versa. We propose DAC (Divide and Cooperate), a role-decomposed multi-agent training framework that divides agentic search into two cooperative subtasks, each handled by a dedicated agent trained with role-specific learning signals. The generator serves a dual role as both an answer producer and an evidence sufficiency verifier, abstaining when retrieved evidence is insufficient. This abstention signal is incorporated into the search agent's reward, providing structured cross-agent learning signals that improve credit assignment. Conversely, the searcher exposes the generator to diverse and challenging evidence environments by hard-positive evidence augmentation, improving its robustness. Experiments on general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that DAC, implemented via parameter-efficient LoRA modules over a shared backbone, achieves strong performance against prior baselines that rely on full fine-tuning of monolithic models.

</details>


### [285] [Event-Driven Reinforcement Learning Enables Long-Horizon Control in Semiconductor Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10705)
*Yavar Yeganeh,Mahsa Shekari,Nicla Frigerio,Daniele Pagano,Andrea Matta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向半导体制造系统的深度强化学习框架，采用集中式智能体策略协调全局决策，基于事件驱动的时间差分方法，在离线与在线训练下实现吞吐量和利用率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造系统具有随机性高、约束强、异构晶圆需跨数百道工序在大型设备网络中加工等特点，导致传统生产规划与控制方法难以应对复杂、高维、延迟反馈和长时域序列决策问题。

Method: 将控制问题建模为集中式智能体问题，核心策略协调系统范围决策；系统演化表示为离散事件驱动的互联时序过程；开发了一种通用的事件驱动时序差分（temporal-difference）公式，可集成多种策略优化方法；结合多种无模型算法，在高保真仿真环境中进行离线与在线训练。

Result: 在多样化工业级实际运行场景的高保真仿真中，离线与在线训练的智能体在吞吐量和利用率上均取得显著且一致的提升；进一步评估了不同训练阶段的性能与泛化能力，揭示了各强化学习算法与公式的相对优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架在可扩展性、通用性和可迁移性方面表现良好，适用于事件驱动复杂自适应系统的控制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning promises to optimize sequential decisions in large-scale systems. Semiconductor manufacturing systems are stochastic and highly constrained environments where heterogeneous wafers traverse hundreds of processing steps across extensive equipment networks. These characteristics yield complex, high-dimensional decision problems with delayed feedback and long-horizon requirements, complicating production planning and control. We propose a deep reinforcement learning framework for multi-objective policy optimization at this scale. Specifically, we formulate control as a centralized-agent problem, where a core policy coordinates system-wide decisions, while system evolution is represented as an interconnected temporal process driven by discrete events. Accordingly, we develop a tailored event-driven temporal-difference formulation that remains general and can be integrated with various policy optimization methods under relevant training settings. We investigate several core model-free algorithms incorporated into this framework and evaluate their effectiveness using high-fidelity simulations of diverse, industry-real operating scenarios. Across extensive validation experiments, agents trained in both offline and online settings show significant and consistent gains in throughput and utilization. We further evaluate performance and generalization across training phases, clarifying the relative strengths of alternative reinforcement learning formulations and algorithms. Overall, the results support the scalability, generality, and transferability of the proposed framework for controlling event-driven complex adaptive systems.

</details>


### [286] [Unifying Data, Memory, and Compute Efficiency in LLM training: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10706)
*Vanessa Schmidt,Huy Hoang Nguyen,Cédric Jung,Shirin Salehi,Anke Schmeink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从资源约束视角出发，系统综述了大语言模型在数据效率、内存效率和计算预算感知三个耦合瓶颈上的研究进展，提出了资源条件化决策的统一原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将效率问题孤立看待，缺乏对资源约束作为交互系统的整体理解。本文旨在通过约束中心视角，统一梳理数据选择、内存优化和计算分配之间的耦合关系。

Method: 采用综述方法，围绕三个核心瓶颈组织文献：1) 数据效率：综述基于学习动态的代理信号、梯度/影响力评分、难度感知和课程策略等数据选择与剪枝方法；2) 内存效率：分析GPU显存瓶颈，强调权重存储、优化器状态和激活内存的联合优化；3) 计算预算感知：探讨计算最优分配和早停规则。

Result: 发现最优数据子集依赖于任务目标和资源预算而非普适；显存而非算力是微调的主要瓶颈；计算应在边际收益低于预算阈值时停止或重新分配。

Conclusion: 计算感知的数据选择、缩放定律和自适应推理可统一在资源条件化决策框架下，为资源受限环境下的LLM训练和部署提供系统性指导。

Abstract: Resource constraints increasingly determine what can be trained, fine-tuned, and deployed in large language models (LLMs), yet efficiency is often studied through isolated techniques rather than as an interacting system of limits. This survey adopts a constraint-centric perspective and organizes recent progress around three coupled bottlenecks: data efficiency (what to train on), memory efficiency (how to fit training), and compute budget awareness (when and where to spend FLOPs). On the data axis, we review selection and pruning methods that maximize learning per token, ranging from scalable proxy signals based on learning dynamics to gradient- and influence-based scoring, as well as difficulty-aware and curriculum-style strategies. We highlight emerging evidence that different notions of good data dominate in different regimes, implying that optimal subsets depend on the task objective and resource budget rather than being universal. On the systems side, we show that GPU memory, not raw compute, is often the dominant bottleneck in fine-tuning, and that effective scaling requires jointly reducing weight storage, optimizer states, and activation memory rather than optimizing any single component in isolation. Beyond memory, we frame training and inference as compute-governed processes in which optimization, data selection, and decoding must explicitly account for finite FLOP budgets. We review evidence for compute-optimal allocation and stopping rules, where computation should be halted or reallocated once marginal performance gains fall below a budget-dependent threshold. Together, these results unify compute-aware data selection, scaling laws, and adaptive inference under a common principle of resource-conditioned decision-making.

</details>


### [287] [Transformer Based Model for Spatiotemporal Feature Learning in EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10718)
*Xinglong Cui,Dian Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEG-TransNet，结合ResNet、局部自注意力块和模糊注意力同步Transformer（FAST），在三个EEG数据集上实现优于现有方法的分类性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG分析方法难以同时有效捕获信号的时序、区域和同步特征，需要一种统一的架构来提升脑电信号分析的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: EEG-TransNet包含三个模块：1）基于ResNet和小波去噪的预处理与特征提取模块；2）局部自注意力块用于区域特征学习；3）模糊注意力同步Transformer（FAST）建模时空依赖关系。解码器引入深度可分离卷积降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在BETA、SEED、DepEEG三个数据集上，分类准确率和鲁棒性一致优于对比方法；消融实验验证了局部自注意力块的贡献；深度可分离卷积在保持高精度的同时降低了计算复杂度；跨被试泛化性能稳定。

Conclusion: EEG-TransNet能够高效捕获EEG信号的时序、区域和同步特征，是一种鲁棒的脑电分类与情绪识别工具，具有良好的跨被试泛化能力。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely adopted technique for monitoring brain activity, offering valuable insights into neurological states due to its high temporal resolution and cost-effectiveness. To enhance the analysis of complex EEG data, we propose EEG-TransNet, an architecture designed to capture temporal, regional, and synchronous features of EEG signals. EEG-TransNet introduces three key modules: 1) a preprocessing and feature extraction module leveraging ResNet and wavelet-based denoising, 2) a Local Self-Attention Block for regional feature learning, and 3) a Fuzzy-Attention Synchronous Transformer (FAST) to model spatiotemporal dependencies. Through extensive experiments on three EEG datasets (BETA, SEED, and DepEEG), the proposed model consistently outperforms other methods in terms of classification accuracy and robustness across varying signal lengths. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of the Local Self-Attention Block in improving performance, and the inclusion of depthwise separable convolutions in the decoder reduces computational complexity while maintaining high accuracy. EEG-TransNet's ability to generalize across subjects with minimal performance variation highlights its potential as a robust tool for EEG-based brain activity classification and emotion recognition tasks.

</details>


### [288] [SPACR: Single-Pass Adaptive Training of Uncertainty-Aware Conformal Regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10734)
*Soundouss Messoudi,Sylvain Rousseau,Sébastien Destercke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPACR提出了一种可直接在回归模型中联合优化效率和有效性的单次自适应共形回归方法，无需批分割或预定义置信水平，在推理时支持多置信水平预测区间，性能优于标准CP和DOICR且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测（CP）通常后验应用于已训练模型，导致模型训练与共形目标（产生窄而有效的预测区间）不一致，需要昂贵的重新训练或批分割。

Method: 提出SPACR（Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor），设计可微损失函数直接训练不确定性感知回归器，联合优化区间效率（窄度）和有效性（覆盖），无需批分割或在训练时指定置信水平。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SPACR相比标准CP和DOICR生成更紧的预测区间，实现更好的覆盖-效率权衡，并显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: SPACR通过端到端训练解决了CP后验应用带来的不一致问题，单个模型即可在推理时支持多个置信水平的有效预测区间，是一种高效且实用的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) provides robust uncertainty guarantees for predictive models, but is typically applied post hoc, which misaligns model training with the conformal goal of producing efficient (i.e, narrow) intervals. We propose SPACR (Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor), a novel method for directly training uncertainty-aware regressors within a differentiable loss. SPACR jointly optimizes efficiency and validity without batch-splitting or a predefined confidence levels during training. As a result, a single SPACR model yields valid prediction intervals at multiple confidence levels during inference, avoiding the costly retraining required by methods like DOICR. Experiments on diverse datasets show that SPACR consistently gives tighter intervals and better coverage-efficiency trade-offs compared to standard CP and DOICR, while significantly reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [289] [Inverse Probability Weighting and Age-of-Information Aggregation for Decentralized Federated Learning under Partial Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10774)
*Chanuka A. S. Hewa Kaluannakkage,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DFL-AA，一种结合逆概率加权和基于信息年龄加权的去中心化联邦学习方法，用于解决有损无线网络中的选择偏差和更新过时问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习在有损无线网络中面临两个关键挑战：一是选择偏差（劣质链路更新被系统性地低估），二是更新过时（异步节点贡献过时信息）。现有方法（如均匀八卦聚合和基于完整性的加权）会引入并放大链路质量导致的偏差。

Method: 提出DFL-AA方法，包含两个核心机制：(1) 逆概率加权结合在线EWMA信道估计来修正选择偏差；(2) 基于信息年龄的加权来缓解更新过时问题，无需全局同步。

Result: 理论证明DFL-AA能在期望意义上消除链路质量失真；实验证明在不同丢包率、网络规模和异构无线条件下，该方法持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DFL-AA有效解决了有损无线网络中DFL的选择偏差和更新过时问题，在理论上消除链路质量失真，并在多种无线条件下展现出一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) over lossy wireless networks faces two key challenges: selection bias, where updates from poor-quality links are systematically underrepresented due to partial model reception, and update staleness, where asynchronous nodes contribute outdated information. We show that uniform gossip aggregation with local-fill reconstruction introduces persistent link-quality-induced bias, while completeness-based weighting further amplifies this effect. To address these challenges, we propose DFL-AA (Decentralized Federated Learning with Adaptive AoI-weighted Aggregation), which combines Inverse Probability Weighting with online EWMA-based channel estimation to correct selection bias and Age-of-Information-based weighting to mitigate staleness without requiring global synchronization. We theoretically show that DFL-AA removes link-quality distortion in expectation and experimentally demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across varying loss rates, network sizes, and heterogeneous wireless conditions.

</details>


### [290] [Can we trust our models? Epistemic calibration in second-order classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10777)
*Arthur Hoarau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知校准"（epistemic calibration）概念，用于评估二阶分类模型中认知不确定性估计的可信度，并证明其比经典校准更严格。研究者提出期望认知校准误差（EECE）作为一致性估计量，实验表明不同不确定性量化方法在认知校准上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典校准只评估预测概率的可靠性，无法判断认知不确定性估计本身是否可信。在高风险应用场景中，这一局限尤为关键，因为模型可能给出看似合理的置信度但实际不确定性估计失真。

Method: 定义认知校准准则，证明其严格强于经典校准；提出EECE（期望认知校准误差）作为TECE（真实认知校准误差）的一致估计量；通过不可能定理建立与现有文献的联系；在多种不确定性量化方法上进行实验验证。

Result: 认知校准是一个一致且有意义的标准；不同不确定性量化方法在认知校准上表现出显著差异，尽管它们的预测性能相似；经典校准无法捕捉的失败模式可通过认知校准发现。

Conclusion: 认知校准为评估二阶分类模型的不确定性估计提供了更严格、更全面的准则，有助于在高风险场景中更可靠地部署机器学习模型。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying machine learning models in high-stakes settings. However, classical calibration only assesses the reliability of predicted probabilities and does not evaluate whether epistemic uncertainty estimates are themselves trustworthy. This limitation is particularly relevant for second-order classification models. We introduce epistemic calibration, a principled criterion that measures whether reported epistemic uncertainty faithfully reflects the dispersion of model predictions around the ground truth. We show that epistemic calibration is a strictly stronger notion than classical calibration and captures failure modes invisible to standard metrics. We relate this work to the existing literature through an impossibility theorem that holds under the epistemic calibration hypothesis. To operationalize this concept, we propose the Expected Epistemic Calibration Error (EECE), which we prove to be a consistent estimator of a True Epistemic Calibration Error (TECE). Experiments across a broad range of uncertainty quantification methods show that epistemic calibration is a coherent and meaningful criterion and reveal substantial differences across methods, despite similar predictive performance.

</details>


### [291] [Closing the Modality Gap in Zero-Shot HAR: Contrastive Training and Separability-Optimized Prototypes on IMU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10789)
*Anik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于IMU的零样本人体活动识别中，传感器嵌入与语义类表示之间的模态差距问题，发现该差距是编码器目标导致的训练时现象。通过使用判别性活动描述替代标签名作为原型目标，可将传感器与文本原型的余弦相似度从0.30提升至0.69，结合对比训练与逆softmax校正，在未见类上达到73.2%准确率和0.583 macro F1。同时发现丰富文本描述会降低原型间可分离性，但对比对齐仍有效，并推荐macro F1作为ZSL-HAR标准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本学习在IMU人体活动识别中面临传感器嵌入与语义类表示之间的模态鸿沟，现有方法未能充分理解和解决这一核心挑战。

Method: 在PAMAP2数据集上系统评估三种推理方法、两种训练管道的七种配置，使用14个可见类和4个未见类，以102和109号受试者作为测试。采用TCN编码器，比较标签名与判别性活动描述作为Sentence-BERT原型目标的差异，结合对比学习和逆softmax校正方法。

Result: 判别性活动描述将传感器与文本原型的余弦相似度从0.30提升至0.69；最强配置（对比训练+逆softmax校正）在未见类上达到73.2%准确率和0.583 macro F1，而标签名基线仅为58.3%准确率和0.34 macro F1。

Conclusion: 模态间隙是训练时现象，可通过优化编码器目标来缓解；丰富文本描述虽降低原型间可分离性，但对比对齐仍有效；推荐macro F1而非准确率作为ZSL-HAR报告标准指标。

Abstract: Zero-shot learning (ZSL) for inertial measurement unit (IMU)-based human activity recognition (HAR) faces a central challenge: bridging the gap between sensor embeddings and semantic class representations. We systematically evaluate seven configurations combining three inference methods with two training pipelines on the PAMAP2 dataset, using 14 seen and 4 unseen activity classes with subjects 108 and 109 held out for testing. We find that the modality gap is a training-time phenomenon governed by the encoder objective. A temporal convolutional network (TCN) trained with cross-entropy over label-name Sentence- BERT prototypes yields sensor embeddings with a mean cosine similarity of 0.30 to the corresponding text prototypes, while replacing the label-name prototype targets with discriminative activity descriptions raises this to 0.69. This alignment improvement transfers consistently across all three inference methods. The strongest result combines contrastive training with inverted softmax correction, achieving 73.2% accuracy and 0.583 macro F1 on unseen classes, compared to 58.3% accuracy and 0.34 macro F1 for the label-name baseline. A secondary finding is that richer text descriptions reduce inter-prototype separability in Sentence-BERT space, because shared biomechanical vocabulary causes the language model to compress the prototype cloud. This effect does not negate the benefits of contrastive alignment provided prototype descriptions retain sufficient discriminative vocabulary. We also demonstrate that overall accuracy is a misleading primary metric when test-set class distributions are imbalanced, and recommend macro-averaged F1 as the standard reporting metric for ZSL-HAR benchmarks.

</details>


### [292] [CITRAS-FM: Tiny Time Series Foundation Model for Covariate-Informed Zero-Shot Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10798)
*Yosuke Yamaguchi,Issei Suemitsu,Yuki Kajihara,Wenpeng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CITRAS-FM 是一个仅7M参数的小型时间序列基础模型，支持单变量、多变量及协变量驱动的零样本预测，可在CPU上实现实时推理（<0.1秒），通过Shifted Attention和CovSynth协变量合成技术，在fev-bench上达到sub-10M TSFM中的SOTA零样本精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型计算成本高，对多样化的变量类型支持有限，尤其缺乏对影响目标变量的外生协变量的建模能力。

Method: 基于patch的decoder-only Transformer架构，提出Shifted Attention机制用于跨变量模块以有效利用已知协变量；提出CovSynth方法，从目标序列的分解组件中合成逼真的协变量，解决协变量数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在fev-bench的100个任务上，CITRAS-FM在sub-10M参数模型中达到SOTA零样本预测精度，CPU推理时间<0.1秒。

Conclusion: CITRAS-FM在预测精度与实时可部署性之间取得了良好平衡，为资源受限场景下的时间序列预测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Pretrained time series foundation models (TSFMs) have enabled zero-shot forecasting on unseen target series. However, existing TSFMs often incur high computational cost and provide limited support for diverse variable types, often failing to account for covariates that exogenously influence target variability. To address these challenges, we propose CITRAS-FM, a tiny 7M-parameter TSFM that supports univariate, multivariate, and covariate-informed zero-shot forecasting with real-time CPU inference. Built on a patch-based, decoder-only Transformer, CITRAS-FM introduces Shifted Attention into the cross-variate module to effectively exploit known covariates accessible throughout the forecast horizon. Moreover, to enable covariate-aware pretraining despite the scarcity of covariate-rich corpora, we propose CovSynth, which synthesizes realistic covariates from decomposed components of target series. Experiments on fev-bench, spanning 100 tasks across various settings, demonstrate that CITRAS-FM achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot accuracy among sub-10M TSFMs while delivering sub-0.1-second CPU inference, offering a strong balance between forecasting accuracy and real-time deployability.

</details>


### [293] [Boosting ECG Classification Performance by Pre-training with Synthesized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10802)
*Naoki Nonaka,Jun Seita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于医学领域知识的高斯成分合成算法生成合成心电图数据，用于预训练深度神经网络。实验表明，合成数据在四种异常ECG分类中，对三种异常类别（尤其是房扑）的性能提升显著，且在小数据集场景下效果更佳，验证了合成数据作为预训练资源的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学领域深度学习模型需要大量训练数据，但医疗数据因隐私问题和疾病罕见性往往难以获取，数据稀缺成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 开发知识驱动的Gaussian-composition合成算法，将每个心跳表示为P、Q、R、S、T波的高斯分量，生成AF、AFLT、PVC、WPW四类异常ECG合成数据；使用十种不同DNN架构进行合成-真实联合训练与分类评估。

Result: 合成-真实联合训练在四种目标异常中改善三种的分类性能，AFLT的平均架构增益最大（33.2%）；合成数据在真实数据集规模较小时提升效果更显著。

Conclusion: 基于领域知识的合成ECG可作为有效的预训练资源，尤其适用于真实数据有限或难以获取的场景。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) typically require extensive datasets for effective training. In the medical domain, acquiring large-scale data is often challenging due to privacy concerns and the rarity of certain diseases. To address this data scarcity, we investigate the efficacy of training DNN models using synthetic data, generated based on domain-specific medical knowledge. Specifically, we develop a knowledge-driven Gaussian-composition synthesis algorithm for single-lead II ECGs, in which each heartbeat is represented by Gaussian-shaped P, Q, R, S, and T wave components. Using this simulator, we generate synthetic data for four abnormal electrocardiogram (ECG) classes: atrial fibrillation (AF), atrial flutter (AFLT), premature ventricular complex (PVC), and Wolff-Parkinson-White Syndrome (WPW). We evaluate the utility of this synthetic data by conducting abnormal ECG classification using ten different DNN architectures. Our results demonstrate that synthetic-to-real training improves classification performance for three of the four target abnormalities, with the largest architecture-averaged gain of $33.2\%$ observed for AFLT. Further analysis reveals that the performance enhancement from synthetic data is more pronounced with smaller real-world datasets. These findings suggest that domain-knowledge-based synthetic ECGs can serve as a useful pre-training resource, particularly in scenarios where real-world data are limited or difficult to obtain.

</details>


### [294] [Encoding the Euler Characteristic Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10824)
*Nello Blaser,Odin Hoff Gardaa,Lars M. Salbu,Elena Xinyi Wang,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Euler特征曲线的连续编码方法，通过为每个方向上的每个顶点记录净Euler特征变化，生成令牌序列并由小型Transformer映射为特征向量。将流程分解为ECC编码器（方向内）和ECT表示（跨方向）两个阶段，研究了6种ECT架构。在6个分类基准上，连续编码改进了5个数据集的准确率，且收益源自令牌化本身而非Transformer容量。表示架构的归纳偏置效果依赖于编码方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Euler特征变换（ECT）通过离散化每个ECC曲线来编码形状描述符，这种离散化方式本身构成了神经网络的归纳偏置。作者旨在引入一种连续编码方法，以更自然地表示ECT信息，并探索不同ECT表示架构的归纳偏置效果。

Method: 提出连续编码方法：对每个方向和每个顶点，记录该顶点贡献的净Euler特征变化，形成每个方向的令牌序列，由小型Transformer映射为固定长度特征向量。将流程分解为两个正交阶段：(1) ECC编码器（方向内，将曲线映射为固定长度向量）；(2) ECT表示（跨方向，聚合各方向向量）。研究了6种ECT表示架构，从结构无关的前馈基线到保持平面旋转等变性的卷积和复值模型。

Result: 在6个分类基准（涵盖点云、图、复形和网格）上，连续编码在5个数据集中提升了准确率。控制实验表明收益来自令牌化本身而非Transformer容量。表示架构的选择不如编码方式重要，且归纳偏置的效果依赖于编码方式：前馈网络在连续编码下表现最佳，但在离散化下不如卷积架构鲁棒。

Conclusion: 连续编码是提升ECT性能的关键因素，而表示架构的归纳偏置效果受编码方式影响。这为形状分析中的特征表示提供了新的思路，即编码方式的设计比后续架构选择更为重要。

Abstract: The Euler Characteristic Curve (ECC) records the Euler characteristic of a linearly embedded cell complex as a function of filtration height in a given direction, and the Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT) is the injective shape descriptor obtained by collecting ECCs over many directions. How the ECT is encoded for a neural network is itself an inductive bias, conventionally fixed by discretizing each ECC. We introduce a continuous encoding: for each direction and each vertex it records the net Euler-characteristic change attributed to that vertex, producing a per-direction token sequence that a small transformer maps to a feature vector. We separate the resulting pipeline into two stages on orthogonal axes: an ECC encoder that acts within each direction, mapping its curve to a fixed-length vector, and an ECT representation that acts across directions, aggregating the per-direction vectors into one. We study six ECT representation architectures spanning a range of inductive biases, from a structure-agnostic feedforward baseline to convolutional and complex-valued models that preserve equivariance under planar rotations. Across six classification benchmarks covering point clouds, graphs, cubical complexes, and meshes, the continuous encoding improves accuracy on five of six datasets, and control experiments attribute the gain to the tokenization itself rather than to the added transformer capacity. The representation architecture matters less than the encoding, and the payoff from its inductive biases depends on the encoding: a feedforward network performs best under continuous encoding but is less robust under discretization than convolutional architectures.

</details>


### [295] [MODIP: Efficient Model-Based Optimization for Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10825)
*Zakariae El Asri,Philippe Gratias-Quiquandon,Nicolas Thome,Olivier Sigaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MODIP是一个离线到在线微调扩散策略的框架，通过世界模型和模型预测控制生成高质量轨迹作为监督目标，结合行为克隆的简洁性与世界模型的引导能力，在D4RL和RoboMimic任务上超越了纯行为克隆和扩散策略强化学习微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在行为克隆中表现优异，但直接应用强化学习进行微调面临多步去噪过程带来的挑战，需要一种既能利用强化学习引导策略优化，又能保持行为克隆简单性和稳定性的方法。

Method: 使用世界模型进行模型预测控制生成高质量轨迹作为扩散策略的监督目标；采用终端状态值替代策略相关的状态-动作值以提高规划效率；使用策略无关的TD目标训练评论家以减少训练时间。

Result: 在D4RL（MuJoCo、Kitchen）和RoboMimic任务上，MODIP改进了扩散策略超越行为克隆，与扩散策略RL微调方法和TD-MPC2等强模型基基线相比具有竞争力或表现更优。

Conclusion: MODIP通过世界模型引导策略适应，在保持行为克隆简洁性的同时实现了对扩散策略的有效微调，验证了模型预测控制与扩散策略结合在机器人学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion policies (DPs) have emerged as expressive policy representations for robot learning, often used with imitation learning methods such as behavioral cloning (BC). However, while their success has largely been confined to BC, direct reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning remains challenging because actions are generated through a multi-step denoising process. In this work, we propose MODIP, a framework for the offline-to-online fine-tuning of DPs. Rather than directly applying RL to the DPs, MODIP leverages a world model (WM) to guide policy adaptation and keeps the simplicity and stability of BC. We utilize model predictive control (MPC) to generate high-quality trajectories within the WM, and use them as supervised targets for fine-tuning the DP. To make MPC planning efficient, MODIP uses a terminal state value instead of a policy-dependent state-action value, reducing inference time. Additionally, MODIP trains critics with policy-independent TD targets, reducing training time. Experiments on D4RL (MuJoCo, Kitchen) and RoboMimic tasks show that MODIP improves diffusion policies beyond BC, and is competitive with or outperforms diffusion policy RL fine-tuning methods and strong model-based baselines such as TD-MPC2.

</details>


### [296] [Geometrically Averaged Hard Target Updates for Linear Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10835)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了线性Q学习中的λ-目标更新机制，该机制通过几何加权平均不同周期目标更新映射，统一了周期性目标更新与投影Q值迭代两种方法，并利用切换系统模型进行了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性硬目标更新是深度Q学习中常见的稳定化手段，但不同更新周期的效果及其与投影Q值迭代之间的关系尚缺乏统一的理论分析框架。

Method: 提出λ-目标更新机制，对m周期目标更新映射施加λ几何权重(1-λ)λ^{m-1}进行平均；λ=0时恢复单周期目标更新，λ↑1时恢复投影Q值迭代；采用切换系统模型对线性Q学习进行理论分析。

Result: 建立了λ-目标更新在确定性线性Q学习中的理论性质，并指出该方法可推广至随机强化学习环境。

Conclusion: λ-目标更新统一了周期性目标更新与投影Q值迭代，为理解目标更新对Q学习稳定性的影响提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Periodic hard target updates are among the most common stabilization devices in modern deep Q-learning. Recent studies suggest that target updates can improve stability in Q-learning with function approximation, including linear function approximation. We introduce and analyze the so-called $λ$-target update, obtained by averaging the $m$-periodic target update maps with $λ$-geometric weights $(1-λ)λ^{m-1}$, $λ\in [0,1]$. The endpoint $λ=0$ recovers the one-period target update, while the continuous endpoint $λ\uparrow1$ recovers projected Q-value iteration. We study this mechanism for Q-learning with linear function approximation, namely linear Q-learning, using a switching-system model and related tools. For clarity, the paper treats a deterministic version; the formulation extends to stochastic reinforcement-learning settings.

</details>


### [297] [When Do Autoregressive Sequence Models Forecast Physical Wavefields? A Controlled Study on Synthetic Seismograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10868)
*Waleed Esmail,Stuart Russell,Jana Klinge,Alexander Kappes,Christine Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自回归模型在振荡波场（如地震图）长程预测中的滚动稳定性问题，发现多token预测是稳定滚动的主导因素，而相位漂移是主要失败模式，相位感知损失是自然改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程自回归预测振荡物理信号（如地震图、引力波应变等）时，因果模型在数百步的自生成输出过程中，微小逐歩误差会累积为相位偏移，而逐点指标无法检测到这种退化。现有研究缺乏对滚动稳定性贡献因素的受控分析。

Method: 使用合成三分量地震图作为物理结构化的测试平台，以SeismoGPT自回归预测器为研究对象，通过受控的架构内消融实验结合配对显著性检验，在自由滚动预测上隔离各设计选择的贡献。使用多token预测、horizon-embedding混合预测头和跨horizon的STFT幅度一致性损失作为变量。

Result: 多token预测是主要稳定因素，在单个token基线上贡献几乎全部改进（中位NCC提升+0.040）；horizon-embedding混合预测头和STFT幅度一致性损失各带来微小但一致的额外增益；滚动泛化在上下文比率接近1（约P-S间隔）时急剧崩溃；主要残差失败模式为极性反转，幅度损失无法惩罚。

Conclusion: 本文是对振荡波场滚动稳定性的受控研究，而非预测架构的基准测试；相位感知目标是自然的下一个改进方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoregressive forecasting of oscillatory physical signals, such as seismograms, gravitational-wave strain, and similar wavefields is limited by error accumulation: as a causal model is fed its own outputs over hundreds of steps, small per-step errors compound into phase drift that pointwise metrics fail to detect. We ask when such rollout stays stable, using synthetic three-component seismograms as a physically structured testbed and the \textsc{SeismoGPT} autoregressive forecaster as the model under study. Through controlled, intra-architecture ablations evaluated on free-running rollout with paired significance tests, we isolate the contribution of each design choice. Multi-token prediction is the dominant stabilizer, accounting for almost the entire improvement over a single-token baseline ($+0.040$ median NCC); a horizon-embedding hybrid prediction head and a cross-horizon STFT-magnitude coherence loss each add a small but consistent further gain. Performance depends sharply on a context-ratio threshold near one, roughly the full P-S interval of observed signal, below which rollout generalization collapses. The dominant residual failure is a polarity inversion that a magnitude-based spectral loss cannot, by construction, penalize, identifying phase-aware objectives as the natural next step. We frame this as a controlled study of rollout stability on oscillatory wavefields, not a benchmark of forecasting architectures.

</details>


### [298] [XtrAIn: Training-Guided Occlusion for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10877)
*Thodoris Lymperopoulos,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Denia Kanellopoulou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XtrAIn，一种将遮挡操作从输入空间转移到参数空间的训练引导型归因方法，通过追踪特征关联的参数更新轨迹来估计特征重要性，避免了传统遮挡方法中的基线偏差、分布外样本和归因偏移问题。同时引入轻量级近似Xstep和目标聚焦变体XtrAIn+，在图像数据集和乳腺癌亚型分类任务上生成更清晰、可解释性更强的归因模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于遮挡的归因方法存在三大问题：外部选择的基线会引入偏差和分布外样本，导致不稳定的解释；在非线性模型中，遮挡一组特征会改变非遮挡特征的贡献（称为归因偏移）。这些问题使得解释结果不可靠。

Method: XtrAIn将遮挡操作从输入空间转移到参数空间，通过追踪训练过程中特征关联的参数更新如何影响输出logits来估计特征重要性，而非用人工基线替换输入值。同时提出Xstep（轻量级近似降低计算成本）和XtrAIn+（强调与目标类别对齐的更新）两种变体。

Result: 在受控图像数据集和PAM50乳腺癌亚型分类任务上，XtrAIn及其变体相比标准归因基线生成了更干净、更可解释的归因模式。

Conclusion: XtrAIn提供了一种训练感知的特征归因视角，可作为研究训练过程中特征级证据形成过程的有用诊断工具。

Abstract: Occlusion-based attribution methods provide an intuitive way to estimate feature importance by perturbing input features and measuring the resulting change in model output.
